ድ፣ ወለኩሉ ፡ መሳፍንተ ፡ አበው ፡፣ ቀደምት ፡ ዘደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ፡፤ በኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ከመ ፡ ያዕርጉ ፤ ታቦተ ፡ ትእዛዘ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ እምሀገረ ፣ ዳዊት ፣ እንተ ፣ ስማ፣ ጽዮን ። ወተጋብኡ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፡ ንጉሥ ፡፣ ዥሉ ፡ ፅደወ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ለበዓል፣ ወዝውእቱ ፡ ወርኅ ፡ ሳብዕ ። ወመጽኡ ፡ ዙሉ ፡ መላህቅተ ፡ እስራኤል ። ወነሥእዋ ፡ ዙሉ ፡ ሌዋዊያን ፡ ለታቦት ፡ 'ወአፅረግዋ ፣ ለይእቲ ፡ ታቦት ፣ ወለደብተራ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ ወለ ዙሉ ፡ ንዋያተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ግብር ፡ ዘቅድሳት ፡ ዘሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብተራ ። ወአዕረግዋ ፡ ክካ ሀናት ፡ ወሌዋዊያን ። "ወሰሎሞን ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ማኅበረ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ወዙሎሙ ፡ ፈራህያነ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ በማኅበሮሙ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ ታቦተ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይሠውዑ ፡ አልህምተ ፡ ወአጣሌ ፡ ዘአልቦ ፡ ጐልቄ ፣ ወመስፈርተ ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ኀሳ በ፣ አምብዝጉ ። 'ወአብእዋ ፡ ካህናት ፡ ለታቦተ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካን፣ ዝአስተዳለዉ ፡ ላቲ ፡ በዳቢር ፡ ቤተ ፡ ቅድሳት ፣ መትኅተ ፣ ክነፊሆሙ ፡ ለኪሩቤል ። ወሀለዉ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ኪሩቤል ፡ ስፉሓን ፡ ክነፊሆሙ ፣ መልዕልቴሃ ፡ ለይእቲ፣ ታ
tribes, and all the heads of the families of the children of Israel in Jerusalem, so that they might bring up the ark of the covenant of the Lord from the city of David called Zion. All the men of Israel gathered to King Solomon for the feast; this took place in the seventh month. All the elders of Israel came. All the Levites took and brought up the ark, the tabernacle of the testimony, and all the sacred furniture that was in the tabernacle. The priests and the Levites brought up (the ark). King Solomon, the whole assembly of Israel, and all those who feared the Lord (stood) gathered before the ark of the Lord, sacrificing oxen and sheep (text: goats) (without) number, without measure, and without evaluation because of their multitude. The priests brought the ark of the Lord into the place that they had prepared for it in the davir (or) sanctuary under the wings of the cherubim. The wings of the cherubim were spread out above the ark; the cherubim covered the ark of the Lord and (extended) over the
ቦት ፣ ወይከድንዋ ፣ ኪሩቤል ፡ እልክቱ ፡ ታቦተ ፣ እግዚኣብሔር ፡፣ ወዲበ ፣፡ ፍጽ ማ ፡ ለይእቲ ታቦት ፡ አምላዕሉ ፡ ወአጉዝ ፡ ፍጽማ ። "ወይትናጸራ ፡ አርእስተ ፡ መ ብዋአት ፡ አምቤት ፡ ቅድስት ፡ አንጻረ ፣ ዳ”ቢር ፡ ወኢይኔጽሩ ፡ ለአፍኣ ። ወሀሎ ፡ ህየ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ፅለት ። "ወአልቦ ፡ ዘሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ታቦት ፡ ዘእንበለ፣ ክልኤቱ ፡ አብላቅ ፣ ዘአንበረ ፣ ሙሴ ፡ በደብረ ፡ ኮሬብ ፣ ዘቦሙ ፡ ሠርዐ ፣ እግዚኣ ብሔር ፡ ለደቂቀ ፡፣ እስራኤል ፡ አመ ፡ ይወፅኡ ፣ እምድረ ፡ ግብጽ ። ''ወኮነ ፣ እም ድኅረ ፡ ወፅኡ ፡ ካህናት ፡ እምቤተ ፡ ቅድሳት ፡ አስመ ፣ ዙሎሙ ፡ ካህናት ፣ እምቤተ ፣ ቅድሳት ፡ አለ ፣ ተረክቡ ፡ በሀየ ፣ ተቀደሱ ፣ ወኢኮኑ ፡ ስሙያነ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እብሬ ት ፡ ”ወሌዋዊያን ፣፤ ወመዘምራን ፣ ኩሎሙ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ አሳፍ ፡ ወለኤማት ፣ ወለኢዱቱ ን ፡ ወለደቂቁ ፡ ወለአኃዊሁ ፡ እለ ፡ ይረብቡ ፡ አልባሰ ፡ ሲራይ ፡ በጸናጽል ፣ ወበከ በሮ ፣ ወበብዕዛ ፡ ይቀውሙ ፣ መንገለ ፡ ጽባሐ ፡ ቤተ ፣ ምሥዋዕ ፡ ወምስሌሆሙኒ ፤ ክህናት ፡ ፻ወ፳ ፤ ይነፍጐ ፡ በአቅርንት ። ”ወኮነ ፡ ቃል ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ ወድምፅ ፡ ሶበ፣ ይነፍጉ ፡ በውእቱ ፡ አቅርንት ፣ ወሶበ ፣ ዘመሩ ፡ ወሶበ ፣ ዐረዩ ፣ ዙሎሙ ፡፣ በአኅ ቲ፡ ቃል ፡ ከመ ፡ ይግነዩ ፡ ወይሰብሕዎ ፣ ለአግዚኣብሔር ። ወአልዐሉ ፣ ቃሎሙ ፡ በውእቱ ፡ አቅርንት ፣፤ ወበብዒዛት ፣ ወበጸናጽል ፣ ወበመሐልይ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይብሉ =
front of the ark from the top; the front was covered. The ends of the poles were visible from the sanctuary before the davir, but they were not seen from the outside. (The ark) has been there until this day. There was nothing in the ark but the two stone tablets that Moses deposited (there) at Mount Horeb, upon which the Lord instituted (his law) for the children of Israel, when they came out of the land of Gebs (Egypt). After the priests had come out of the sanctuary, for all the priests who had been found there in the sanctuary had been consecrated without being designated according to the distribution (of functions), the Levites and the singers, all sons of Asaph, of Heman, and of Jeduthun, with their sons and their brothers, wearing garments of (fine) linen and having cymbals, drums, and harps, stood toward the east of the altar; with them one hundred and twenty priests sounded trumpets. There was great clamor and (great) noise when they sounded the trumpets, when they sang, and when all agreed with one voice to celebrate and glorify the Lord. They raised their voices with the trumpets, the harps,


[p. 652] {#po.23.p652}


[p. 653] {#po.23.p653}
ግነዩ ፣ ለእግዚኣብሔር ፡ አስመ ፡ ሔር ፡ እስመ ፡ ለዓለም ፣ ምሕረቱ ። ወቤተ ፡ እግዚ ኣብሔር ፡ መልአ ፡ ወተከድነ ፡ ደመናተ ፡ ስብሐተ ፣ እግዚኣብሔር ። "'ወኢክህሉ ፡ ዳግመ ፡ ክህናት ፡ ቀዊመ ፡ ወተልእኮ ፡ አምቅድመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዐውሎ ፡ ደመና ፡ እ ስመ ፡ መልአ ፡ ስብሐተ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ አምላከ ፡ እስራኤል ።
the cymbals, and the instruments, saying: "Give thanks to the Lord, because he is good and because his mercy is forever." The house of the Lord was filled and covered with the cloud (text: the clouds) of the glory of the Lord. The priests could not stand (there) any longer, (nor) perform their ministry because of the darkness (text: the storm) of the cloud, because the glory of the Lord, God of Israel, had filled (the temple).
ላ1, 'አሜሃ ፣ ይቤ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፣ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ነበበ ፣ ወይቤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሕድር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዐውሎ ። ወአንሰ ፣ ኅነጽኩ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ለስመ ፡ ዚኣሁ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ወድልው፣ ማኅደሩ ፡ ለዓለም ። '"ወሜጠ ፡ ገጾ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፡ ወባረከ ፡ ዙሎ ፡ ማኅበረ ፡ አስራኤል ፤ ወቬዥሉ ፡ ማኅበረ ፣ እስራኤል ፣ ይቀውሙ ። ወይቤ ፡ ሰሉሞን ፡ 'ይትባረ ክ ፡ አግዚኣብሔር ፡ አምላከ ፣ አስራኤል ፡ ዘተናገረ ፡ በአፈ ፣ ዚኣሁ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ዳዊት 0፣፡ ወበአደወ ፡ ዚኣሁ ፡ ፈጸመ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ "እምዕለት ፡ አንተ ፡ አዕረጉ ፡ ሕዝበ ፡ ዚኣየ ፡ እምድረ ፣ ግብጽ ፣ ወ"ኢሐረይኩ ፡፣ በሀገር ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ ነገደ ፡ እ ስራኤል ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሕንጹ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ቤተ ፡፣ ወከመ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ስምየ ፡ በህየ ፣፣፡ ወኢሐረይ ኩ ፣ ብአሴ ፡ ከመ ፣ ይኩን ፣ መጋቤ ፡ ላዕለ ፣ ሕዝበ ፡ ዚኣየ ፡ አስራኤል ። ወኀረ ይኩ ፣ ወሐነጽኩ ፣ በኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ሊተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሠመይ ፡ ስምየ ፡ በህየ ፡
VI. Then Solomon said: "The Lord had spoken and had said that he would dwell in darkness (text: the storm). But I have built the house to your holy name; the dwelling is ready forever." King Solomon turned his face and blessed the whole assembly of Israel. The whole assembly of Israel stood. Solomon said: "Blessed be the Lord, God of Israel, who spoke with his mouth to David and realized (it) by his hands, saying: Since the day I brought my people up from the land of Gebs (Egypt), I have not chosen a city in all the tribes of Israel, so that one might build a house for me (there) and that my name might be there; I have not chosen a man to be leader of my people of Israel. I choose (now) and I build in Jerusalem a house for me, so that my name may be invoked there, and I choose for myself David,

ወኀረይኩ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ዳዊትሃ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩን ፡፣ ንጉሠ ፡ ላዕለ ፣ እስራኤል ። '"ወሐለየ ፡ ወዐርገ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልቡ ፡ ለዳዊት ፡ አቡየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኅንጽ ፣ ቤተ ፣ ለእግዚኣብሔር ፡ አምላከ ፡ እስራኤል ። ወይቤ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ አበየ ፡ እስመ ፡ ተሐ ለየ ፡ ወዐርገ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ልብከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትኅንጽ ፣ ቤተ ፡ ለስምየ ፣ ወሠናየ ፡ ገበርከ ፡ አስመ ፣ ሐለይከ ፡ በልብከ ። "ወበኅቱ ፡ አንተ ፤ ኢተሐንጽ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ለስምየ ፡ አላ ፡ ወልደከ ፡ ዘወፅአ ፡ እምኀቋከ ፣ ውእቱ ፡ የሐንጽ ፡ ቤተ ፣ ለስምየ ። '"ወአቀመ ፡ እግ ዚኣብሔር ፡ ቃለ ፡ ዚኣሁ ፡ ዘተናገረ ፣ ወተወለድኩ ፡ ለዳዊት ፡ አቡየ ፣ ወነበርኩ ፡ ዲበ፣ መንበረ ፡ መንግሥተ ፡፣ እስራኤል ፡ በከመ ፡ ነበበ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ወሐነጽ ኩ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ለስመ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ አምላከ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ "'ወአንበርኩ ፡ህየ ፡ ታቦተ ፡ እንተ ፣፤ ሀለወት ፡ ውስቴታ ፣ ትእዛዝ ፣ ወኪዳናት ፣ ዘእግዚኣብሔር ፣ ዘተካየዶሙ ፡ ለእስራኤል ፡ ሕዝቡ ። "ወቆመ ፡ አንጻረ ፡፣ ቤተ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ወቤተ ፡ ምሥዋ ዕ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ማኅበረ ፡ አስራኤል ፡ ወሰፍኀ ፡ እደዊሁ ። "ወገብረ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፤ መዐርገ ፡ ዘብርት ፡ ወአቀሞ ፡ ማእከለ ፣ ዐጸደ ፣ ቤተ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ። ፳፣ በእመ ት፣ ኑጉ ፡ ወ፳ ፡ በአመት ፡ ራኅቡ ፡ ወስልስ ፣ በአመት ፣ ግድሙ ። ወቆመ ፡ መልዕ
so that he may be king over Israel. There arose in the heart of David, my father, the thought of building a house to the Lord, God of Israel. The Lord said to David, my father: "Since the thought of building a house to my name has arisen in your heart, you have done well to have thought (so) in your heart. However, it is not you who will build the house to my name, but it is your son, who has come from your loins, who will build the house to my name." The Lord has confirmed the word that he had spoken: I have been begotten by David, my father, I have sat on the royal throne of Israel as the Lord had said, I have built the house in the name of the Lord, God of Israel, and I have deposited there the ark where the covenant and the engagements that the Lord concluded with Israel, his people, are." (Solomon) stood before the house of the Lord and the altar in the presence of the whole assembly of Israel, and he stretched out his hands. Solomon had made a bronze platform and he had put it in the middle of the court of the house of the Lord. Its length was five cubits, its width five cubits, and its height three cubits. He stood on this bronze platform, knelt before the assembly of Israel, and stretched
ልቴሁ ፣ ለውእቱ ፡ መዓርገ ፡ ብርት ፡ ወሰገደ ፣ በብረኪሁ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ ማኅበረ ፣ እስራ ኤል ፡ ወሰፍሐ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማይ ። '"ወይቤ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ አምላከ ፡ እ ስራኤል ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘይመስለከ ፡ አምላከ ፡ በሰማይ ፡ ወበምድር ፡ እንዘ ፡ ታቀውም ፡ ወተ ዐቅብ ፡ ኪዳነከ ፡ ወሣሀለከ ፣ ለአግብርቲከ ፡ አለ ፣ የሐውሩ ፣ በቅድሜከ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ል ቦሙ ጦ። ”ወአቀምክከ ፡ ለዳዊት ፡ አቡየ ፡ ገብርከ ፡ ዘተናገርከ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትብል ፡ ወነበብከ ፡ በአፉከ ፣ ወበእደዊከኒ ፡ ፈጸምከ ፣ በከመ ፣ ዛ"ቲ ፡ ፅለት ። '"'ወይእዜኒ ፣ እግዚኦ ፡ አ ምላከ ፡ አስራኤል ፡ ዕቀብ ፡ ወአቅም ፡ ለገብርከ ፡ ዳዊት ፣ አቡየ ፡ ዘነገርኮ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትብል ፡ ኢየሐልቀከ ፡ ብእሲ ፣፡ አምቅድመ ፡ ገጽየ ፡ ዘይነብር ፡ ዲበ ፣፡ መንበረ ፡ መ ንግሥተ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ወአመሰ ፡ ዐቀቡ ፡ ደቂቅከ ፡ ፍናውየ ፡ ወአመ ፡ ሖሩ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ሕግየ ፡ በከመ ፣ ሖርከ ፡ አንተኒ ፡ በቅድሜየ ። '"ወይእዜኒ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ አምላከ ፡ እስራ ኤል ፡ ይኩን ፡ ቃልከ ፡ እሙነ ፡ ዘተናገርከ ፣ ለዳዊት ፡ ሞልዔከ ። "ወእመሰ ፡ አማ ን ፡ ጥቀ ፡ የሐድር ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ምስለ ፡ እጓለ ፤ እመሕያው ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድር። ወእመሰ ፣ እግዚኦ ፡ ሰማይ ፣ ወሰማየ ፡ ሰማያት ፣ ኢይክሉ ፡፣ ጸዊረ ፡ ስብሐቲከ ፣ወ ምንትኑ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቤት ፡ ዘአነ ፡ ሐነጽኩ ። "ወነጽር ፡ ላፅለ ፡ ጸሎተ ፡ ገብር ከ፣ ወላዕለ ፡ ስእለቱ ፡ አግዚኦ ፤ አምላክየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትስማዕ ፡ ጸሎትየ ፡ ወስአለተ ፣
his hands toward heaven. Solomon said: "O Lord, God of Israel, there is no god who is like you in heaven and on earth, since you confirm and keep your covenant and your mercy for your servants who walk before you with all their heart. You have confirmed to David, my father, your servant, what you said (to him) by your word; (what) you expressed (to him) by your mouth you have realized by your hands, as (it is manifest in) this day. Now, O Lord, God of Israel, keep and confirm to your servant David, my father, what you declared to him, saying: "There shall not fail you a man before my face to sit on the royal throne of Israel, if your sons keep my ways and if they walk according to my law, as you yourself have walked before me. Now, O Lord, God of Israel, let the word that you spoke to David, your servant, be true. (Can it be) truly that the Lord dwells with men on the earth? If, O Lord, the heaven and the heaven of heavens cannot contain your glory, what (then) is this house that I myself have built? (But) look upon the prayer of your servant and his request, O Lord, my God, so that you

ገብርከ ፡ ዘጸለየ ፡ ኀቤከ ፡ በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፤ ”ወከመ ፡ ይኩና ፣ አፅይንቲከ ፡ ንቁሃተ ፡ ላ ፅለ ፣ ዝንቱ ፣ ቤት ፡ መዓልተ ፡ ወሌሊተ ፡ በሀየ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ቤት ፡ ዘአንተ ፣ ትቤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሰመይ ፡ ስምከ ፡ በላዕሌሁ ፡”ወከመ ፡ ትስማዕ ፡ ጸሎተ ፡ ገብርከ ፣ ዘይጴሊ ፡ ኀቤከ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ መካን ። ወስማፅ ፡ ጸሎተ ፡ ገብርከ ፡ ወስአለተ ፡ ሕዝብከ ፡ እስራኤል ። አመሰ ፡ ጸለዩ ፡ ኀቤከ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ መካን ፣ ወአንተኒ፣ አግዚኦ ፡ ስማዕ ፣ በውስተ ፣ ድልው ፡ መካነ ፡ ማኅደርከ ፣ እምሰማይ ፡ ስምዖሙ፣0፣ ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወመሓሬ ፡ ወሰራዬ ፡ ኩን ፡ ሎሙ ። ወአመቦ ፡ ዘአበሰ ፡ ብአሲ ፡ ላዕለ ቢጹ ፡ ወአመቦ ፡ ዘአንሥአ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ መንሱተ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያመንስዎ ፡ ወመጽአ፡ወ ጸለየ ፡ ወሰአለ ፣ ቅድመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ቤት ፡ ወምሥዋፅ ፣ ውስተ ፣ ውእቱ ፡ ቤት ፡ ”ወ አንተኒ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ ስማዕ ፡ አምሰማየ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ ወግበር ፡ ፍትሐ ፡፤ ለአግብርቲከ፣ ወከመ ፣ ትፍ”ድዮ ፣ ለዓማዊ ፡ ወከመ ፣ ታአክዮ ፡ ወታግብእ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ርአሱ ፡ በከ መ ፡ ፍናዊሁ ፡ ወታጽድቆ ፡ ለጻድቅ ፡ ወከመ ፡ ትዕስዮ ፡ ለለ ፡ ጸ፳ ፡ ከመ ፡ጽ ድቁ ። ወአእመሰ ፡ ቦ ፡ ዘተሰብሩ ፡ ሕዝብከ ፣ አስራኤል ፡ በቅድመ ፡ ፀሮሙ ፡ አመቦ ፣ ዘአበሱ ፡ ለከ ፣0፣ ወተመይጡ ፡፣ ወገነዩ ፡ ለስምከ ፡ ወጸለዩ ፡ ወሰአሉ ፡ በቅድ
may answer my prayer and the request that your servant addresses to you on this day, so that your eyes may watch day and night over this house and over this temple where you have said that your name would be invoked, and so that you may answer the prayer that your servant addresses to you in this place. Hear the prayer of your servant and the request of your people of Israel. If they pray to you in this place, you on your side, O Lord, hear in the prepared place of your dwelling, from heaven answer their prayers, and be merciful and indulgent to them. If someone has sinned against his neighbor, if someone has done evil against him to afflict him, if he comes, prays, and supplicates before this house and the altar (that) is in this house, you on your side, O Lord, hear from the heaven of heavens and do justice to your servants, so as to punish the iniquitous, to render evil to him, to turn back upon his head according to his ways, to do justice to the just, and to reward each according to justice. If your people of Israel are in rout before their enemies, if they have sinned against you, if they return, implore your name, pray, and supplicate before you in this house, you on your side, O Lord, hear (them)
ሜከ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ቤት ፣ ”ወአንተኒ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ ስማዕ ፡ አምሰማይ ፡ ወመሓሬ ፡ ኩን ፡ ወስረይ ፡ ለአበሳ፣ ሕዝብከ ፣ እስራኤል ፡ ወትሚጦሙ ፡ ልቦሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድር፣ እንተ ፤ አውረስኮሙ ፡ ለአበዊሆሙ ። ”አመቦ ፣ ዘአብኩረት ፤ ዝናመ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወ አበየት፣ አውርዶ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድር ፡ እስመ ፣ አበሱ ፡ ለከ ፣፡ ወጸለዩ0፣ ውስተ፣ ው አቱ ፡ መጓን ፣ ወይሴብሑ ፡ ለስምከ ፡ ወተመይጡ ፡ ወነሥሒሑ ፡ በአንተ ፡ ኃጢአቶ ሙ ፡ አስመ ፡ አኅሰርኮሙ ፡”ወአንተኒ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ ስማፅ ፣ እምሰማይ ፣ ወመሐሬ ፡ ኩን ፣ ወስረይ ፣ አበሳሆሙ ፡ ለሕዝብከ ፣ እስራኤል ። እስመ ፣ አርአይኮሙ ፣ ፍኖ ተ ፡ ርትፅተ ፣ ከመ ፡ ይሖሩ ፡ ባቲ ፡ ወትፌኑ ፤ ወትሁብ ፡ ዝናመ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ምድር ፤ እንተ ፤ አውረስኮሙ ፡ ለሕዝብከ ። ወአመቦ ፡ ዘመጽአ ፡ አኪት ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወእ መኒ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ረኅብ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወእመ ፡ አኮ ፡ ነፋሰ ፡ ደዌ ፡ ወአመ ፡ አኮ ፡ አና ዙዕ ፣፤ ወእመ ፡አኮ ፡ አንበጣ ፡ ወእመ ፡ አኮ ፡ደጎብያ ፡ አመ ፡-በጽሐ ፡ ወአልጸቀ ፡ወ አመንደበ ፡ ጸላኢ ፡ አንጻረ ፡ አህጉረ ፡ ሕዝብከ ፡ በከመ ፡ ዙሉ ፡ ምንዳቤ ፡ ወሕማም ፡ ዘይበጽሖሙ ፡ ”ዙሉ ፡ እንከ ፡ ጸሎት ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ስአለት ፡ ዘተፈነወ ፡ እምኅበ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ሰብእ ፣ ወእምኀበ ፡ ዙሉ ፣ ሕዝብከ ፣ እስራኤል ፤ ወአመቦ ፡ ዘተጠየቀ ፡ ብአሲ ፡ ደ ዌሁ ፡ ወሕማሙ ፣ ወመጽአ ፡ ወሰፍሐ ፡ አደዊሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቤት ፡ ”ወአንተ ኗ ፤ እግዚኦ ፡ ስማዕ ፡ እምሰማይ ፣ ወእምድልው ፡፣ ማኅደርከ ፡ ወፈውሶ ፣ ወዕሥ
from heaven, be merciful, forgive the sin of your people of Israel, convert their heart (and bring them back) to the land that you gave as an inheritance to their fathers. If the heaven holds back the rain and refuses to make it descend upon the earth, because they have sinned against you, if they pray in this place, if they glorify your name, if they convert and do penance because of their sins, because you have afflicted them, you on your side, O Lord, hear (them) from heaven, be merciful, and forgive the sins of your people of Israel. Indeed, you will show them the right way in which they must walk; you will send and grant rain upon the land that you gave as an inheritance to your people. If a calamity comes upon the land, if famine comes upon the land, or the wind of disease, or the various species of locusts (text: or the 'anakue, or the 'anbata, or the dagobya), if the enemy arrives, approaches, and brings danger before the cities of your people as well as all the afflictions and sufferings that (could) happen to it, whatever the prayer and whatever the supplication made by a man or by all your people of Israel, whatever the man who, having recognized his disease and his pain, comes and stretches his hands toward this house, you on your side, O Lord, hear from heaven and from your prepared dwelling, heal and reward


[p. 657] {#po.23.p657}


[p. 658] {#po.23.p658}
ዮ ፡ ለብአሲ ፡ በከመ ፡ ፍናዊሁ ፡ ወበከመ ፡ ጠየቀ ፡ ኅሊናሁ ፡ እስመ ፡ አንተ ፡ ባ”ኅቲትከ ፣ እግዚኦ ፣ ታአምር ፣ ወትጤይቅ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ኅሊናሆሙ ፡ ለእጓለ ፡ እ መሕያው ፡ "ወከመ ፡ ይፍርሁ ፡ ትእዛዘከ ፡ ወፍናዊከ ፡ በዙሉ ፣ መዋዕለ ፡ ሕይወቶ ሙ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድር ፡ እንተ ፡ አውረስከ ፡ ለአበዊሆሙ ። ”ወኩሉ ፡ ነኪር ፡ ዘኢኮነ ፡ እአምሕዝብክ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ወእመቦ ፡ ዘበጽሐ ፡ እምብሔር ፡ ርሔቅ ፡ በአንተ ፡ ስ ምከ ፡ ወበእዴክ ፡ ጽንዕት ፡ ወበመዝራዕትከ ፡ ልዑል ፡ ወለእመ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ወቦኡ ፡ ወ ጸለዩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ መካን ፡ ”ወአንተኒ ፡ እግዚኦ ፣፡ ስምዖሙ ‹፡ እምሰማይ ፡ እም ድልው ፡ ማኅደርከ ፡ ወግበር ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ዘሰአለ ፡ ኅቤክከ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ነኪር ፡ ከመ ፡ ያአእም ሩ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ አሕዛበ ፡ ምድር ፡ ስመከ ፡ ወከመ ፣ ይፍርሁከ ፣ በከመ ፡ ሕዝብከ ፡ አስራኤል ፡ ወከመ ፡ ያእምሩ ፣ ወይእመኑ ፡፣ ከመ ፡ ተጸውዐ ፡ ስምከ ፡ ላዕለ ፣ ዝንቱ፣ ቤት ፡ ዘአነ ፡ ሐነጽኩ ። ”ወአእመሰ ፡ ወፅኡ ፡ ሕዝብከ ፡ ለፀቢአ ፡ ተዓይነ ፡ ፀሮሙ ፡ ዘአንተ ፡ ትፌንዎሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሖሩ ፡ ባቲ ፡ ወጸለዩ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ በከመ ፡ ሥርዓተ፣0፣ ሀገሮሙ ፡ ዘአንተ ፡ ሐረይከ ፡ በላዕሌሃ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጸዋዕ ፡ ስምከ ፡ በላዕለ ፡ ዝንቱ፣ ቤት ፡ ዘአነ ፡ ሐነጽኩ ፣ ለስምከ ፣ ”ወአንተኒ ፣ እግዚኦ ፣ ስምዖሙ ፡ እምሰማይ ፡ ስ አለቶሙ ፡ ወጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወትግበር ፡ ፍትሖሙ ። ”እስመ ፡ አበሱ ፡ ለከ ፡ እስመ ፡ አ
this man according to his ways and according to what you know of his sentiments, for you alone, O Lord, know and penetrate all the thoughts of men so that they may fear your commandments and your ways, all the days of their life, on the land that you gave as an inheritance to their fathers. (If) foreigners who are not of your people of Israel come from a distant land because of your name, your strong hand, and your raised arm, if they come, enter, and pray in this place, you on your side, O Lord, hear them from heaven and from your prepared dwelling and grant to these foreigners what they have asked of you, so that all the peoples of the earth may know your name, so that they may fear you like your people of Israel, and so that they may know and believe that your name is invoked in this house that I myself have built. If (the men of) your people go to war against the army of their enemies, so as to go by (the way) where you have sent them, if they pray to you (by turning), according to the rule, toward the city that you have chosen so that your name may be invoked in the temple that I myself have built to your name, you on your side, O Lord, hear from heaven their request and their prayer, so as to support their cause. But if they sin against you, for there is


[p. 659] {#po.23.p659}
ልቦ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ዘኢይኤብስ ፡ ወትቀትሎሙ ፡ ወትሜጥዎሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እደ ፡ ጸላእቶ ሙ ፡ ወይጹውውዎሙ ፡ በምድረ ፡ፀሮሙ ፡ በምድር ፡ ርሑቅ ፡ ወአመ ፡አኮ ፡ ቅርብት፣0፣ "ወተመይጠ ፡ አልባቢሆሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድሮሙ ፡ እንተ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡፣ ተወልዱ ፡ ወአመሰ ፣ ተመይጡ ፡፤ ወተማህሀለሉ ፡ ለከ ፡ በብሔር ፡ ኀበ ፡ ጴወውዎሙ ‹፡ እንዘ ፤' ይብሉ ፡ አበስነ ፡ ወዐመፅነ ፡ ወኢዐቀብነ ፡ ትእዛዘከ ፣ ”“ወተ”መይጡ ፡ ኀቤክ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ልቦሙ ፡ ወበኩሉ ፡ ነፍሶሙ ፡ በብሔር ፡ ኀበ ፡ ጹወውዎሙ ፡ ወጸለዩ ፡ በፍና ፡ ብ ሔሮሙጮ ፡ እንተ ፣ መጠውክከ ፡ ለአበዊሆሙ ፡ ወሀገር ፡ እንተ ፣ ሐረይከ ፡ ወቤት ፣ እንተ ፡ ሐነጽኩ ፡ ለከ ፣ ለስምከ ፡ ” ወአንተኒ ። ስማዕ ፡ እግዚኦ ፣ እምሰማይ ፡ እምድ ልው ፡ ማኅደርከ ፡ ወስማዕፅ ፡ ስአለቶሙ ፡ ወትግበር ፣ ፍትሐ ፣ ወመሓሬ ፡ ኩን ፣፡ለ ሕዝብከ ፡ አለ ፡ አበሱ ፡ ለከ ። “ወይእዜኒ ፣ እግዚኦ ፡ ይኩና ፡ አፅይንቲከ ፡ ንቁሃተ ወእዘኒከ ፡ ለአጽምኦ ፡ ጸሎት ፡ እንተ ፡ ትትፌነወከ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ መካን ። "ወይአእዜ ኒ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ አምላክየ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዕረፍትከ ፡ አንተ ፡ ወታቦተ ፡ መቅደስከ ። ከህናቲከ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ ይለብሱ ፡ ጽድቀ ፡ ወጻድቃኒከ ፡ ትፍሥሕተ ፡ ይትፌሥሑ ። ”እ ግዚኦ ፡ አምላክየ ፡ ኢትሚጥ ፡ ገጸከ ፡ እምኔየ ፡ አመሲሕከ ፡ ወተዘከር ፣ ምሕረተ ፣ ለዳዊት ፡ ገብርከ ።
no man who does not sin, and you strike them down and deliver them into the hands of their enemies, and (the latter) take them captive into the land of their enemies in a distant or near land, if they turn (in) their hearts toward the land where they were born, if they convert and if they supplicate you in the land where they were taken captive, saying: "We have sinned, we have acted iniquitously, and we have not kept your commandments," if they return to you with all their heart and with all their soul in the land where they were taken captive, and if they pray (by turning) toward the land that you gave to their fathers, toward the city that you have chosen, and toward the house that I have built to your name, you on your side, O Lord, hear from heaven and from your prepared dwelling their prayer, so as to do justice (to them), and be merciful to your people who have sinned against you. Now, O Lord, let your eyes be vigilant and your ears (attentive) to hear the prayer that is addressed to you in this place. Now, O Lord, my God, arise for (fixing here) your rest, you and the ark of your sanctuary. Your priests, O Lord, will be clothed with justice and your just ones will rejoice with joy. O Lord, my God, do not turn your face from me, your anointed, and remember (your) mercy toward David, your servant."


[p. 660] {#po.23.p660}
'ወዕበ ፣ ፈጸመ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፡ ዘንተ ፣ እንዘ ፡ ይጹሊ ፡፣ እንከ ፡ እሳት ፣ ወረ ደ ፡ እምሰማይ ፡ ወበልዐ ፡ ወአሕለቀ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ቀ“ርባናተ ፡ ወዙሎ ፣ መሥዋዕተ ። ወስብሐተ ፣ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ መልአ ፣ ውስት ፡ ቤት ፣'ወኢክህሉ ፡እንከ ፣ ዳግመ ፡ካ ሀናት ፣፤ በዊአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ በይእቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ እስመ ፡ ስብሐ ተ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ መልአ ፡ በውእቱ ፡ ቤት ። "ወኩሎሙ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል፣ ርእይዋ ፣ ለይእቲ ፡ አሳት ፡ እንዘ ፣ ትወርድ ፡ ወስብሐተ ፣ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ውእ ቱ ፡ ቤት ፡ ወወድቁ ፡ በገጾሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወውስተ ፡ መካን ፡ ዘይብልዎ፡ጎ ልስሮጣን ፣፡ ዝውእቱ ፡፣ ጸፍጸፍ ፡ ወሰገዱ ፡ በህየ ፡ ወሰብሕዎ ፣ ለእግዚኣብሔር ፡ እን ዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ እስመ ፡ ለዓለም ፣ ምሕረቱ ። 'ወሰሎሞን ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ሕዝበ ፣ አስራኤል ፡ ይሠውፁዑ ፡ መሥዋዕተ ፡ በቅድመ ፣ እግዚኣብሔር ። "ወሦዐ ፡ ሰሎ”ሞን ፡ በይእቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አልህምተ ፡፪፳ ፡ ወ፳፻ ፡ ወመራፅየ ፡ አባግዕ ፡፲፳ ፡ ወ፪፳ ። ወገብረ ፣ ወሐደሰ ፡፤ ቤተ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፣ ሰሎሞን ፣ ንጉሥ ፣ ወኩሉ ፡፤ ሕዝበ ፣ እስራኤል ። "ወካህናትኒ ፡ ይቀውሙ ፡ ወየዐጥኑ ፡ በበ ፡ ምቅዋማቲሆሙ ፡ ወሌዋዊያን ፡ በዕን
VII. When Solomon had finished this prayer, then fire descended from heaven, devoured and consumed all the burnt offerings and all the sacrifices. The glory of the Lord filled the house and the priests could no longer enter the house of the Lord on that day, for the glory of the Lord had filled this house. All the children of Israel saw the fire and the glory of the Lord descend upon this house; they fell face to the ground in the place that was called golserotan, that is to say the pavement, prostrated themselves there, and glorified the Lord, saying: "For his mercy is forever." King Solomon and all the people of Israel offered sacrifices before the Lord. Solomon sacrificed that day twenty-two thousand oxen and one hundred and twenty thousand sheep. King Solomon made the dedication of the house of the Lord with all the people of Israel. The priests were standing and offering incense (standing) each at his post; the Levites with musical instruments performed the songs of King David to the Lord, (saying): "Give thanks to the Lord, for his mercy is forever." Among the hymns to the glory
ዚራ ፡ ይፌንዉ ፡ ማኅሌተ ፡ ለእግዚኣብሔር ፡ ዘዳዊት ፡ ንጉሥ ፡፣ ግነዩ ፣ ለአግዚኣብ ሔር ፡ እስመ ፡ ለዓለም ፣ ምሕረቱ ። በማኅሌት ፡ በስብሐተ ፣ እግዚኣብሔር ፣ መዝ ሙረ ፡ ዘዳዊት ፡ ይዜምሩ ፤፡ በእደዊሆሙ ፡ ወካህናት ፡ ይነፍሩ ፡ በአቅርንት ፡ በአን ጻጸሪሆሙ ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ አስራኤል ፡ ይቀውሙ = 'ወገብረ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፡ ወቀደሰ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ዐጸደ ፡ ቤተ ፤ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ እስመ ፡ ሆየ ፡ ገብረ ፣ ጽንሓሐ ፣ ወመቋዕልተ ፡ መ ድኀኒት ፣ እስመ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ምሥዋዕ ፡ ዘብርት ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፡ ኢገብሮ ፡ ከመ፣ ያንብሩ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ጽንሓሐ ፣ ወሞ'ርባናተ ፡ ወመቋዕልተ ። "ወገብረ ፡፣ ሰሎሞን ፡ በ ዓለ ፡ በውእቱ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ሰቡዐ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ጉቡኣን፣ ወኮነ ፡ ማኅበር ፡ ዐቢይ ፣ ፈድፋደ ፣፤ አምብዋአ ፡ ኤሞት ፡ ወእስከ ፡ ተከዜ ፡ ዘግብጽ ። ወበሳኒታ ፣ በሳምንት ፡ ዕለት ፡፣ ገብረ ፣ ሰሎሞን ፣ ሙፃአ ፡ እስመ ፣ ተሐደሰ ፣ ወተቀደ ሰ ፡ ቤተ ፣፡ ምሥዋዕ ፡ ወገብረ ፣ ሰሎሞን ፡ በውአቱ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ በዓለ ፡ ሰቡዐ ፡ ዕለ ተ ። “ወአመ ፡ ፳ ፡ ወሰሉሱ ፡ ለሳብዕ ፡ ወርኅ ፡ ፈነዎሙ ፣ ለሕዝብ ፡ በበ ፡ መሓድ ሪሆሙ ፡፣ እንዘ ፣ ይትፌሥሑ ፡ ወይትኀሠዩ ፡ በልብ ፡ ፍጹም ፡ ወፍሥሕ ፡ ወአዳም ፡ ወዲበ ፡ሠናይ ፡ እንተ ፡ ገብረ ፣ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ለዳዊት ፣ ወለሰሎሞን ፡፣ ወለሕዝቡ ፣ አስራኤል ። ''ወሐነጸ ፤ ወፈጸመ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፡ ቤተ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ወቤተ ፡ መን ግሥቱ ። ወኩሎ ፡ ዘሐለየ ፡ ወአደሞ ፡ ለሰሎሞን ፡ በነፍሱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይግበር ፡ በቤተ ፣
of the Lord they sang the psalms of David (which they accompanied) with their hands. The priests sounded trumpets before them and all Israel stood. Solomon made the consecration of the interior of the court of the house of the Lord, for he offered there the burnt offerings and the fat of the peace offerings, because the bronze altar that he had made he had not established to put (so many) burnt offerings, oblations, and fats (there). Solomon made at that time a feast for seven days; all Israel was gathered with him; there was a very great assembly from the entrance of Hamath to the torrent of Gebs (Egypt). The next day, the eighth day, Solomon made the closing, for the altar had been inaugurated and consecrated and Solomon had made at that time the feast for seven days. The twenty-third day of the seventh month, he sent the people away, each toward his dwelling. (All) were rejoicing and exulting, the heart satisfied, rejoiced, and happy for the benefits that the Lord had granted to David, to Solomon, and to his people of Israel. Solomon built and finished the house of the Lord and the royal house. Everything that Solomon had planned and had taken pleasure (in imagining) in his soul, to execute (it) in the house of the Lord and in his house, was there for him an exact success.

[p. 662] {#po.23.p662}
አግዚኣብሔር ፡ ወበቤቱ ፡ በህየ ፣ ተራከቦ ። ”ወአስተርአዮ ፣ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ለ ሰሎሞን ፡ በይእቲ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ሰማዕኩ ፡ ጸሎተከ ፡ ወኀረይኩ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ መካን ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩነኒ ፡ ቤተ ፣ ምሥዋዕ ። "ወእመሰ ፡ አጽናዕክዋ ፡ ለሰማይ ፡ ወከላ እክዋ ፣ አውርዶ ፡ ዝናም ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወአመኒ ፡ አዘዝክዎ ፡ ለአንበጣ ፣ ከመ፣ ይብላፅ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ፅፀወ ፡ ዘይፈሪ ፣ ወለእመኒ ፡ ፈነውኩ ፡ ሞተ ፡ ላዕለ ፣ ሕዝብየ ፡ “ወ ተመይጡ ፡ ወተከልኡ ፡ ሕዝብየ ፡ ዘተጸውፀዐ ፡ ስምየ ፣ በላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ወጸለዩ ፡ ኀቤ የ ፡ ወኀሠሠ ፡ ገጽየ ፡ ወተመይጡ ፡ እምፍኖተ ፡፣ እከዮሙ ፡ ወአነኒ ፡ እሰምዖሙ ' እምሰማይ ፡ እምድልው ፡ ማኅደርየ ፣ ወመሓሬ ፡ እከውን ፣ ወሰራዬ ፡ ለአበሳሆሙ ፡ ወአፌውሶዕሙ ፡ ወአሐዩ ፡ ምድሮሙ ። ”ወይእዜኒ ፡ አፅይንትየ ፣ ንቁሃተ ፡ ይከውና ፡ ወእዘንየኒ ፣ ያጸምኣ ፣ ጸሎተ ፡ እንዘ ፤ ትትፌኖ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ዝንቱ ፡ መካን ። "ወይ እዜኒ ፡ ኅረይክዎ ፡ ወቀደስክዎ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቤት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሰመይ ፡ ስምየ ፡ እስከ፣ ለዓለም ፡ ወህየኒ ፣ ይከውና ፡ አዕይንትየ ፣ ወልብየኒ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ መዋዕል ። '' ወአንተኒ ፣ ለእመ ፡ ሖርከ ፡ በቅድሜየ ፡ በከመ ፡ ሖረ ፡ዳዊት ፡ አበዝ ፡ ወገበርከ ፣ ኩሎ ፡ ዘአዘዝኩ ከ ፡ሕግየ ፡ ወኩነኔየኒ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ዐቀብከ ፡ "አቀውም ፡ ወአጸንፅ ፡ መንበረ ፡ መንግሥ ትከ ፡ በከመ ፡ ተካየድክዎ ፡ ለዳዊት ፡ አቡከ ፡ እንዘ ፡ እብል ፡ ኢየሐልቀከ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘይ
The Lord appeared to Solomon that night and said to him: "I have heard your prayer; I have chosen this place, so that it may be my altar. If I close the heaven and if I prevent it from making rain descend upon the earth, if I command the locusts to eat all the trees that give fruit, if I send death upon my people and if (the sons of) my people upon whom my name has been invoked turn away and move away (from evil), if they pray to me, if they seek my face, and if they convert from the way of their malice, I on my side, I will answer them from heaven and from my prepared dwelling, I will be merciful and indulgent to their sins, I will heal them, and I will save their land. Now my eyes will be vigilant and my ears will hear the prayer that will be addressed in this place. Now I have chosen and I have sanctified this house, so that my name may be invoked forever and that my eyes and my heart may be there all the days. If you also walk in my presence as David, your father, walked, if you accomplish all my law that I have prescribed to you, and if you keep my commandments, I will maintain and I will strengthen the throne of your royalty just as I promised to David, your father, saying: "There shall not fail you a successor (text: a man) who is


[p. 663] {#po.23.p663}
ከውን ፡ ሰፋኔ ፡ ላዕለ ፣ ሕዝብየ ፡ እስራኤል ። ”ወለአመሰ ፡ ተመየጥክሙ ፡ አንትሙ ፡ ወሐደግሙ ፡ ወረሳዕክሙ ፡ ትእዛዝየ ፡ ወሕግየ ፡ ዘመጠውኩክሙ ፡ ወሖርክሙ ፡ ወአምለክሙ ፡ አማልክተ ፡ ነኪር ፡ ወሰገድክሙ ፣ ሎሙ ፡ወአነኒ ፡ አፈልሰክሙ ፡ እምድር ፡ እንተ ፣ አውረስክዎሙ ፡ ለአበዊክሙ ፡ ወለዝንቱ ፡ ቤት ፡ ዘቀደስክዎ ፡ ለስምየ ፣ እመይጥ ፡ ገጽየ ፤ እምኔሁ ፣ ወእሜጥዎ ፡ ወእሬስዮ ፡ ነገረ ፣ ወአምሳለ ፡ ለ ዙሉ ፡ አሕዛብ ፡ ወዜና ፣ ለኩሉ ፡ በሐውርት ። " ወዝንቱ ፡ ቤት ፡ ክቡር ፡ወኩሉ፣ ሐላፌ ፡ ፍኖት ፣ ዘይሬአዮ ፡፣ ይደነግፁ ፡ ወይብሉ ፡ በእ”ንተ ፡ ምንት ፡ ገብረ ፣ እግዚኣ ብሔር ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቤት ፡፤ ወላዕለ ፣ ዝንቱ ፡ ምድር ፡ ከመዝ ። ”ወያወሥኡ ፣ ወይብሉ ፡ እስመ ፡፣ ረስዕዎ ፣ ለእግዚኣብሔር ፡ አምላከ ፡፣ አበዊሆሙ ፣ ዘአውፅኦ ሙ ፡ እምድረ ፡ ግብጽ ፣ ወተለዉ ፡ ወአምለኩ ፡ አማልክተ ፡ ነኪር ፣ ወተቀንዩ ፡ሎ
prince over my people of Israel." But if you turn away (from me), if you abandon and forget my commandments and my law that I have given you, if you go to worship the gods of foreigners and prostrate yourselves before them, I on my side, I will make you emigrate from the land that I gave as an inheritance to your fathers; from this house that I have sanctified for my name I will turn away my face, and I will give it and put it as a lesson and an example to all peoples and as a fable to all lands. As for this glorious house, whoever passing by the road sees it, will be stupefied and will say: "Why has the Lord acted thus against this house and against this land?" One will answer and say: "Because they have forgotten the Lord, God of their fathers, who had brought them out of the land of Gebs (Egypt), that they have followed and worshipped the gods of foreigners and have served them, because of that, he has brought upon them all these evils."
ሙ ፡ ወበእንተዝ ፡ አምጽአ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ እኩየ ። ሻ[01, 'ወኮነ ፣ አምድኅረ ፡ ፳ ፡፤ ዓመት ፡ ሐነጸ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፡ ቤተ ፡፣ እግዚ አብሔር ፣ ወቤተ ፡ መንግሥቱ ፡ ወአህጉረኒ ፡ አለ ፡ ወሀበ ፡ ኪራም ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ጢ ሮስ ፡ ለሰሎሞን ፣ አሕነጸ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፣ ወአሕደሮሙ ፡ህየ ፣ ለደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ። "ወ በጽሐ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፡ እስከ ፡ ደወለ ፡ ኤማት ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወአጽንዐ ። 'ወአሕነጸ ፡ በህየ ፡ ተድ
VIII. Twenty years after Solomon had built the house of the Lord and his royal house, he had the cities that Hiram, king of Tyre, had given him rebuilt and had the children of Israel dwell there. Solomon reached the territory of Hamath-Zobah (which) he seized. He had Tadmor built in the land


[p. 664] {#po.23.p664}
ሞር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ገዳም ፣ ወኩሎን ፡ አህጉረ ፡፣ ጸናዕተ ፡ ዘሐነጸ ፡ በብሔረ ፣ ኤናሜት ። "ወሐነጸ ፡ ካዕበ ፣ ቤቶሮን ፣፡ ላዕላየ ፣ ወቤቶሮን ፡ ታሕታየ ፡ አሀጉረ ፡ ጽኑዓተ ፡ ወገብረ ፡ ሎቶን ፣ አረፍተ ፡ ወአናቅጸ ፣ ወመናስግተ ። "ወዓዲ ፡ ካዕበ ፣ ለብሔረ ፡ በላአት ፡ ወለኩሎን ፡ አህጉር ፡ ጽኑዓት ፡ አለ ፡ ወሀብዎ ፡ ለሰሎሞን ፣ወ ለአህጉረ ፡ ሰረገላት ፡ ወለዙሎን ፡፤ አህጉረ ፣ መስተጽዕናን ። ወለኩሉ ፡ እንከ ፡ ዘሐለየ ፡ ወዘፈቀደ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፡ በከመ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዘሐለየ ፡ ከመ ፣ ይሕንጽ ፣ በኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወበሊ ባኖስ ፣ ወበዙሉ ፡ መንግሥቱ ፡ ዘፈጸመ ። 'ወዙሎሙ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ አለ ፣ ተርፉ ፡ አምኬጣ ዊያን ፣ ወእምአሞራዊያን ፣ ወአእምፈሪዛውያን ፣፡ ወእምኢያቡሳውያን ፡ ወአለ ፡ ኢ ክኑ ፡ ጐልቆሙ ፡ ለደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ "እለ ፡ ተወልዱ ፣ እምደቂቆሙ ፡፣ እምእለ ፡ ተርፉ ፡ እምድኅሬሆሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድሮሙ ፡፣ አለ፣ ኢቀተልዎሙ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ እስራ ኤል ፡ ወአፅረጎሙ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፣ ወሜሞሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ የሀቡ ፡ ግብረ ፡ እስከ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ዕ ለት ። ወ”እምደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤልሰ ፡ ኢነሥአ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፣ አግብርተ ፣ መንግሥቱ ፡ እስመ ፡ ዕደው ፡ ጸናፅት ፡ ወመስተጽዕናን ፡ ወመስተቃትላን ፡ አሙንቱ ፡ ወመላእክት ፣
of the desert and he built all the strong cities of the land of Hamath. He also built Upper Beth-horon and Lower Beth-horon, strong cities; he made walls, doors, and bars for them. Solomon (did) the same for the city of Baalath and for all the strong cities that had been given to him, as well as for the cities (intended) for chariots and for all the cities (intended) for horsemen. Solomon therefore realized everything he had conceived and wanted for everything he had resolved to build in Jerusalem, in Lebanon, and in all his kingdom. All the peoples who remained of the Hittites, the Amorites, the Perizzites, the Jebusites who were not of the number of the children of Israel, (but) who were born of the children of those who remained after them in their land and whom the children of Israel had not killed, Solomon levied upon them (the tax) and compelled them to pay tribute until this day. But of the children of Israel Solomon did not take any as servants of his kingdom, because they were valiant men, horsemen, warriors, captains, combatants


[p. 665] {#po.23.p665}
ወጸናዕት ፡ ወመላእክተ ፡ ሰረገላት ፡ ወመስተጽዕናን ። '"አሉ ፣ እሙንቱ ፣ መላእክተ ፡ ትአዛዛቲሁ ፡ ለሰሎሞን ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ፪፻ ፡ ወዛ ፡ አለ ፡ ይቀውሙ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ግብር ፡ ወላ ዕለ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ አሕዛብ ። "'ወለወለተ ፡ ፈርዖንሂ ፡ አፅረጋ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፡ እምሀገረ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ወአግብኣ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ቤት ፡ ዘሐነጸ ፡ ላቲ ፡ አስመ ፡ ይቤ ፣ ሰሎሞን ፣፡ ኢትሕድር ፡ብ አእሲትየ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ዳዊት ፣ ንጉሠ ፡ አስራኤል ፡ እስመ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ይአቲ ፡፣ አስመ ፡ በአታ ፡ ሀየ ፡ ለታቦተ ፣ እግዚኣብሔር ። '"ወአሜሃ ፡ አዕረገ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፣ ጽን ሓሐ ፡ ለአግዚኣብሔር ፡ በላዕለ ፡፤ ቤተ ፣ ምሥዋዕ ፡ ዘእግዚኣብሔር ፡ ዘሐነጸ ፡ ሰሎሞ ን ፡ አንጻረ ፡ ቤተ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ”ወበከመ ፡ ቃል ፡ ዘበበ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ከመ ፡ ያዕርጉ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ በበ ፡ ሰናብት ፡ ወበበ ፣ አውራኅ ፡ ወበበዓላት ፡ ሥልሰ ፡ ለዓ መት ፡ ወበበዓለ ፡ ናአት ፣ ወበበዓላተ ፣ ሰናብት ፡ ወበበዓላተ ፣ መጸለት ። ”ወአቀ መ ፡ በከመ ፡ ትእዛዘ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ በከመ ፡ ክፍለቶሙ ፡ ለካህናት ፣ ወበከመ ፣ መልአእክቶ ሙ ፡ ወሌዋዊያንሰ ፣ በከመ ፡ ምቅዋማቲሆሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሰብሕዎ ፡ ለአግዚኣብሔር ፡ ወይትለአክዎሙ ፡ ለካህናት ፡ በከመ ፡፣ ኩሉ ፡ ቃል ፡ ዘለለ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ወዐቀብተ ፡ አናቅ ጽኒ ፡ በከመ ፡ ክፍለቶሙ ፡ በበ ፡ አንቀጾሙ ፡ እስመ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ትአዛዞሙ ፡ ዘኅበ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ብአሴ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ። ”ወኢሐደገ ፡ ወኢምንተኒ ፡ እምትእአዛዘ ፡ ዳዊ
and heads of chariots and horsemen. The heads of service of King Solomon were (in the number of) two hundred and fifty, who supervised the works of all the people. Solomon had the daughter of Pharaoh brought up from the city of David and led her to the house that he had built for her, for he said: "Let my wife not dwell in the city of David, king of Israel, which is holy, because there is the entrance of the ark of the Lord." Then Solomon offered burnt offerings to the Lord on the altar of the Lord that he had built before the temple of the Lord, so that one might offer (the sacrifices) there according to the order of each day, as Moses (had commanded, each sabbath, each month, and at the three feasts of the year: at the feast of unleavened bread, at the feast of weeks, and at the feast of tabernacles. He established according to the ordinance of David the priests according to their class and according to their ministry; the Levites according to their rank, so that they might glorify the Lord and that they might serve the priests according to all the ordinances of each day; the guards of the doors according to their class, at each door, for such were the commandments of David, the man of the Lord. He omitted nothing of the commandments of King David concerning


[p. 666] {#po.23.p666}
ት ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ዘበእንተ ፡ ካህናት ፣ ወዘበእንተ ፣ ሌዋዊያን ፣ በኩሉ ፡፣ ዘኮነ ፡፣ ቃል ፡ ወላዕለ ፣ መዛግብት ። ”ወተደለወ ፡ ወተፈጸመ ፡ ኩሉ ፣ ቅኔ ፣ እምአመ ፡ ዕለት ፣ እን ተ ፡ አሐዘ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፡ ወአስከ ፡ አመ ፡ ፈጸመ ፤ "ሰሎሞን ፡ ቤተ ፡ አግዚኣብሔር ። "ወአሜሃ ፡ ሖረ ፣ ሰሎሞን ፡ ውስተ ፣ ደወለ ፡ ጽዮን ፡ ገቢር ፣ ወበኤላም ፣ እንተ ፡ ጽንፈ ፡ ባሕር ፡ እንተ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ኤይምያስ ። “ወፈነወ ፡ ኪራም ፡ በእደ ፡ አግብርቲሁ ፡ አሕማረ ፡ ወእለ ፡ ያአምሩ ፡ ባሕረ ። ወሖሩ ፡ ወነገዱ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አግብርቲሁ ፡ ለሰሎሞ ን ፡ ለብሔረ ፡ ሰፌራ ፡ ወነሥኡ ፡ ወአእተዉ ፡ እምህየ ፡ አርባዕተ ፡ ፻ ፡ ወ9፣ መክሊ
the priests and the Levites for all the things that had been prescribed and for the treasuries. All the service had been prepared and organized from the day when Solomon began the house of the Lord until the moment when he finished it. Then Solomon went to the land of Ezion-geber and to Elath on the edge of the sea in the territory of Edom. Hiram sent by his servants ships and experienced navigators. They departed and sailed with the servants of Solomon toward the land of Ophir; they took and brought back from there four hundred and fifty talents of gold, which they brought to King Solomon.
ተ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ወአምጽኡ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፡ ንጉሥ ። 'ወንግሥተ ፡ ሳባእ ፡ ሰምዐት ፣ ዜናሁ ፣ ለሰሎሞን ፣ ወመጽአት ፡ ከመ ፡ ታመክሮ ፡ ለሰሎሞን ፣ ንጉሥ ፣ በአስተማስሎ ፣ በኢየሩሳሌም ፡፣ በሐይል ፡ ጽኑዕ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወምስሌሃኒ ፡ አግማል ፡ ይጸውሩ ፡ አፈወ ፡ ወወርቀ ፡ ብዙኀ ፡ ወዕንቄ ፡ክ ቡረ ፡ ወበጽሐት ፡ ኅበ ፣ ሰሎሞን ፡ ወተናገረቶ ፡ ኩሎ ፣ ዘውስተ ፡ ልባ ። ወሰሎሞን ሂ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ዜነዋ ፡ ዙሎ ፡ ቃላቲሃ ፡ ወኢሐደገ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፡ እምነገሩ ፡ ዘኢዜነዋ፣ በእንቲኣሁ ። "ወርእያ ፣ ንግሥተ ፡ ሳባእ ፡ ጥበቢሁ ፡ ለሰሎሞን ፡ ወቤተ ፡ ዘሐነጸ ፡ ወመባልዕተ ፡ ወማእዳተ ፡ ወመናብርተ ፡ አግብርቲሁ ፡ ወጾታ ፡ ላአካኒሁ ፡ ወአልባሲሆ ሙ ፡ ወአለ ፡ ይቀውሙ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወመላብሷሆሙ ፡ ወጽንሓሔ ፡ዘዝአዕረገ ፤ ሰሎሞን ፡
IX. The queen of Sheba heard of the fame of Solomon; she came to test King Solomon by proposing riddles to him; (she arrived) in Jerusalem with a very powerful retinue: (she had) with her camels carrying aromatics, much gold, and precious stones; she reached Solomon and explained to him everything that was in her heart. Solomon also explained to her all her riddles and did not let (any) riddle pass without interpreting it for her. The queen of Sheba having seen the wisdom of Solomon, the house that he had built, the dishes, the tables, the apartments of his servants, the ordinance of his domestics and their clothes, those who


[p. 667] {#po.23.p667}
ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፣ ወእምኔሁ ፣ ደንገፀት ። "ወአውሥአቶ ፡ ወትቤሎ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፣ ሰሎሞን ፣ እሙን ፡፣ ቃል ፡ ዘሰማዕኩ ፡ በብሔርየ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ቃልከ ፡ ወበእ ንተ ፣ ጥበብከ ። ወኢአመንኩ ፡ ቃላተ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እስከ ፡ ለልየ መጻአኩ ፡ ወበአዕይ ንትየ ፡ ርኢኩ ፡ ወናሁ ፡ ኢዜነዉኒ ፡ መንፈቀ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ጥበብከ ፡ ወወሰከኒ ፡ ዜና ፡ ዲበ ፡ ዜና ፡ ዘሰማዕኩ ። "ብፁዓን ፣ አሙንቱ ፡ ዕደው ፣ ወብፁንን ፡ አግብርት ፡አለ፣ ይቀውሙ ፣ ቅድሜከ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ወዘይሰምዕ ፡ ዙሎ ፡ ጥበቢከ ። ወይኩን ፡ እግ ዚኣብሔ”ር ፡ አምላክከ ፡ ቡሩክ ፣ ዘሰምረ ፡ ብከ ፡ ከመ ፡ የሀብከ ፡ ወያንብርከ ፣ዲ በ፣መንበረ ፡ ዚኣሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትኩን ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ከመ ፣ ይሚም ከ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፣ ንጉሠ ፡ ለዓለም ። ወአንገሠከ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትግበር ፡ ፍትሐ ፡ ወጽድቀ ። "ወወሀበቶ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፡ ንግሥተ ፡ ሳባእ ፡ ምእተ ፡ወ፳ ፡ መክ ሊተ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ወአፈወ ፡ ብዙኀ ፡ ይሙነ ፡ ወዕንቄ ፡ ክቡረ ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ዘይመስሎ ፡ ለው እቱ ፡ አፈው ፡ዘወሀበቶ ፡ ንግሥተ ፡ ሳባእ ፡ ለሰሎሞን ፡ ንጉሥ ። '' ወአግብርተ ፡ ሰሎሞ ን ፡ ወአግብርተ ፡ ኪራም ፡ አምጽኡ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ለሰሎሞን ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወርቀ ፡ እምብሔረ፣ ሴፌራ ፡ ወዕፀወ ፡ ሀቀ ፡ ወዕንቄ ፡ ክቡረ ። ''ወገብረ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ እልክተ ፡
stood before him and their garments, as well as the burnt offerings that he offered in the house of the Lord, was stupefied by it. She declared and said to King Solomon: "It is true, the word that I heard in my land concerning your renown and your wisdom. I had not believed the words of the people until I came myself and saw with my own eyes; behold, one had only reported to me half of your abundant wisdom; you have added renown to the renown that I had heard. Happy are the men and happy are the servants who stand before you at all times and who hear all your wisdom. Blessed be the Lord, your God, who has taken pleasure in you to give you (the royalty) and to put you on his throne, so that you may be king over all Israel, and so that he may establish you king over them forever. He has made you king over them, so that you may render right and justice." The queen of Sheba gave to King Solomon one hundred and twenty talents of gold, a great quantity of perfumes, and precious stones: there were no perfumes similar to those that the queen of Sheba gave to King Solomon. The servants of Solomon and the servants of Hiram brought to King Solomon gold from the land of Ophir, pine wood, and precious stones. The king


[p. 668] {#po.23.p668}
ፅፀወ ፡ ህቀ፦ ፡ መዓርገ ፣ ለቤተ ፣ አግዚኣብሔር ፡፣ ወመዓርገ ፡ ለቤተ ፣ መንግሥቱ ፡ ወዓዲ ፡ ገብረ ፡ እምእልክቱ ፡ ፅፀወ ፡ ሀቀ፦ ፡ መሰናቁተ ፡ ወድንብዛተ ፣ ለመሓ ልይ ፣ ወኢተገብረ ፣ ከመዝ ፣ በቀደምቶሙ ፡ በከመ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ በምድረ ፡ ይሁዳ ። ”ወሰሎሞንሂ ፡ ወሀቢ ፡ ለንግሥተ ፡ ሳባእ ፣ ዙሎ ፡ መፍቅዳ ፡ዘሰ አለቶ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፣ ዘአምፅአት ፣፤ አምኃሁ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ሰሎሞን ። ወገብአት ፣ ወተመይ ጠት ፡ ብሔራ ። "ወኮነ ፡ መክብበ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ዘአምጽኡ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ለሰሎሞን ፡ በአኀቲ ፡ ዓመት ፡ ፪፻ ፡ ወ፳ ፡መካልየ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ "ዘእንበለ ፡ ዕደው ፡ አለ ፡ ጽሐፋን ፡ ወሰያጢ ፡ አለ ፡ ይነግዱ ፣ ያመጽኡ ፡ ሎቱ ፣ ወዘእንበለ ፣ ዙሉ ፡ ነገሥተ ፣ አረብያ ፣ ወመሳ ፍንተ ፡ ምድር ፡ ዘያመጽኡ ፡ ወርቀኒ ፡ ወብሩረ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፣ ሰሎሞን ። "ወገብረ ፡ ሰ ሎሞን ፡ ክልኤ ፡፻ ፡ ወላትወ ፡ ዘወርቅ ፡ ዝብጦ ፡ ወለለ ፡ ፡ አምእልክቱ ፡ ወላትው፣ ሜጡ ፡ ፪፻ ፡ ዲናረ ፡ ወርቅ ። ''ወ፻፻ ፡ሐገፋ ፡ ዘወርቅ ፣፡ ወጡ” ለለ ፡ ፳ ፡፻፻ ፡ ወአን በሮሙ ፡ሰሎሞን ፡ውስተ፡ቤተ ፣ ኦም ፡ዘሊባኖስ። '"ወገብረ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፡ መንበረ፡መ ንግሥት ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ዘቀርነ ፡ ነጌ ፡ እምአቅርንት ፡ ዐበይት ፣ ወአልበጦ ፡ በወርቅ ፡ ጽሩይ ፥
Solomon made from this pine wood the steps of the house of the Lord and the steps of his royal house: he also made from this pine wood harps and lutes for the musicians (text: musical instruments). (Nothing) had been made before similar to what King Solomon made in the land of Judah. Solomon gave to the queen of Sheba everything that she had desired and had asked of him and beyond the gifts that she had brought him. She returned and went back to her land. The sum of gold that was brought to Solomon each year was six hundred and sixty talents of gold, without counting what the designated men, the merchants, and the travelers brought him, and without counting the gold and the silver that all the kings of Arabia and the princes of the land brought to King Solomon. Solomon made two hundred shields of beaten gold; the price of each of these shields was six hundred pieces of gold. (He also made) three hundred (small) golden shields; the price of each was three hundred (pieces of gold). Solomon put them in the house of the wood of Lebanon. Solomon made his royal throne with the ivory of large horns


[p. 669] {#po.23.p669}
ወፍቱን ፡-”ወፄ ፣ መዓርጉ ፡ ለውእቱ ፣ መንበር ፡ ልቡጣን ፣ ወቅቡኣነ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ወገብ ረ ፡ ምዕራፈ ፡ መዛርዕ ፡ በለፌ ፡ ወበለፌ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ መንበረ ፡ መንግሥቱ ወገብረ ፡ ክልኤተ ፡ አናብስተ ፡መንገለ ፡ አልክቱ ፡ ምዕራፈ ፡መዛርአ ፡ ይቀውሙ ፡ “ወዐሠርቱ ፤ ወክልኤቱ ፡ አናብስት ፡ ይቀውሙ ፡ ሀየ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ እልክቱ ፡ ፡ መዓርግ ፡ በለ ፌ ፡ ወበለፌ ፡ ወኢተገብረ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ በዙሉ ፡ መንግሥታት ። ”ወኩሉ ፡ንዋየ ፡ ቅኔሁ ፤ ለንጉሥ ፣ ሰሎሞን ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ወብሩር ፣ ወኩሉ ፡፣ ንዋየ ፡ ቅኔ ፣ ዘቤተ ፣ ኦም ፡ ዘሊባ ኖስ ፡ ዘወርቅ ። ወተርፈ ፡ ወፈድፈደ ፣ ወአልቦ ፡ ሐሳበ ፡ ወኀልቄ ፣ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለ ሰሎሞን ፡ንጉሥ ፡ብሩር ። ”ኝእስመ ፡ ሐመሩ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፡ይነግድ ፡ ለብሔረ ፡ ተርሴስ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አግብርቲሁ ፡ ለኪራም ፡ በምልዕር ፡ ወበበ ፡ ፻ ፡ ዓመት ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ሐመር ፡፤ እምብሔረ ፡ ተርሴስ ፣ ወይመጽእ ፡ ምሉእ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ወርቅ፣ወ ብሩር ፡ ወአቅርንተ ፡ ነጌ ፣ ወአህቡይ ። ”ወከብረ ፣ ወተለዓለ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፣ እምዥሉ፣0 ነገሥትኒ ፡ በብዕልኒ ፣ ወጥበብ ። ”ወኩሉ ፡ ነገሥተ ፣ ምድር ፡ የሐሥሥሠ ፡ ወይጽህቁ ፡ ገጾ ፣ ለሰሎሞን ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስምዑ ፡ ጥበበ ፡ዘዝወሀቦ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልቡ ። “ወእልክቱ ፡ ነገሥት ፡ በበ ፣ ዚኣሆሙ ፡ ያመጽኡ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ አምኃ ፣ ንዋያተ0፣
(of elephant) and he overlaid it with pure and fine gold; the six steps of this throne were overlaid and covered with gold; he made the armrests on one side and the other of the royal throne; he made two lions standing near the armrests; twelve lions on the six steps stood there on one side and the other; there had (nothing) been made like it in any (other) kingdom. All the vessels of service of King Solomon were of gold and silver; all the vessels of service of the house of the wood of Lebanon were of gold. The silver remained in abundance and had (neither) esteem (nor) value in the days of King Solomon. Indeed, the ships of King Solomon sailed toward the land of Tarshish at the same time as the servants of Hiram; every three years the same ships arrived from the land of Tarshish; they came toward the king loaded with gold, silver, ivory, and monkeys. Solomon was glorious and surpassed all the kings in riches and in wisdom. All the kings of the earth sought and desired the face of Solomon to hear the wisdom that the Lord had given him in his heart. Each of these kings brought him (his) gifts: of
ወርቅ ፡ ወብሩር ፡ ወአልባሰ ፣ ወቅንአተ ፡ ወመባልዕተ ፡ ጥዑመ ፡ ወአፍራሰ ፡ ወአብ ቅለ ። ”ወተረክበ፣ ሎቱ ፡ ለሰሎሞን ፡ 8አልፍ ፡ አፍራስ ፡ አእኑስ ፡ ለሰረገላት ፡ ወ፲ወ፪ እልፍ ፡ አፍራስ ፡ አትቡዕ ፡ ወአንበሮሙ ፡ሰሎሞን ፡ ወከፈሎሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አህጉረ ፡ ሰ ረገላ ፡ ወምስሌሁ ፡ "ለንጉሥ ፣፡ በኢየሩሳሌም ። ”ወኮነ ፡ ሰፋኔ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ነገሥት ፡ እምደወለ ፡ ተከዜ ፡ እስከ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ኢሎፍሊ ፡ ወእስከ ፡ ደወለ ፡ ግብጽ ። ” ወአንበረ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፡፣ ወርቀ ፡ ወብሩረ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ከመ ፡ አብን ፡ ወዕፀወ ፡ ዘግባት ፡ ከመ ፡ ፅፀ ፡ በለስ ፣ ዘውስተ ፣ ሐቅል ፡ ብዙኅ ። ”ወምጽአተ ፡፣ አፍራሷሲሁ፣0፣ ለሰሎሞን ፣ ንጉሥ ፣ እምብሔረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ወእምኩሉ ፡ በሐውርት ። ”ወአለ ፡ ተርፉ ፡ እምቃላተ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፡ ቀደምት ፡ ወደሐርት ፡ ነዮን ፣ ጽሑፋት ፡ በቃላተ ፡ ናታን ፣፡ነ ቢይ ፡ ወበቃላተ ፡ አክያ ፡ ስሎናዊ ፣ ወበቃለ ፣ ኢዮሄል ፡ ዘይሬኢ ፡ በእንተ ፣ ኢዮር ብዓም ፡ ወልደ ፡ናባጥ ። ”ወነግሠ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፡፣ ንጉሥ ፡ ላዕለ ፣ ዙሉ ፣ አስራኤል ፡ አር ብዓ ፡ ዓመተ። ”ወኖመ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፡ ወቀበርዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ዳዊት ፣ አቡሁ ። ወነግ ሠ ፡ ሮብዓም ፡ ወልዱ ፡ አምድኅሬሁ ። እ, 'ወመጽአ ፡ ሮብዓም ፡ በኬብሮን ፡ እስመ ፡ ሀየ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ አስራኤል ፡ ከ መ ፡ ያንግሥዎ ፡ለሮብዓም ። "ወኮነ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ሰምዐ ፡ ኢዮርብዓም ፡ ወልደ ፡ናባጥ ፡ አስመ፣
vessels of gold and silver, garments, myrrh, spices, horses, and mules. Solomon possessed four thousand (text: forty thousand) mares for the chariots and twelve thousand (text: one hundred and twenty thousand) horses (for the horsemen); Solomon placed them and distributed them in the cities (destined) for the chariots and near the * king in Jerusalem. || ruled over all the kings from the border of the river (Euphrates) to the land of the Philistines and to the border of Egypt. King Solomon made gold and silver in Jerusalem as common as stones, and cedars as numerous as the sycamores that are in the countryside. Horses were brought for King Solomon from the land of Egypt and from all lands. The other deeds of Solomon, the first and the last, behold, they are written in the prophecies of the prophet Nathan, in the prophecies of Ahijah the Shilonite, and in the prophecies of Iddo, the seer, concerning Jeroboam, son of Nebat. King Solomon reigned over all Israel for forty years. Solomon slept; he was buried in the city of David, his father; Rehoboam, his son, reigned after him. X, Rehoboam came to Hebron, because all Israel had come there to make him king. When Jeroboam, son of Nebat, learned (this), he was

.ውእቱኒ ፣ ሀሎ ፡ በብሔረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ እምአመ ፡ ተኀጥአ ፡ አምገጹ ፣ ለሰሎሞን ፡ ንጉሥ ፣ ወሐደረ ፤ ኢዮርብዓም ፡ ብሔረ ፤ ግብጽ ፡፣ ወተመይጠ ፡ ኢዮርብዓም ፡ እ ምግብጽ ። 'ወለአኩ ፡ ወጸውፅዎ ፡ ወመጽአ ፡ ኢዮርብዓም ። ወኩሉ ፡ ማኅበረ ፡ እስ ራኤል ፡ ኅበ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ሮብዓም ፡ መጽኡ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ 'አቡከ ፡ አክበደ ፡ ቅ ኔ ፡ ላዕሌነ ፣ ወአንተኒ ፣ ዮም ፡ ሕድግ ፣ ወአስሕት ፡ አምክበደ ፡ ቅኔ ፡ አቡከ ፡ መሪ ር፣፤ ወአምቅኔሁ ፡ ጽኑዕ ፡ ዘአንበረ ፡ በላዕሌሴነ ፣ ወንትቀነይ ፡ ለከ ። "ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ሖ ሩ ፡ እንከሰ ፡ እስከ ፡ ሠሉስ ፡ መዋፅል ፡ ወትመጽኡ ፡ ኀቤየ ፡ ወትጤይቁኒ ። ወሖረ ፡ ዙሉ ፡ ሕዝበ ፣ እስራኤል ። "ወዐደመ ፡ ሮብዓም ፣ ንጉሥ ፣ ሎ ፣ መላህቆቅተ ፡ አለ፣ ዘልፈ፣ ይቀውሙ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ ለሰሎሞን ፡ አቡሁ ፡ አመ ፡ ሕያው ፡ ውእቱ ። ወይ ቤሎሙ ፡ አፎ ፡ አንትሙ ፡ ትፈቅዱ ፡ ወእፎ ፡ ያስተርእየክ”ሙ ፡ ከመ ፣፡ ኣውሥኦሙ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ሕዝብ ። 'ቃለ ፡ አውሥኡ ፣ ወይቤልዎ ፡ እመሰ ፡ በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ቴረ ፡ ኮ ንክ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ወተናገርኮሙ ፡፣ ወሰመርከ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ሠናይተ ፣ ወይከውኑ ከ ፣ አግብርተ ፡ በኩሉ ፣ መዋዕል ። 'ወሐደገ ፣፣ ሮብዓም ፣ ምክሮሙ ፣ ለመላሀቅት፣ ዝአምከርዎ ፡ ወተማከረ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ደቂቅ ፡ አለ ፣ ተሐፅኑ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ አለ ፡ ይቀውሙ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ።፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ምንተ ፡ ታመክሩኒ ፡ አንትሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ አውሥኦሙ ፥፡ቃ
in the land of Egypt, for, since he had fled from the face of King Solomon, Jeroboam had been dwelling in the land of Egypt; (then) he returned from Egypt. They had sent (messengers) to him and had called him; Jeroboam came. The whole assembly of Israel came to King Rehoboam, saying, "Your father made our yoke heavy; but you today, lighten and release the weight of the bitter servitude of your father and the hard servitude that he imposed upon us, and we will serve you." He said to them: "Go away then for three days, (then) you will return to me and you will know my (answer)." All the people of Israel went away. King Rehoboam summoned all the elders who stood continually before Solomon, his father, when he was alive. He said to them: "How do you desire and how * does it appear to you that I (should) answer this people?" They answered (these) words and said to him: "If you are good to this people this day and if, receiving (these men) with favor, you speak good words to them, they will be (your) servants forever." (But) Rehoboam abandoned the counsel that the elders had given him. He consulted the young men who had been raised with him and who stood in his presence. He said to them: "What words do you advise me to answer this people who have spoken to me,

ለ ፤ ለዝንቱ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ዘተናገሩኒ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይብሉ ፡ አስሕተነ ፣ እምቅኔ ፣ ዘአንበረ አቡከ ፡ በላዕሌነ ። "ወአውሥአዎዖ ፡ ደቂቅ ፡ አለ ፡ ተሐፅኑ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ከ መዝ ፡ በሎሙ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ዘተናገረከ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ አቡከ ፡ አክበደ ፡ ላዕሌ ነ ፡ ቅኔ ፡ ወአንተኒ ፡ አስሕት ፡ ለነ ። ወከመዝ ፡ አውሥኦሙ ፣ ወበሎሙ ፡ ንኡስ ፡ አጽ ባዕትየ ፡ ትገዝፍ ፡ ወትጸንዕ ፡ አምኅቋ ፡ አቡየ ። "' ወይእዜኒ ፣ አቡየ ፡ ገሠጸክሙ ፡ በቅ ኔ ፡ ክቡድ ፡ ወአነኒ ፡ እዌስክ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ቅኔክሙ ። አቡየኒ ፡ ገሠጸክሙ ፡ በመቅሠፍ ፡ ወ አነኒ ፡ እንሥጸክሙ ፣ በዐቃርብት ። "ወመጽአ ፡ ኢዮርብዓም ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ኅበ ፣ ሮብዓም ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ በሣልስት ፡ ዕለት ፣ በከመ፣0 ዐደሞሙ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ግብ ኡ ፡ ኀቤየ ። በሣልስት ፣ ዕለት ። ”ወአውሥኦሙ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ቃላተ ፡ ዕፁባተ። ወሐደገ ፡ ሮብዓም ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ምክሮሙ ፣ ለመላህቅት ፡ “ወአውሥኦሙ ፡ ለሕዝብ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ፡ በከመ ፡ ምክረ ፡ ወራዙት ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይብል ፡ አቡየኒ ፡፣ አክበደ ፡ ላዕሌክሙ ፡ ቅኔ ፡ ወአነ ኒ ፡ እዌስክ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ አቡየኒ ፡ ገሠጸክሙ ፡ በመቅሠፍት ፡ ወአነኒ ፡ አንሥጸክሙ ፡ በ ዐቃርብት ። ”ወኢሰምቦፀ ፡ ሮብዓም ፡ ቃላተ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ዕልወተ ፡ በኅበ፣ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ዘይቤ ፣ አቀመ ፣ እግዚኣብሔር ፣ ቃሎ ‹ዘነበበ ፡ በእዴሁ ፡ ስአክያ ፡
saying: Release for us the servitude that your father imposed upon us." The young men who had been raised with him answered him and said to him: "Answer this people who have spoken to you thus, saying: Your father made our yoke heavy; but you, lighten (it) for us. Answer them thus and say to them: My little finger is thicker and stronger than the loins of my father. Therefore my father chastised you with a heavy servitude, but I will add to your servitude; my father chastised you with whips, but I will chastise you with scorpions." Jeroboam and all the people came to King Rehoboam on the third day, just as the king had summoned them, saying: "Return to me on the third day." The king answered them with harsh words. King Rehoboam abandoned the counsel of the elders, answered the people, and addressed them according to the counsel of the young men, saying: "My father made your servitude heavy, but I will add to it; my father chastised you with whips, but I will chastise you with scorpions." Rehoboam did not listen to the requests of the people, because there was a change (willed) by the Lord who had said: "The Lord

ስሎናዊ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ኢዮርብዓም ፡ ወልደ ፡ ናባጥ ፡ ወበእንተ ፡ ዙሉ ፡ እስራኤል ። "እ ስመ ፡ ኢሰምዖሙ ፡፣ ንጉሥ ፡ ወአውሥኡ ፡ ዥሉ ፡ ሕዝ”ብ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ምንትኑ ፣ መክፈልትነ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ወምንትኑ ፡ ርስትነ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ውሉደ ፡ እሰ ይ። ጐይይ ፡ ውስተ ፣ መኃድሪክሙ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ወይእዜኒ ፡ ርኢ ፡ ቤተከ ፡ ዳዊት። ወሖረ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ በበ ፡ መኃድሪሆሙ ። '"ወፅደወ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ አለ ፡ የሐድ ሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አህጉረ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ አንገሠ ፣ በላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ሮብዓምሃ ። "ወፈነዎ ፡ ንጉ ሥ ፡ ሮብዓም ፡ ለአዶራም ፡ ርኡሰ ፡ ግብር ፣ ወወገርዎ ፡ ደቂቀ ፣ እስራኤል ፡ በዕብን ፡ ወሞተ ። ወንጉሥ ፡ሮብዓም ፡ ጐፐአ ፡ ከመ ፡ይፅርግ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ። ”“ወዐለዉ ፡ቤተ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ቤተ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ እስከ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ፅለት ።
has confirmed his word that he had uttered through the intermediary of Ahijah the Shilonite concerning Jeroboam, son of Nebat, and concerning all Israel." As the king had not listened to them, all * the people answered and said to the king: "What portion have we with David and what inheritance have we with the son of Jesse? Flee to your dwellings, (children of) Israel, and now provide for your house, David." All Israel went away, each to his dwelling. The men of Israel who dwelt in the cities of Judah kept Rehoboam as king. King Rehoboam sent (to the dissidents) Adoram, the overseer of the tributes; the children of Israel stoned him and he died. King Rehoboam hastened to go up to Jerusalem. The house of Israel rebelled against the house of David until this day.
እ1, 'ወመጽእአ ፡ ሮብዓም ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወአስተጋብአ ፡ ዙሎ ፡ ሕዝበ ፤ ይሁዳ ፣ ወሕዝበ ፡ ብንያም ፡ ወኮነ ፡ መክብቦሙ ፡ ፲፳ ፡ ወቿ፳ ፡ ወራዙት ፡ጽኑዓት' ወመስተቃትላን ። ወተቃተሎሙ ፡ ለሕዝበ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ከመ ፡ ያግብእ ፡ ወያግርር ፡ ወይክብብ ፡ መንግሥቶ ። "ወኮነ ፡ ቃለ ፣ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሰምያ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ እግ ዚኣብሔር ፡ አንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ በሎ ፡ ለሮብዓም ፡ ወልደ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፡ ወበሎሙ ፡ ለሎ
XI, Rehoboam came to Jerusalem and gathered all the people of Judah and the people of Benjamin; their total (number) was one hundred and eighty thousand valiant young men fit for combat. He (wanted) to fight against the people of Israel, to reduce (it), to subdue (it), and to restore the integrity of his kingdom. The word of the Lord came to Shemaiah, a man of the Lord, saying: "Speak to Rehoboam, son of Solomon, and speak to all the people


[p. 674] {#po.23.p674}
ሙ ፡ ሕዝበ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወብንያም ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትብል ፡ "ከመዝ ፡ ይቤ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ኢ ትዕረጉ ፥ ወኢትሖሩ ፡ ወኢትትቃተልዎሙ ፡ ለአኃዊክሙ ፡ ግብኡ ፡ ወተመየጡ ፡ ፡ ውስተ ፣ መኃድሪክሙ ፡ እስመ ፡ አምኅቤየ ፡ ውእቱ ፣ ዝንቱ ፡ ነገር ። ወሰምዑ ፡ ቃለ ፡ አግዚኣብሔር ፡ ወተመይጡ ፡ ወኢሐሖሩ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ኢዮርብዓም ። "ወሮብዓምሂኒ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፣ ንጉሥ ፡ ነበረ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወአኅነጸ ፡ አህጉረ ፡ ጽኑዓተ ፣ በብ ሔረ ፡ ይሁዳ። ወአሕነጸ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ልሔም ፡ ወኢጣንያ ፡ሀገር ፡ ወኒቆኢ ፡ ወቤተ ፡ ሱራ ኢ ፡ ወሰክ ፡ ወአይለም ፡ ወጌት ፡ ወጎርሶን ፡ ወዞፋ ፡ ሀገር ፡ 'ወአዶራኢም ፡-ወአለኪስ ፣፡ ወአዛቃ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ”ወሰራኢ ፡ወኢያሎን ፡ ወኬብሮን ፡ እንተ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወብንያም ፡ አህ ጉር ፡ ጽኑዓት ። ”'ወአልበጠ ፤ ኩሎ ፡ አረፍተ ፡ ወሜጫመ ፡ መሳፍንተ ፡ ወአንበረ ፣፡ ው ስቴቶሙ ፡ መዛግብተ ፡ እክል ፡ ወዘይት ፡ ወወ”ይን ፡ "በበ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወአንበረ ፡ ህየ ፡ ኩ ናታተ ፣፡ ወአርማሐ ፡ ወአስተጻንዖሙ ፡ ፈድፋደ ። ወኮንዎ ፡ ሕዝቦ ፡ ለይሁዳ ፡ ወሕዝ በ፣ ብንያም ። ”"'ወክህናትኒ ፡ ወሌዋዊያን ፡ አለ ፣ በዙሉ ፡ እስራኤል ፣ ተጋብኡ ፡ ኅቤ ሁ፣ አምኩሉ ፡ በሐውርት ። ”“'ወሐደጉ ፡ ሌዋዊያን ፡ ማኅደረ ፡ መዓቅቢሆሙ ፡ ወሖ
of Judah and of Benjamin, saying: "Thus says the Lord: Do not go up and do not go to fight against your brothers; return and go back, each to your dwelling, because this event (has happened) by my (will)." They listened to the word of the Lord; they returned and did not go against Jeroboam. Rehoboam, son of King Solomon, dwelt in Jerusalem and built strong cities in the land of Judah. He built Bethlehem, the city of Etam, Tekoa, Beth-Zur, Soco, Adullam, Gath, Mareshah, the city of Ziph, Adoraim, Lachish, the city of Azekah, Zorah, Aijalon, Hebron, in Judah and in Benjamin (and made them) strong cities. He surrounded them all with walls; he placed governors (there); he put stores of food, oil, and * wine in each city; he established (there arsenals) of shields (text: pikes) and spears; he made (these cities) very strong. The people of Judah and the people of Benjamin were with him. The priests and the Levites who were in all Israel gathered near him (coming) from all the lands. The Levites abandoned (their) dwellings (and) their posts and went


[p. 675] {#po.23.p675}
ሩ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ በኢየሩሳሌም ፡ እስመ ፡ ሰደዶሙ ፣ ኢዮርብዓም ፡ ወደቂቁ ፡ ከመ፣ ኢይትቀነዩ ፤ ለእግዚኣብሔር ። ”ወጫመ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ካህናተ ፡ አውግር ፣፡ ወለአምሳ ል ፡ ወለዘከንቱ ፣ ወለአምሳለ ፡ ላህም ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ ኢዮርብዓም ። "” ወአውፅኦሙ ፡ እም ነገደ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ እለ ፡ ሐለዩ ፡ በልቦሙ ፣ ከመ ፡ ይሳኅሥሥዎ ፡ ለእግዚኣብሔር ፡ አም ላከ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ወመጽኡ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሠዑ ፡ ለእግዚኣብሔር ፡ አምላ ከ ፡ አበዊሆሙ ። '"ወጸንዐት ፤ መንግሥቱ ፡ እስከ ፡ ፻ ፡ ዓመት ፡ እስመ ፡ ሖረ ፡ በፍ ናወ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ወሰሎሞን ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ሠለስተ ፡ ዓመተ ። ”ወነሥአ ፣፡ ሎቱ ፡ ሮብዓም ፡ን ጉሥ ፡ ብእሲተ ፡፣-እንተ ፡ ስማ ፡ ሞላት ፡ ወለተ ፡ ኢያርሙት ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ለዳዊት ፡ ወአብ ያ ፡ ወለተ ፡ ኤልያብ ፡ ወልደ ፣ ኢያፅይ ። ”ወወለደት ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ደቂቀ ፣ ኢዮአስ፣ ወሰማ ርያ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ወዘሉዛለም ። “ወእምድኅረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ነሥአ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ብእሲተ ፡ እንተ ፡ ስማ ፡ መአክት ፡ ወለተ ፡ አቤሴሎም ። ወወለደት ፡ ሎቱ ፡ አብያ ፣ ወአያቲ ፡ ወዚዛ፣ ወሰሎሞን ። ”'ወአፍቀራ ፡ ሮብዓም ፡ ለመአክ ፡ ወለተ ፡ አቤሴሎም ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ አንስት ያሁ ፡ ወእምኩሉ ፡ ፅቁባቲሁ ፡ አስመ ፡ ዓሥሩ ፣ ወሰማኒ ፡ አንስተ ፡ ነሥአ ፡ ወዕቁባተ ፡
to Judah (and) to Jerusalem, because Jeroboam and his sons had driven them out, so that they would no longer fulfill the ministry of the Lord. Jeroboam appointed for himself priests of the high places for the idols, for the false (gods), and for the statues of calves that he had made. He brought out from the tribes of Israel those who aspired in their hearts to seek the Lord, God of Israel; they came to Jerusalem to sacrifice to the Lord, God of their fathers. The reign (of Rehoboam) was strengthened for three years, because he walked in the ways of David and of Solomon, his father, during (these) three years. King Rehoboam took for himself a wife named Mahalath, daughter of Jerimoth, son of David, and (another wife named) Abihail, daughter of Eliab, son of Jesse. (The latter) bore him sons: Jeush, Shemariah, his son, and Zaham. Then he took for himself a wife named Maacah, daughter of Absalom. (The latter) bore him Abijah, Attai, Ziza, and Shelomith. Rehoboam loved Maacah, daughter of Absalom, more than all his wives and more than all his concubines. Indeed, he took eighteen wives and sixty

፳ ፡ ወወለደ ፡ውሉድይ ፡ ፳ ፡ ወሰማኒተ ፡ አትቡዐ ፡ ወአዋልደ ፡ ፳። ”ወሜሞ ፡ ሮብዓም ፣ ንጉሥ ፡ መልአከ ፡ ለአብያ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ማአክ ፡ ወሰፋኔ ፣ ላዕለ ፡ አኃዊሁ ፡ እስመ ፡ ያፈ ቅሮ ፡ ”ወቀዋም ፡ ወላሕይ ፣፤ ወጽኑዕ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ ደቂቁ ፡ በኩሉ ፣ በሓውርተ ፡ ይሁ ዳ ፡ ወብንያም ፡ ወበአህጉር ፡ ጽኑዓት ። ወአንበረ ፡ ው”ስቴቶሙ ፡ እክለ ፡ ብዙኀ ። ወ ሰአለ ፡ ብዙኀ ፡ አንስተ ። 'ወኮነ ፤ አምድኅረ ፡ ጸንዐት ፡ መንግሥቱ ፡ ለሮብዓም ፡ ወተኀጥአ ፡ ወረስዐ ፡ ት አዛዘ ፣ እግዚኣብሔር ፡፣ ወኩሉ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ምስሌሁ ። "ወኮነ ፡ በሓምስ ፣ ዓመተ፣ መንግሥቱ ፡ ለሮብዓም ፡ መጽአ ፡ ሱስቅኤም ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ እስመ ፡ አበሱ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡"በ፲፻ ፡ ወ፪፻ ፡ ሰረገላ ፡ወ5ሸ ፤ አፍራስ። ወአ ልቦ ፡ ጐልቄ ፡ ለዘ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፣ እምብሔረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ወእምበሐውርተ ፡ ልብ ያ ፡ወጠራግላዊያን ፡ ወሰብአ ፣፡ ኢትዮጵያ። 'ወአሐዝዎሙ ፡ ለአህጉር ፡ ጽኑዓት ፡ አለ፣ ሀለዋ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወበጽሑ ፡ እስከ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ። "ወመጽአ ፡ ሰማ ኢያስ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሮብዓም ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወኅበ ፡ መላእክተ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ እለ ፡ ጉቡኣን
concubines, and he begot twenty-eight sons and sixty daughters. King Rehoboam established Abijah, son of Maacah, as chief and prince of his brothers, because he loved him and he was taller, more handsome, and more valiant than all his sons (who were) in all the lands of Judah and of Benjamin and in the fortified cities. He put much food * in (these cities). He asked for (for his sons) many wives. XII, After his kingdom had been strengthened, Rehoboam relaxed and forgot the commandments of the Lord, and all Israel with him. In the fifth year of the reign of Rehoboam, Shishak, king of Egypt, marched against Jerusalem, - for (the children of Judah) had sinned before the Lord, - with twelve hundred chariots and sixty thousand horsemen (text: horses). Innumerable was (the multitude) that came with him from the land of Egypt and from the land of Libya as well as the Troglodytes and the people of Ethiopia. They took the fortified cities that were in the land of Judah and they arrived as far as Jerusalem. The prophet Shemaiah came to find King Rehoboam and the princes of Judah who were gathered in Jerusalem


[p. 677] {#po.23.p677}
ውስተ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ እምገጸ ፡ ሱስቂም ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ይ ቤ ፣ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ አንትሙ ፡ ሐደግሙኒ ፡ ወአነኒ ፣ ሐደጉክሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ለሱስቂም ፡ ንጉሠ ፣ ግብጽ ። ወኀፍሩ ፡ መላአክተ ፡ አስራኤል ፡ ወንጉሦሙ ፡ ወይቤ ሉ ፡ ጻድቅ ፣ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ "ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ተመይጡ ፡ ወነስ ሑ ፡ወኮነ ፡ቃለ ፣ አግዚኣብሔር ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሰማእያስ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ አንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ እመ፡ተ መይጡ ፡ ኢያሐልቆሙ ፡፣ ወእሁቦሙ ፡ ሳሕተ ፣ ንስቲተ ፣ ለመድኀኒት ፣ ወኢያንጠበ ጥብ ፡ መዓትየ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ "ከመ ፡ ይኩኑ ፣ አግብርተ ፣ ወያአእምሩ ፡ ቅኔየ ፡ ወቅኔ ፡ ነገሥተ ፡ ምድር ። "ወዐርገ ፡ ሱስቂም ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ግብጽ ፣ ወነሥአ ፡ መዛግብተ ፡ ዙሎ ፣ ዘሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ቤተ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ወመዛግብተ ፡ ዘሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ንጉሥ ፣ ወነሥአ ፡ሐገፋተ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ አለ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ሰሎሞን ። "'ወገብረ ፡ ሮብዓም ፡ ሀየ ንቴሆሙ ፣ ሐገፋተ ፡ ብርት ። ወመ ፣ " ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ሮብዓም ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ መላእክተ ፣፡ አለ ፡ ይቀውሙ ፡ ወየዐቅቡ ፡ አናቅጸ ፡ ንጉሥ ። '' ወኮነ፣ አመ ፡ ቦአ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ቤተ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ይበውኡ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ መላእክት ፡ እለ ፡ ይቀውሙ ፡ ወየዐቅቡ ፡ ወአለኒ ፡ ይትለአክዎሙ ፡፣ ለመላእክት ፡ አለ ፡ ይቀውሙ ፡፣ ወየዐቅቡ ፣ አናቅጸ ፡ ንጉ ሥ ፡ "ወሶበ ፡ ነስሑ ፡ ተመይጠ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ መዐተ ፡ አግዚኣብሔር ፡ ወኢያሕለ
(fleeing) the face of Shishak, king of Egypt, and he said to them: "Thus has said the Lord: You have abandoned me; I also have abandoned you into the hand of Shishak, king of Egypt." The princes of Israel and their king were confounded and said: "The Lord is just." When the Lord saw that they were converting and repenting, the word of the Lord came to the prophet Shemaiah, saying: "Since they are converting, I will not annihilate them, (but) I will give them a little respite for (their) salvation; I will not pour out my wrath upon Jerusalem, (but I will ensure) that they be subjected, so that they may know (the difference between) serving me and serving the kings of the earth." Shishak, king of Egypt, went up, took all the treasures that were in the house of the Lord and the treasures that were in the house of the king; he took the shields of gold that Solomon had made. Rehoboam made shields of bronze in their place. * King Rehoboam handed them to the officers who stood and guarded the gates of the king. When the king entered the house of the Lord, the officers who stood and kept guard entered with him as well as (the men) serving (these) officers who stood and guarded the gates of the king. When (the children of Judah) had repented, the wrath of the Lord turned away from them and he did not


[p. 678] {#po.23.p678}
ቆሙ ፡ለዝሉፉ ፡ አስመ ፡ በውስተ፣ ይሁዳ ፡ ይትረከብ ፡ ቃል ፡ ሠናይ ። “ወጸንዐ ፡ ሮብ ዓም ፡ ወነግሠ ። ጓ ፡ ዓመተ ፡ ውአቱ ፡ ሮብዓም ፡ አመ ፡ አንገሥዎ ። ዐሰርተ ፡ ወ፲፣ ዓመተ ፡ ነግሠ ፡ በኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገር ፡ እንተ ፡ ሐረያ ፤ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ወ ተሰምየ ፡ ስሙ ፡ በህየ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ ነገደ ፡ እስራኤል ። ወስማ ፡ ለእሙ ፡ ኑኡነማ ፡ ዓማ ናዊት ። “ወገብረ ፡ እኪተ ፡ ወኢያርትዐ ፡ ልቦ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኅሥሦ ፡ ለእግዚኣብሔር ። ”ወቃላተ ፡ ሮብዓም ፣ ቀደምት ፡ ወደሐርት ፡ አኮኑ ፣ ጽሑፋን ፣ እሙንቱ ፡በቃላተ፡፡ሰ ማእያስ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ወአውዶ ፣ ዘይሬኢ ፡፣ ወኩሎ ፡ ምግባሮ ። ወይትቃተሎ ፡ ሮብዓም ፣ ለኢዮርብዓም ፡ በኩሉ ፡ መዋዕል ። "'ወኖመ ፡ ሮብዓም ፡ ወተቀብረ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አበዊሁ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ዳዊት-። ወነግሠ ፡ አብያ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ እምድኅሬሁ ።
exterminate them forever, because good works were found in Judah. Rehoboam strengthened himself and reigned. Rehoboam was forty-one (text: forty) years old when they made him king. He reigned seventeen years in Jerusalem in the city that the Lord had chosen from among all the tribes of Israel, (so that) his name might be invoked there. The name of his mother was Naamah the Ammonitess. (Rehoboam) did evil and did not apply his heart to seek the Lord. Are not the deeds of Rehoboam, the first and the last, written in the prophecies of Shemaiah, the prophet, and of Iddo, the seer, as well as all his exploits? Rehoboam fought against Jeroboam all the days (of his life). Rehoboam slept and was buried with his fathers in the city of David. Abijah, his son, reigned after him.
እ111, 'አመ ፡ ዐሠርቱ ፣ ወሰማንቱ ፣ ዓመተ ፡ መንግሥቱ ፡ ለኢዮርብዓም ፡ ንጉ ሠ ፡ አስራኤል ፡ ወነግሠ ፡ አቢያ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ይሁዳ ። ሠለስተ ፡፣ ዓመተ ፡ ነግሠ ፣ በኢ የሩሳሌም ። ወስማ ፡ ለእሙ ፣ መአካእ ፡ ወለተ ፡ ኡርኤል ፡ እንተ ፣ እምብሔረ ፡ ገ ባኦን ። ወቀትል ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ማእከለ ፡ አቢያ ፡ ወማእከለ ፡ ኢዮርብዓም ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ እስራ
XIII, In the eighteenth year of the reign of Jeroboam, king of Israel, Abijah reigned over Judah. For three years he reigned in Jerusalem. The name of his mother was Micaiah, daughter of Uriel, from the land of Gibeon. There was war between Abijah and Jeroboam, king of Israel. Abijah fought him
ኤል ። ወተቃተሎ ፡ አብያ ፡ በፅደው ፡ ጸናዕት ፡ ወመስተቃትላን ፡ በጣሸ ፡ ዕደው ፥ ጸናዕት ። ወኢዮርብዓም ፡ ተቃተሎ ፡ በ2፳ ፡ ዕደው ፡ ጸናዕት ፡ ወመስተቃትላን ። ወተንሥአ ፡ አቢያ ፤ እምብሔረ ፡፤ ሴሞሮን ፡ ዘሀሎ ፣ ው"”ስተ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ኤፍሬ ም ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ስምዑኒ ፡ ቃልየ ፡ ኢዮርብዓም ፡ ወኩሉ ፣ እስራኤል ። "አእምሮ ፡ አኮኑ ፡፣ ለክሙ ፡ እስመ ፣ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ አምሳከ ፡ አስራኤል ፡ አቀመ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ለዓለም ፡ ለዳዊት ፡ ወለደቂቁ ፡ በኪዳን ። ወተንሥአ ፣ ኢዮ ርብዓም ፡ ወልደ ፡ ናባጥ ፡፣ ገብሩ ፣ ለሰሎሞን ፣ ወልደ ፡ ዳዊት ፣ ወተግኅሠ ፡ ወተ ፈልጠ ፤ እምአግዚኡ ፡ ወተጋብኡ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ዕደው ፡ እኩያን ፡ ወደቂቅ ፡ ረሲ ዓን ፡ ወተንሥአ ፡ ወሖረ ፤ ኅበ ፡ ሮብዓም ፡ ወልደ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፡ ወሮብዓም ፡ ወልደ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፡ ወሬዛ ፡ ወኢኮነ ፡ ጥቡዕ ፡ በልቡ ፡ ወኢጸንዐ ፡ መንገሌሁ ። ወይእዜ ኒ ፡ አንትሙ ፡ ትብሉ ፡ ታጸንዑ ፣ ወታጠብዑ ፡ መንገለ ፣ መንግሥተ ፣ እግዚኣብሔ ር ፣ ዘበአዴሆሙ ፡ ለደቂቀ ፣ ዳዊት ። ወአንትሙኒ ፡ ይእዜ ፣ ብዙኅቶ ፣ ፈድፋደ ። ምስ ሌክሙ ፡ አልህምት ፣ ዘወርቅ ፣ ዘገብረ ፣ ኢዮርብዓም ፡ አማልክተ ። "ወኢያውፃእክ ሙ ፡ ካህናተ ፣ አግዚኣብሔር ፡ እምኔክሙ ፡፣ ደቂቀ ፡ አሮን ፣ ወሌዋዊያን ። ወረሰይክ
with valiant men of war (to the number of) four hundred thousand courageous men. Jeroboam fought him with eight hundred thousand (text: eighty thousand) valiant men of war. Abijah rose from the land of Zemaraim which is * in the mountains of Ephraim, and said: "Listen to my words, Jeroboam and all Israel. Do you not know that the Lord, God of Israel, established as king over Israel forever David, (then) his sons by a covenant, that Jeroboam, son of Nebat, servant of Solomon, son of David, having rebelled, has moved away and separated himself from his master, that evil men and impious sons have joined themselves to him, that he rose and marched against Rehoboam, son of Solomon, and that Rehoboam, son of Solomon, being young and not courageous in his heart, did not resist him? Now you say that you will have strength and power against the kingdom of the Lord which is in the hand of the sons of David. You are now extremely numerous. With you are the golden calves that Jeroboam has made gods. Have you not driven (far) from you the priests of the Lord, the sons of Aaron and the Levites? You have made yourselves priests from (all) the

ሙ ፡ ለክሙ ፡ ካህናተ ፡ እምአሕዛበ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ዘሖረ ፣ ይመልእ ፡ እደዊሁ ፡ ለጣዕዋ ፡ ላህም ፣ አምአልህሀምት ፡ ወአባግዕ ፡ ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ ካህናተ ፡ ለዘ ፡ ኢኮነ ፡ አምላክከ ። "ወንኅነኒ ፣ ለአግዚኣብሔር ፡ አምላክነ ፡ ኢንረስዕ ። ወካህናቲሁኒ ፡ ዘይት ለአክዎ ፣ ለእግዚኣብሔር ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ አሮን ፣ ወሌዋዊያን ። ''ወበበ ፡ አብሬቶሙ ፡ የ ዐጥኑ ፣ ለአግዚኣብሔር ፡ ጽንሓሐ ፡ በበ ፡ ነግህ ፡ ወበበ ፡ ሰርክ ፡ ዕጣነ ፡ ለመዐዛ ፡ ወኅብስተ ፡ ምስያም ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ማእድ ፡ ንጽሕት ፣ ወተቅዋመ ፡ ማኅቶት ፡ ዘወርቅ ፣ ወመኃትው ፡ እለ ፣ ያሐትዉ ፡ በበ ፡ ነግህ ፡ ወበበ ፡ ሰርክ ። እስመ ፡ ንኅነ ፣ ነዐቅብ ፣ ቤተ ፡ አግዚኣብሔር ፡ አምላከ ፡ አበዊነ ፡ ወአንትሙሰ ፡ ረሳዕክምዎ ። '”ወናሁ ፡ ምስሌ ነ ፡ ፍጹማን ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ወካህናቲሁኒ ፡ ወአቅርንቲሁኒ ፡ ”አለ ፡ ይጸርጉ ፡ ወያ ሰምዑ ፣ ላዕሌክሙ ። ወደቂቀ ፣ እስራኤልሰ ፤ ኢትቅትልዎ ፡ለአግዚኣብሔር ፡ አምላከ ፡ አበዊክሙ ፡፣ ወኢተረክበክሙ ። "'"ወኢዮርብዓምኒ ፡ ተመይጠ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይዕግቶሙ ፡ ወይምጽኦሙ ፡ ድኀኅሪተ ፡ ወኮነ ፣ በአንጻረ ፣ ትዕይንቶሙ ፡ ለይሁዳ ፣ ወዐገተ ፣ ድኅሬ ሆሙ ። ወተመይጡ ፡ ይሁዳ ፥ ወናሁ፣ ቀትል፤ አምድኅሬሆሙ ፣ ወአምቅድሜሆሙ ። ወጸርጉ ፡ ኀበ ፣ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ወካሀናትኒ ፡ ይነፍጉ ፣ በአቅርንት ። ”ወይጸርሩ ፡ ዕደ
populations of the land. Whoever comes and presents with his hands a young bull and seven rams is made a priest of what is not god. But we, we do not forget the Lord, our God. The priests who serve the Lord are sons of Aaron and Levites. They burn, each in his turn, holocausts to the Lord, every morning and every evening, and perfumes; (they place) the bread of the presence on the pure table and (they take care) of the golden candlestick and the lamps that they light every morning and every evening. For it is we who guard the house of the Lord, God of our fathers, but you, you have forgotten (the Lord). Behold, the Lord and his priests are with us and at our head with the trumpets that * sound and resound against you. Children of Israel, do not fight against the Lord, God of your fathers, (for) you will not obtain (any success)." Jeroboam turned to encircle them and to march against them from behind; he was in front of the army of Judah and he outflanked (it) from behind. (But) Judah turned and behold, the battle (took place) at the rear and at the front. (The men of Judah) cried out to the Lord and the priests sounded the trumpets. (Then) the men of Judah

[p. 681] {#po.23.p681}
ወ ፡ ይሁዳ ፣ ወእግዚኣብሔር ፡ ቀተሎ ፡ ለኢዮርብዓም ፡ ወለሕዝበ ፡ አስራኤል ፣ ቅድመ ፡ አብያ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ይሁዳ ፣ ወበቅድመ ፡ ሕዝቡ ። ”ወጐዩ ፡ ሕዝበ ፡ እስራኤ ል ፣፤ አምገጸ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወመጠዎሙ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ውስተ ፣ እደዊሆሙ ።፡ '"ወቀተ ለ ፡ ወአውደቀ ፣ እምኔሆሙ ፡ አብያ ፡ ንጉሠ ፣ ይሁዳ ፡ ወሕዝቡሂ ፤ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ወኮነ ፣ ድቀተ ፣ ዐቢየ ፣ ወወድቁ ፣ ተቃቲሎሙ ፣ እምሕዝበ ፡ አስራኤል ፡ ዕደው ፥ ዛ፳ ፡ ጸናዕት ፡ ወመስተቃትላን ። “ወኀስሩ ፡ ወተትሕቱ ፡ እምደቂቀ ፡ አስራኤል ፡ በይ እቲ ፡ ዕለት ፣ ወጸንፁ ፣ ደቂቀ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ እስመ ፡ ተወከሉ ፡ በእግዚኣብሔር ፡ አምላከ ፡ አበዊሆሙ ። ”ወሰደደ ፡ አብያ ፡ ድኅሬሁ ፣ ለኢዮርብዓም ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ ድኅሬሁ ፡፣ አህጉረ ፣ ቤቴል ፡ ወአዕጻዳቲሃ ፡ ወለኦና ፣ አህጉር ፣ ወለአዕጻዳቲሃ ፣ ወለእፍሬን ፡ ወለአዋልዲሃ ። ”ወኢረከበ ፡፣ ኢዮርብዓም ፡ ሐይለ ፣፤ እምአሜሃ ፣ በኩሉ ፣ መዋዕለ ፣ አብያ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወቀተሎ ፣ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ወሞተ ። ”ወጸንዐ ፡ አብያ ፡ ወነ ሥአ ፡ ፳ ፡ ወክልኤተ ፡ አንስተ ፡ ወተወልዱ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ደቂቅ ፡ጣ፪ ፡ ወ፲ወ8 ፡ አዋልድ። ”ወአለ ፡ ተርፋ ፡ ቃላተ ፡ አብያ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ይሁዳ ፣ ወምግባሩ ፡፣ ወቃላቲሁ ፡ ነዮን ፡ ጽ ሑፋት ፣ በመጽሐፈ ፡ አዶ ፡ ነቢይ ።
raised shouts and the Lord struck Jeroboam and the people of Israel before Abijah, king of Judah, and before his people. The people of Israel fled before Judah and the Lord delivered them into their hands. Abijah, king of Judah, and his people struck them and put them to flight; it was a great defeat: five hundred thousand valiant men of war of the people of Israel fell in fighting. The children of Israel were humiliated and brought low on that day, (but) the children of Judah were strengthened, because they had trusted in the Lord, God of their fathers. Abijah pursued Jeroboam and took from him the city of Bethel and its villages, the city of Jeshanah and its villages, Ephron and its dependencies. From then on Jeroboam did not recover strength during all the days of Abijah, king of Judah. The Lord struck him and he died. Abijah strengthened himself. He took twenty-two wives and there were born to him forty-four sons and sixteen daughters. The other deeds of Abijah, king of Judah, his works and his words, behold, they are written in the book of the prophet Iddo.

ኣ|ነ', 'ወኖመ ፡ አብያ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አበዊሁ ፡ ወተቀብረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ዳዊት ። ወነግሠ ፡ አሳ ፡ ወልዱ ፣ እምድኅሬሁ ። ወበመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለአሳ ፡ ሀድአት ፣ ወአርመ መት ፡ ምድረ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ዐሠርተ ፡ ዓመተ ። ወገብረ ፣ ሠናየ ፣፡ ወርትዐ ፣ በቅድመ፣ አግዚኣብሔር ፡ አምላኩ ፡ ወነሠተ ፡ ቤተ ፣ ምሥዋዓተ ፡ ነኪር ፡ ወአውግረ ፡ 'ወቀ ጥቀጠ ፣ ዙሎ፣ ግልፎሆሙ ፡ ወገዘመ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ አአዋሚሆሙ ። 'ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ለሕዝ በ ፡ ይሁዳ ፣ ከመ ፡ ይኅሥሥዎ ፣ ለእግዚኣብሔር ፡ አምላከ ፣ አበዊሆሙ ፡ ወከመ ፣ ይግበሩ ፡ ሕገ ፤ እግዚኣብሔር ፣ ወትእዛዛቲሁ ። 'ወአግኀሠ ፣ ወነሠተ ፡ አምኩሉ ፡ አህ ጉረ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ አብያተ ፡ ምሥዋዓት ፡ ዘነኪር ፡ ወግልፎሆሙ ። ወነበሩ ፡ በሰላ ም ፡ ኩሉ ፡ አሀጉር ፡ ጸናዕት ፡ በምድረ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ እስመ ፣ አርመመት ፣ ምድር ፡ ወአ ዕረፈት ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ዘይትቃተሎሙ ፡ በውእቱ ፡ ዓመት ፡ እስመ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ አዕረ ፎሙ ፡፡ 'ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ለሕዝበ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ንኅንጻ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወንግበር ፡ ላቲ ፡ አረ ፍተ ፡ ወመኃፍዳተ ፡ ወአናቅጸ ፡ ወመናስግተ ፡ አለ ፡ ቦሙ ፡፣ ንሰፍና ፣ ለምድር ፡ አስ መ ፡ በከመ ፡ ኅሠሥናሁ ፡ ለእግዚኣብሔር ፣ አምላክነ ፡ ውእቱኒ ፡ ኀሠሠነ ፡ ወአዕረፈ ነ ፡ በአጽናፊነ ፡ ወተመጠወነ። ወኮንዎ ፡ ለአሳ ፡ ሰራዊት ፡ ጸናዕት ፡ ወመስተቃትላን ፡
XIV, Abijah slept with his fathers: he was buried in the city of David. * Asa, his son, reigned after him. In the days of Asa the land of Judah was in peace and in rest for ten years. He did what was good and right before the Lord, his God; he destroyed the altars of the foreign (gods) and the high places; he broke all their idols and cut down all their (sacred) woods. He told the people of Judah to seek the Lord, God of their fathers, and to observe the law of the Lord and his commandments. He removed and destroyed in all the cities of Judah all the altars of the foreign (gods) and their idols. All the fortified cities in the land of Judah remained in peace, because the land was in calm and rest. No one fought them in those years, for the Lord had given them tranquility. (Asa) said to the people of Judah: "Let us rebuild these cities and let us remake for them walls, towers, gates, and bars. By these (fortifications) we will dominate the land. Indeed, as we have sought the Lord, our God, he also has sought us, has given us peace at our borders, and has supported us. Asa had an army of valiant warriors able to carry the shield


[p. 683] {#po.23.p683}
እለ ፡ ይክሉ ፡ ጸዊረ ፡ ሐገፋ ፡ ወረምኅ ፡ በምድረ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ፀ፳ ፡ ወበምድረ ፡ ብንያ ም ፡ ወጸፍት ፡ ወነደፍት ፡ ፳፳ ፡ ወ፳ ፡ ፳ ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ፅደው ፡ ጸናዕት ፡ ወመስተቃትላ ን ። ወወፅአ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ዘሬ ፣ ንጉሥ ፡ ዘኢትዮጵያ ፡ በሰራዊት ፡ ጽኑዓን ፣ ትእል ፊተ ፡ አአላፍ ፡ በ፻፻ ፡ ሰረገላት ፡ ወበጽሐ ፡ እስከ ፡ መሬስ ። "ወወፅአ ፣ ንጉሠ ፡ ይ ሁዳ ፡ ለቀበላሁ ፡ ወተዐየነ ፡ ለተቃትሎ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ፈለግ ፡ ዘአንጻረ ፡ ደቡብ ፡ ለውእ ቱ፣ ፈለገ ፡ ማሬስ ። ''ወጸርኀ ፡ አሳ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አግዚኣብሔር ፡ አምላኩ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል፣ እግዚኦ ፡ ኢይሰአን ፡ በኀቤከ ፡ አድኅኖ ፣ በብዙኅኒ ፣ ወበኅዳጥኒ ። አጽንዐነ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ አምላክነ ፡ እስመ ፡፤ ብከ ፡ ተወከልነ ፡ ወበስምከ ፣ መጻአነ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ለአሉ ፣ ሰራ ዊት ፡ ዘአልቦ ፡ ጐልቄ ፤ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ አምላክነ ፡ ኢይጽናዕ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ በቅድሜ ከ። ወቀተሎሙ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ለሰብአ ፡ ኢትዮጵያ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ ሕዝበ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወተሰብሩ ፡ ወጐዩ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ኢትዮጵያ ። “ወሰደደ ፡ አሳ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወሕዝቡ ፡ ድኅሬ ሆሙ ፡ እስከ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ጌዶር ፡ ወወድቁ ፡ ወተቀትሉ ፡ ሰብአ ፣ ኢትዮጵያ ፣ እስመ፣ ኀጥኡ ፡ ዝክረ ፡ እስመ ፡ ተቀጥቀጡ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፣፤ ወበቅድመ ፣ ኀይሉ፣
and the spear (to the number of) three hundred thousand in the land of Judah, and in the land of Benjamin slingers and archers (to the number of) two hundred and fifty thousand, all valiant men of war. Zerah, king of Ethiopia, marched against him with (his) valiant soldiers (to the number of one million and with three hundred chariots and advanced as far as Mareshah. The king of Judah marched to meet him and arranged (his army) in battle in the valley to the north (text: to the north of the valley) of Mareshah. Asa invoked the Lord, his God, saying: "O Lord, it is not impossible with you (to obtain) salvation, (whether one be) numerous or few. Strengthen us, O Lord, our God, for we have confidence in you and it is in your name that we have come against this innumerable army. O * Lord, our God, let no man prevail against you." The Lord struck the people of Ethiopia before the people of Judah; the people of Ethiopia were put to flight and fled. King Asa and his people pursued them as far as the land of Gedor; the people of Ethiopia fell, were struck, and could not recover, for they were crushed before the Lord and before his army and


[p. 684] {#po.23.p684}
ወማሀረክዎሙም ፡ ምሀርካ ፡ ብዙኀ። ”“ወተመትሩ ፡ ወኅልቁ ፡ አዕፅጻዳቲሆሙ ፡እለ፣ተመ ትሩ ፡ እለ ፡ አድያማተ ፤ ጌዶር ፡ እስመ ፡ ኮነ ፣፡ ድንጋፄ ፣ አምኅበ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ላ ፅሌሆሙ ። ወማሀረኩ ፡ ዙሎ ፡ አህጉሪሆሙ ፡ እስመ ፡ ብዙኀ ፡ ምሀርካና ፡ አጥረዩ ፡”እ ስመ ፡ ተዓይነ ፤ ጥሪቶሙ ፡፣ ዘአመዛናዊያን ፡ አሕለቁ ፡ ወነሥኡ ፡ ብዙኀ ፡ መራዕየ ፡ አጣሊ ፡ ወአግማለ ፡ ወተመይጡ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ።
they took from them a great booty. The villages that were in the surroundings of Gedor were sacked and destroyed; they were sacked, because the terror of the Lord was upon them. (The children of Judah) pillaged all the cities and acquired a considerable booty. Indeed, they sacked the sheepfolds (text: the tents of the flocks) of the Hagarenes, took many flocks of sheep (text: goats) and camels, and returned to the land of Jerusalem.
