XV, Azariah, son of Oded, was filled with the spirit of the Lord. He went to meet King Asa and all the people of Judah and of Benjamin and said: "Listen to me, Asa and (you) all the people of Judah and of Benjamin. The Lord is with you, when you are with him. If you seek him, you will find him; but if you forget him, he will abandon you. Numerous will be the days during which (the children of) Israel will no longer worship the true God and (during which) there will no longer be a priest to teach them, nor a law. But (if they convert), the Lord, God of Israel, will return to them and will go before them. In those days there will be no peace for him who goes out (nor) for him who enters, for the wrath of the Lord will be upon all the inhabitants of the lands. People will fight


[p. 685] {#po.23.p685}
ተል ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ለሕዝብ ፡ ወሀገር ፡ ለሀገር ፡ እስመ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ አደንገፆሙ ፡ በ ዙሉ ፡ ምንዳቤ ። "ወአንትሙሰ ፡ ጽንዑ ፡ ወኢይድክሙ ፡እአደዊክሙ ፡አስመ ፡ብክሙ፣ ዕ"ሴተ ፡ ምግባሪክሙ ። "ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዐ ፡ አላንተ ፡ ቃላተ ፡ ወትንቢተ ፡ አዛርያስ ፡ነ ቢይ ፡ ጸንዐ ። ወአውፅአ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ርኩሰ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ አሀጉረ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወብንያም ፡ ወእምዙሉ ፡ አህጉር ፡፣ ዘኀበ ፣ ደብረ ፣ ኤፍሬም ፡ ወአስተሐደሰ ፡ ቤተ ፣ ምሥዋዐ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ዘሀሎ ፡ አንጻረ ፡ ቤተ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ። "ወአስተጋብአ ፡ ሕዝበ ፡ ይሁ ዳ ፡ ወብንያም ፡ ወባሕታውያነ ፡ አለ ፡ የሐድሩ ፣ ምስሌሁ ፡ እአምሕዝበ ፡ ኤፍሬም ፡ወ ምናሴ ፡ ወስምዖን ፣ እስመ ፡ ገብኡ ፣ ወተጻፈቁ ፣ ኀቤሁ ፡ ብዙኃን ፣ እምእስራኤል፣ ሶበ ፡ ርእዩ ፡ ከመ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ አምላኩ ፡ ምስሌሁ ። “ወተጋብኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኢየሩሳ ሌም ፤ በሣልስ ፡ ወርኅ ፡ በዓሥር ፡ ወኃምስ ፡ ዓመተ ፡ መንግሥቱ ፡ ለአሳ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ይ ሁዳ ። ''ወሦዐ ፡ አሜሃ ፣ በይእቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ለእግዚኣብሔር ፡ እምሀርክና ፡ ዘአምጽአ ፡ ፪፻ ፡ አልሀምተ ፡ ወድ፻ ፡ ጠሌ ። “ወአመልአ ፡ በኪዳን ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኅሥሦ ፡ ለእግዚኣብ
against people and city against city, because the Lord will terrify them by all sorts of calamities. But you, be strong and let your hands not weaken, for you will have * the reward of your works." When (Asa) had heard these words and (this) prediction of the prophet Azariah, he was strengthened. He removed all the abominations from all the cities of Judah and of Benjamin and from all the cities that were on Mount Ephraim; he restored the altar of the Lord that was before the house of the Lord. He gathered the people of Judah and of Benjamin and the proselytes who dwelt with them among the populations of Ephraim, of Manasseh, and of Simeon, for many (children) of Israel had returned to join him, when they had seen that the Lord, his God, was with him. They gathered at Jerusalem in the third month of the fifteenth year of the reign of Asa, king of Judah. Then (Asa) sacrificed to the Lord that day from the booty that he had taken seven hundred oxen and seven thousand sheep (text: goats). He bound himself by a covenant that (the people) should seek the Lord, God of


[p. 686] {#po.23.p686}
ሔር ፡ አምላከ ፡ አበዊሁ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ ልቡ ፡ ወእምኩሉ ፡ ኅሊናሁ ፡ "ወኩሉ ፡ ዘኢኅ ሠዎ ፡ ለእግዚኣብሔር ፡ አምላከ ፣ አበዊሁ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ ልቡ ፡ ወእምኩሉ ፡ ኅሊናሁ ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ዘኢሐሰሦ ፡ ለእግዚኣብሔር ፡ አምላከ ፣፡ እስራኤል ፡ ይሙት ፣፡ እምወሬዛ ፡ እ ስከ ፣፡ ልሂቅ ፡ ወእምብእሲሷ ፡፣ እስከ ፡ አንስት ። '"ወመሐለ ፣ በእግዚኣብሔር ፡ በቃል ፡ ዐቢይ ፣ ወበአቅርንት ፣ ወበብዕዛ ። "ወተፈሥሑ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ በእንተ ፡ መሐላ ፡ እ ስመ፣ እምኩሉ ፡ ልቦሙ ፡ ወእምኩሉ ፡ ኅሊናሆሙ ፡ መሐሉ ፡ ወበኩሉ ፡ ልቦሙ ፡ ኀሠሥዎ ። ወተራከቦሙ ፡ ወአፅረፎሙ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ በበ ፡ አጽናፊሆሙ ። “ወለማ እካእ ፡ እሙ ፡ ከመ ፣ ኢትስግድ ፡ አግኀሳ ፣ ለአስጣርጤን ፡ አምላክ ፡ ወቀጥቀጦ ፡ ለምስል ፣፡ ወአውዐዮ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ፈለገ ፡ ቄድሮን ። '' ወባኅቱሰ ፡ ኢሐልቀ ፡ አውግር ፤ ዓዲ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወውስተ ፡ አስራኤል ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ልቡ ፡ ለአሳ ፣ ኮነት ፣ ፍጹመ ፡ በኀበ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ በኀበ ፡ ኩሉ ፣ መዋፅሊ" ሁ ። “ወአብአ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ቅድ ሳት ፡ ዘዳዊት ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ወቅድሳተ ፡ ቤተ ፤ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ አምላኩ ፡ ዘወርቅ ፡ ወዘ ብሩር ። ”ወአልቦ ፡ ዘተቃተሎ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ አሳ ፡ እስከ ፡ ለቋወ፳፣ ዓመተ ፣፡ መንግሥቱ፣ ለአሳ ።
his fathers, with all his heart and with all his soul. That whoever would not seek the Lord, God of his fathers, with all his heart and with all his soul, and that whoever would not seek the Lord, God of Israel, should die, young man or old man, man or woman. He made an oath (of it) to the Lord with a loud voice and at the (sound) of trumpets and horns. All (the children of) Judah rejoiced at (this) oath, for they swore with all their heart and with all their soul and they sought (the Lord) with all their heart. The Lord came to them and gave them peace at each of their borders. (Asa) removed Maacah, his mother, so that she would no longer worship the divinity Astarte; he broke the idol and burned it in the torrent of Kidron. However, the high places did not disappear and still remained in Judah and in Israel, although the heart of Asa was perfect before the Lord during all * his days. He brought (into the temple) the sacred vessels of his father (text: of David, his father) and the sacred objects of gold and silver (destined for) the house of the Lord, his God. No one made war on King Asa until the twenty-fifth (text: thirty-fifth) year of his reign.


[p. 687] {#po.23.p687}
፡ኢ'፤, 'ወበሠላሳ ፡ ወስድስቱ ፤ ዓመተ ፣ መንግሥቱ ፡ ለአሳ ፡ዐርገ ፡ በአሳ፣ ንጉሠ፣ አስራኤል ፡ ላፅለ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወሐነጻ ፡ ለራማእ ፣ ከመ ፡ ኢይርከብ ፡ ምብዋአ ፡ ወሙየፃ አ፣ አሳ ፣ ንጉሠ ፡ ይሁዳ ። ወነሥአ ፡ አሳ ፡ ወርቀ ፡ ወብሩረ ፡ እመዛግብተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ አግዚኣብሔር ፤ ወአመዛግብተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወፈነወ ፡ ለወልደ ፡አዴር ፡ንጉሠ ፡ ሶር ያ ፡ ዘይነብር ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ደማስቆ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ "ሚምአ ፣ ኪዳነ ፡ ማአከሌየ ፡ ወማእ ከሌከ ፡ ወማእከለ ፡ አቡየ ፡ ወአቡከ ። ወናሁ ፡ ፈነውኩ ፡ ለከ ፡ ወርቀ ፡ ወብሩረ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትምጻእ ፡ ወትሁኮ ፡ አምኔየ ፡ ለበአሳ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ አስራኤል ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትሀወክ ፡ ወይትገኀ ሥ ፡ እምኔየ ። ወሰምዖ ፡ ወልደ ፡ አዴር ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ አሳ ፡ ወፈነወ ፣ መላእክቲሁ ፡ ጸናዕተ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ አህጉረ ፤ እስራኤል ። ወተቃተለ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ አእዮን ፣ ወብሔረ ፡ ዳን ፣ ወብሔረ ፡ አብላ ፡ ማኢን ፡ ወኩሎ ፡ አድያመ ፡ ንፍታሌም ። "ወዕበ ፡ ሰምዐ ፡ በአሳ ፡ ፈነወ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዳግመ ፡ ኢይኅንጽዋ ፡ ለራማእ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ግብሩ ። "ወአ ሳ፣ ንጉሠ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ አስተጋብአ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ሕዝበ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ ወጾረ፣ ኩሎ፣ አብነ ፡ ራማእ ፡ ወፅጸዊሃ ፡ ዘሐነጸ ፡ በአሳ ፡ ወአሕነጸ ፡ በውእቱ ፡ ዕፀው ፡ ወአብን ፡ ብ ሔረ ፡ ገባኢ ፡ ወብሔረ ፡ መሳፍአ ። 'ወበውአቱ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ መጽአ ፡ አናኒ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ኀ
XVI, In the twenty-sixth (text: thirty-sixth) year of the reign of Asa, Baasha, king of Israel, marched against Judah and built Ramah, so that Asa, king of Judah, would find (neither) entry (nor) exit. Asa took gold and silver from the treasures of the house of the Lord and from the treasures of the house of the king, and sent it to the son of Hadad (Ben-Hadad), king of Syria, who dwelt in the land of Damascus, saying: "Establish an alliance between me and you (as) between my father and your father. Behold, I have sent you gold and silver, so that you may come to disturb (and divert) from me Baasha, king of Israel, and that, being disturbed, he may move away from me." The son of Hadad (Ben-Hadad) listened to King Asa and sent his valiant chiefs against the cities of Israel. He struck the land of Ijon, the land of Dan, the land of Abel-Maim, and all the surroundings of Naphtali. When Baasha had learned (this), he sent (messengers) so that they would no longer build the city of Ramah, and all the work was stopped. Asa, king of Judah, gathered all the people of Judah (who) took and transported all the stones and the timbers of Ramah with which Baasha had built. He had built with these timbers and (these) stones the city of Geba and the city of Mizpah. In those days the prophet Hanani


[p. 688] {#po.23.p688}
በ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ አሳ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ እስመ ፣ ተወከልከ ፡ በንጉሠ ፡ ሶራዊያን ፡ ወኢተወከል ከ ፣ በአግዚኣብሔር ፡ አምላክከ ፡ በእንተዝ ፡ ድኅኑ ፡ ሰራዊተ ፡ ሶራዊያን ፡ አምአደዊ ከ ። "ወአኮኑ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ኢትዮጵያ ፡ ወሰብአ ፡ ልብያስ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ብዙኃነ ፡ ወሐያላነ ፡ በጽ ንዕ ፡ ወበትብዕና ፤ ወበአፍራስ ፡ ወመስተጽዕናን ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ጥቀ ፡ ወአመሰ ፡ ተወከል ከ ፣ በእግ”ዚኣብሔር ፡ አምላክከ ፣ ያገብኦሙ ፡ ወይሜጥዎሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አደዊከ ፡ "አ ስመ ፡ አዕይንቲሁ ፡ ለእግዚኣብሔር ፡ ይኔጽራ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ምድር ፡ ከመ ፡ ያጽንዕ ፡ ሕሊናሆሙ ፡ ለፍጹማነ ፡ ልብ ። ወይእዜኒ ፡ በኢያእምሮትከ ፡ ጌገይከ ፡ በዝንቱ ፣ ቃ ል ፣፡ ወእምይእዜሰ ፣ ይከውን ፡ ምስሌከ ፡ ቀትል ። "'ወተምፅዐ ፡ አሳ ፣ ንጉሥ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ኦናኒ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ወአንበሮ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ፡ እስመ ፡-ተምዕዐ ፣ ንጉሥ ፡ አሳ ፣ ላዕለ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቃል ፣ ወአርኩሰ ፡ አሳ ፡ በውእቱ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ በማእከለ ፡ ሕዝብ ። ''ወ ነዮን ፡ ቃላተ ፡ አሳ ፡ ቀደምት ፡ ወደኅርት ፡ ጽሑፋት ፡ውስተ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ነገሥተ ፡ይ ሁዳ ፡ ወእስራኤል ። ”ወደወየ ፡ አሳ ፡ አመ ፡ ፡ ወክ ፡ ዓመተ ፡ መንግሥቱ ፡ ደወየ ፣ እገሪሁ ፣ ብዙኀ ፣ ወበደዌሁኒ ፣ ኢኅሠሦ ፡ ለእግዚኣብሔር ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ለዐቀብተ ፣ ሥራይ ። ”ወኖመ ፡ አሳ ፡ ምስለ ፣ አበዊሁ ፣ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በጓ ፡ ወአኃቲ ፡ ዓመተ ፣፡ መን
came to find King Asa and said to him: "Because you have trusted in the king of the Syrians and have not had confidence in the Lord, your God, because of that the army of the Syrians has escaped from your hands. Were not the people of Ethiopia and the people of Libya numerous and valiant, in strength and in courage, with horses and very numerous horsemen, and because you had confidence in the * Lord, your God, did he not deliver and hand them over into your hands? Indeed, the eyes of the Lord look upon all the earth, so as to strengthen the courage of those who are perfect of heart. Now that you have sinned in your unconsciousness in this matter, from now on war will be made against you." King Asa became angry with the prophet Hanani and put him in prison, for King Asa had become irritated because of these words. In those days Asa caused blood to flow (text: defiled) among the people. Behold, the deeds of Asa, the first and the last, are written in the book of the Kings of Judah and of Israel. Asa fell ill in the thirty-ninth year of his reign; he suffered much from his feet; in his illness he did not seek the Lord, but the physicians. Asa slept with his


[p. 689] {#po.23.p689}
ግሥቱ ።፡ ''ወቀበርዎ ፣ ውስተ ፡ መቃብር ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ ለሊሁ ፡ በሀገረ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ወአስከብ ዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡.ዓራቱ ፡ ወመልእዎ ፡ እምአፈው ፡ ወአምዘመደ ፡ ዕፍረት ፡ ዘይገብሩ ፡ ወገብሩ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ሙፃአ ፡ ቀበር ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ ጥቀ ። ኢዝ, 'ወነግሠ ፡ ዮሳፍጥ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ እምድኅሬሁ ። ወጸንዐ ፣ ዮሳፍጥ ፡ በመንግሥ ቱ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ እስራኤል ። ወአንበረ ፡ ሰራዊተ ፡ ጸናዕተ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ኩሉ ፡ አህጉረ ፣ ይሁ ዳ ፡ ጸናዕት ፡ ወሜመ ፡ መሳፍንተ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ በሓውርተ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወበኩሉ ፡ አህ ጉረ ፡ ኤፍሬም ፡ እለ ፡ ገብኡ ፡ ለአሳ ፡ ለአቡሁ ። "ወሀሎ ፡ አግዚኣብሔር ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ለዮሳፍጥ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሖረ ፡ በፍናወ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ቀደምት ። ወኢኀሠሠ ፡ አማልክተ ፡ ነኪር ፡'ዘእንበለ ፣ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ አምላከ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ኀሠሠ ፡ ወበትእዛዘ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ሖረ ወአኮ ፡ በግብረ ፣ እስራኤል ። "ወአርትዐ ፣ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ወአጽንዐ ፡ መንግሥቶ፣ በእደዊሁ ። "ወአምጽኡ ፡ ሎቱ ፣ ኩሉ ፡ ሕዝበ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ አምኃ ፣ ለኢዮሳፍጥ ፡ ን ጉሥ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ ክብር ፡ ወብዕል ። "ወገብአ ፡ ልቡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፍናወ ፡ እግ ዚኣብሔር ፡ ወዓዲ ፡ ሰረዎሙ ፡ ወደምሰሶዕሙ ፡ ለአውግር ፡ ወለአአዋም ፡ እምኩሉ፣ ምድረ ፤ ይሁዳ ። 'ወበሣልስ ፣ ዓመተ ፡ መንግሥቱ ፡ ፈነወ ፡ መሳፍንተ ፡ ወውሉደ ፡ ጸ ናዕተ ፣ ለአብድያስ ፣ ወለዘካርያስ ፣ ወለታናዊሄል ፡ ወለሚክያስ ፣ ከመ ፡ ይምሀሩ ፡ በ
fathers and died in the forty-first year of his reign. They buried him in the sepulcher that he himself had built in the city of David; they laid him on a bed that they filled with aromatics and (all) sorts of perfumes prepared (specially) and they made for him very great funeral rites. XVII, Jehoshaphat, his son, reigned after him. Jehoshaphat during his reign strengthened himself against Israel. He placed valiant soldiers in all the fortified cities of Judah and established governors in all the lands of Judah and in all the cities of Ephraim that had been taken by Asa, his father. The Lord was with Jehoshaphat, because he walked in the first ways of his father. He did not seek the gods of the foreign (peoples), but he sought the Lord, God of his father. He walked according to the commandments of his father and not according to the works of Israel. The Lord made the royalty prosperous and strong in his hands. All the people of Judah * brought presents to King Jehoshaphat; he had great glory and (great) riches. His heart turned toward the ways of the Lord. He also caused to disappear and destroyed the high places and the (sacred) woods from all the land of Judah. In the third year of his reign he sent chiefs and powerful men, Obadiah, Zechariah, Nethaneel, and Micaiah, so as to instruct (the people) in the


[p. 690] {#po.23.p690}
አህጉረ ፡ ይሁዳ ። ወምስሌሆሙኒ ፡ ፈነወ ፡ ሌዋዊያነ ፡ ወስሙኢያስ ፡ ወናትያስ ፡ ወዝ ብድያስ ፡ ወኢያሄል ፤ ወሶዕማሮት ፣ ወኢዮታን ፣ ወአዶንያስ ፡ ወአጣስ ፣ ወአይንያስ ፣ ሌዋዊያን ፡ ወእለኒ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ኤልሰማ ፣ ወኢዮራም ፡ ካህናት ። "ወመሀሩ ፡ በአ ህጉረ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወምስሌሆሙኒ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ሕጉ ፡ ለእግዚኣብሔር ፡ ወአንሶሰዉ ፡ ው ስተ ፣ ኩሉ ፡ አህጉር ፣ ዘይሁዳ ፣ ወመሀርዎሙ ፣ ለሕዝብ ። "ወኮነ ፡ ድንጋፄ ፡ እግዚ ኣብሔር ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ዙሉ ፡ መንግሥታተ ፡ ምድር ፡ አለ ፡ አጽናፈ ፣ ይሁዳ ፣ ወኢተቃ ተልዎ ፡ ለኢዮሳፍጥ ። '' አምኢሎፍሊ ፡ ያመጽኡ ፡ ሎቱ ፣ ለኢዮሳፍጥ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ አም ኃ ፡ ወርቀ ፣ ወብሩረ ፡ ወግብረ ። ወሰብዐ ፡ ዐረብኒ ፡ ያመጽኡ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ አባግዐ ፡ አትቡ ዐ ፡ ፳፻ ፡ ወ፪፻ ፡ ወሐራጊተኒ ፣ ጠሊ ፡፳፻ ፡ ወ፪፻ ። ”ወኢዮሳፍጥ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ይጸንዕ ፣ የሐውር ፡ ወኀበ ፡ ይከብር ፡ ጥቀ ። ወሐነጸ ፡ አብያተ ፡ ውስተ ፡ በሐውርተ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወአህጉረ ፡ ጸናዕተ ። "ወገብረ ፡፣ ብዙኀ ፡ ተግባረ ፡፣ ሎቱ ፡ በይሁዳ ። ወአዕዳው ፡ ጸናዕ ት ፡ ወመስተቃትላን ፡ ወጽኑዕ ፡ በኢየሩሳሌም ። "ወዝንቱ ፣፡ ውእቱ ፡ ጐልቆሙ፣።በ በ፣ ቤተ ፡ አበዊሆሙ ፡ ወበይሁዳ ፡ ርኡሳነ ፡ ፲፻ ፡ እድናስ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ወምስሌሁ ፡ ፅደው ፡ ጽኑዓን ፡ በኀይል ፡ በ፳ ፡ ”ወምስሌሆሙ ፡፣ ኢዮአናን ፡፣መስፍን ፡ወምስሌሁ
cities of Judah. He sent with them the Levites Shemaiah, Nethaniah, Zebadiah, Asahel, Shemiramoth, Jehonathan, Adonijah, Eliab, and Tobadonijah (text: Adonijah); with these Levites were Elishama and Jehoram, priests. They instructed (the people) in the cities of Judah, (having) with them the book of the law of the Lord; they traveled through all the cities of Judah and instructed the people. The terror of the Lord was upon all the kingdoms of the lands surrounding Judah and they did not make war on Jehoshaphat. The Philistines brought to King Jehoshaphat presents, gold, silver, and tributes. The people of Arabia brought him seven thousand seven hundred rams and seven thousand seven hundred goats. Jehoshaphat grew to the point of becoming very powerful and very illustrious. He built barracks in the land of Judah and fortified cities. He did many works in Judah. The valiant and courageous men of war (were found) in Jerusalem. Here is their numbering according to each paternal house. In Judah the chiefs of thousands were: Adnah, the chief (principal): with him were three hundred thousand men valiant in combat; after him (text: with them) was Jehohanan,


[p. 691] {#po.23.p691}
፡ ፳፳ ፤ ወ፳፻ ፡ ፅደው ፣ “ወምስሌሁኒ ፣ ማአስያስ ፡ ወልደ ፡፣ ዝክሪ ፡ ዘይጸመዶ ፡ ለእግዚኣብሔር ፡ ወምስሌሁኒ ፡ ዕደው ፡ ጸናዕት ፡ ፳፳ ። '"ወእምነገደ ፡ ብንያም ፡ ብእ ሲ ፡ ጽኑዕ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ኤልያብ ፡ ወምስሌሴሁኒ ፡ እደው ፡ ነዳፍያን ፡ ወወጻፍያን ፡ ፳፳ ። ” ወምስሌሁኒ ፡ ዮዛብድ ፣፡ ወምስሌሁ ፡ ፲፳ ፡ ወሰማንያ ፡ ፻ ፡ ጽኑዓን ፡ ለተቃት ሎ ። ”ወአሉ ፡ ዙሎሙ ፡ ይትለአክዎ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ዘአንበረ ፡ ዮሳፍጥ ፡ ውስ ተ ፡ አህጉር ፡ ጽኑዓት ፣ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ በሐውርተ ፡ ይሁዳ ። ኢነ 'ወኮኖ ፣ ለዮሳፍጥ ፤ ፈድፋደ ፣ ብዕል ፣ ወስብሐ”ት ። ወአውሰበ ፡ ዮሳፍ ጥ ፡ ብአሲተ ፡ እምቤተ ፡፣ አከዐብ ። ወወረደ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ፪ ፡ ዓመት ፡ ኅበ ፡ አከ ዐብ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ሰማርያ ። ወሐረደ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ አከዐብ ፣ አልህምተ ፣ ወአባግዐ ፡ ብዙ ኃ ፡ ወለሕዝብኒ ፡ አለ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ወአፍቀሮ ፡ ወሰአሎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይዕርግ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ገ ለዓድ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አከዐብ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ አስራኤል ፡ ለዮሳፍጥ ፣ ንጉሠ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ነዓ ፡፤ ምስሌየ ፡ ገለዓድ ። ወአውሥአ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ አንሰ ፣ ከማከ ፡ ወሕዝብየ ፡ከመ ፡ ሕ ዝብከ ፡ ለተቃትሎ ። 'ወይቤሎ ፡ ዮሳፍጥ ፡፣ ለንጉሠ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ኅሥሦ ፡-እንከ፣ዮ ም ፡ ለአግዚአብሔር ። ወአደመ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ አስራኤል ፡ ነቢያተ ፡ አርባዕተ ፡፻ ፡እ ደወ፣ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ እመሰ ፡ እሖር ፡ ውስተ ፡፣ በራሞት ፡ ሀገር ፣ እንተ ፡ ብሔረ ፣
the commander: with him were two hundred and eighty thousand (text: two hundred and eight thousand) men; after him was Amasiah, son of Zichri, who served the Lord: with him were two hundred thousand valiant men. In the tribe of Benjamin was a valiant man called Eliada: with him were two hundred thousand archers and slingers; after him was Jehozabad: with him were one hundred and eighty thousand (text: one hundred and eight thousand) men of war. All these (men) served the king, without counting those whom Jehoshaphat had placed in the fortified cities in all the lands of Judah. XVIII, Jehoshaphat had much riches and * glory. Jehoshaphat married a woman from the house of Ahab. Two years later, he went down to Ahab in the land of Samaria. Ahab sacrificed many oxen and sheep for him and for the people who were with him; he treated him with friendship and asked him to go up with him against (Ramoth of) Gilead. Ahab, king of Israel, said to Jehoshaphat, king of Judah: "Come with me against (Ramoth of) Gilead." (Jehoshaphat) answered and said to him: "I as you and my people as your people (are one) to fight." Jehoshaphat said to the king of Israel: "Consult then today the Lord." The king of Israel summoned four hundred prophets and said to them: "Must I go to fight against the city of Ramoth which is in the land of Gilead, or must I abstain?"


[p. 692] {#po.23.p692}
ገለዓድ ፡ ለቀትል ፡ አው ፡ እቁም ። ወአውሥኡ ፡ ወይቤልቻ ፡ ዕርግ ፡ ወሖር ፡ ወይሴር ኅ ፡ አግዚአብሔር ፡ በእደዊሁ ፡ ለንጉሥ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ዮሳፍጥ ፡ አልቦኑ ፡ ዝየ ፡ ነቢ የ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወዓዲ ፣ ኅሥሥ ፡ በኅቤሁ ። "ወአውሥአ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ለዮሳፍጥ ፡ ተረፈኒ ፡ ዓዲ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ ብአሲ ፣ ከመ ፣ እኅሥሥ ፡ በአዴሁ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአንሰ ፡ ጸላአክዎ ፡ ለውአቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኢይትኔበይ ፡ ላዕሌየ ፣ ሠናየ ፡ እስመ ፡ በዙሉ ፡ መዋዕሊሁ ፡ ኢይትኔበይ ፤ ላዕሌየ ፡ ሠናይተ ፡ ወዝንቱ ፡ ው እቱ ፡ ሚክያስ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ይምላእ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ዮሳፍጥ ፡ ኢይንብብ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ዘንተ ። ወጸውዓ ፡ አከዓብ ፣ ንጉሠ ፡ እስራኤል ፡፤ አሐደ ፡ እምሕጽዋኒሁ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ፍጡነ ፣ አምጽኦ ፡ ለሚክያስ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ይምላእ ። "ወንጉሠ ፣ አስራኤል ፡ ወዮሳፍጥ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ይነብሩ ፡ ዲበ ፡ መንበረ ፣ መንግሥት ፣ በዘዘዚአሆሙ ፣ ወልቡሳን ፣ አዕጽፍ ተ ፡ ወይነብሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጥፕኅተ ፣ መርኅበ ፡ አንቀጸ ፡ ሰማርያ ፡ ወኩሎሙ ፡ ነቢያት ፡ ይትኔበዩ ፡ በቅድሜሆሙ ።፡ "ወገብረ ፡ ሰዴቅያ” ስ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ከናአን ፡ አቅርንተ ፣ ዘሐ ን ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ይቤ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በእላንቱ ፣ አቅርንት ፡ ትወግኦሙ ፡ ለሶራ ውያን ፡ እስከ ፡ ታኅልቆሙ ። ''ወኩሎሙ ፡ ነቢያት ፡ ይትኔበዩ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ዕርግ ፡ በራሞት ፡ እንተ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ገለዓድ ፡ ወይሴርሐከ ፡ ወይሜጥዎሙ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ው ስተ ፡ እደ ፡ ንጉሥ ። ”ወዝክቱ ፡ ዘሖረ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጸውዖ ፣ ለሚኪያስ ፡ ተናገ ሮ ፡ አንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ነዮሙ ፡ ነቢያት ፡ ተናገሩ ፣ በአሐቲ ፡ ቃላት ፡ ሠናይተ ፡ በእንተ ፡
They answered and said to him: "Go up and go. The Lord will give success, (by delivering the city) into the hands of the king." Jehoshaphat said to him: "Is there not here a prophet of the Lord? Inquire also through him." The king of Israel answered and said to Jehoshaphat: "There remains for me still one man through whom I (can) consult the Lord, but I hate this man, for he does not prophesy (anything) good to me and all throughout his days he has not prophesied (anything) favorable to me: it is Micaiah, son of Imlah." Jehoshaphat said to him: "Let not the king speak thus." Ahab, king of Israel, called one of his eunuchs and said to him: "Bring quickly Micaiah, son of Imlah." (Ahab), king of Israel, and Jehoshaphat, king of Judah, were sitting, each on his royal throne, and clothed (each) in (his) mantle. They were sitting in the open space at the entrance of the gate of Samaria and all the prophets were prophesying before them. * Zedekiah, son of Chenaanah, (made) for himself horns of iron and said: "Thus says the Lord: With these horns you will strike the Syrians until you have destroyed them." All the prophets were prophesying, saying: "Go up against Ramoth which is in the land of Gilead; the Lord will give you success and will deliver it into the hands of the king." The messenger who had gone to call Micaiah spoke to him, saying: "Behold, the prophets have pronounced with one voice (things) favorable to the king. Let


[p. 693] {#po.23.p693}
ንጉሥ ፡ ወይኩን ፡ ቃልከ ፡ ከመ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ወትንብብ ፡ ሠናይተ ። "ወይ ቤ ፡ ሚክያስ ፣ ሕያው ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እስመ ፣ ዘይቤለኒ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኪያ፣ሃ ፡ እትናገር ። “ወመጽአ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ለሚክያስ ፡ እሖርኑ ፡ በራ ሞት ፡ እንተ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ገለዓድ ፡ ለተቃትሎ ፡ ወአመ ፡ አኮ ፡ እንበር ። ወይቤ ፣ ሚክ ያስ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ፅርግ ፡ ወሑር ፡ ወይሴርሐከ ፡ ወይገብኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እደዊክሙ ። "ወይቤ ሎ።፣ ንጉሠ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ሚመጠነ ፡ አምሕለከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢትንግረኒ ፡ በኅቤየ ፡ ዘኢኮ ነ ፡ ጽድቀ ፡ በኅበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። “ወይቤ ፡ ርኢክዎሙ ፡ ዝርዋነ ፡ ለአስራኤል ፡ ው ስተ ፡ አድባር ፡ ከመ ፡ አባግፅ ፡ ዘአልቦ ፡ ኖላዊ ፡ ወይቤ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አልቦሙ ፡ ሰፋኔ ፡ ይግብኡ ፡ ዙሎሙ ፡ በበ ፡ ማኅድሪሆሙ ፡ በሰላም ። '"ወይቤ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ እስራኤ ል ፡ ለኢዮሳፍጥ ፡ ኢይቤለክኑ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይትኔበይ ፡ ላዕሌየ ፡ ሠናይተ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ እ ኪተ ። “ወይቤ ፡ አኮ ፡ ክመዝ ፡ ስምዑ ፡ቃለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ርኢክዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔ ር፣ዲበ፣መንበሩ ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ኃይለ ፡ ሰማያት ፣ ይቀውሙ ፡በየማኑ ፡ ወበጸጋሙ ። “ወይ ቤ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ መኑ ፡ ያስሕቶ ፡ ለአከዐብ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ አስራኤል ፡ ከመ ፡ ይዕርግ ፡ ወይደቅ ፡ በራሞት ፡ ዘገለዓድ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ከመዝ ፣ ወዝንቱ ፡ ይቤ ፣ ከመዝ ። ወወጽአ ፡ መንፈስ ፣ ወቆመ ፣ ቅድሜሁ ፡ ለአግዚአብሔር ፡ ወይቤ ፡ አነ ፣፤ አስሕ ቶ። ወይቤ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በምንት ፡ ታስሕቶ ። ”ወይቤ ፡ አወፅእ ፡ ወእከውን ፡ መ
your word be like that of each of them and let it announce (things) favorable." Micaiah said: "The Lord is living! What the Lord has said to me, I will declare." He came to the king. The king said to Micaiah: "Must I go to fight against Ramoth which is in the land of Gilead, or must I abstain?" The prophet Micaiah said: "Go up and go. (The Lord) will give you success and (the people of Ramoth) will be delivered into your hands." The king of Israel said to him: "How many times have I adjured you not to say before me what is not the truth before the Lord?" (Micaiah) said: "I saw Israel scattered in the mountains like sheep that have no shepherd. The Lord has said: They have no leader; let each return in peace to his dwelling." The king of Israel said to Jehoshaphat: "Did I not tell you that he did not prophesy good to me, but evil?" (Micaiah) said: "It is not so: Listen to the word of the Lord. I saw the Lord on his throne and all the army of the heavens was standing at his right and at his left. The Lord said: Who will deceive Ahab, king of Israel, so that he may go up and fall before Ramoth of Gilead? * One spoke in one way and another spoke in another. A spirit came forward, presented himself before the Lord and said: It is I who will deceive him. The Lord said: How will you deceive him? (The spirit) said: I will go and I will become a spirit of falsehood in


[p. 694] {#po.23.p694}
ንፈሰ ፡ ሐሰት ፡ በአፈ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ነቢያቲሁ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ታስሕቶ ፡ ትክ ል ፡ ፃእ ፡ ወግበር ፡ ከመዝ ። ወናሁ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ ወደየ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ውስ ተ ፡ አፈ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ነቢያቲሁ ፡ መንፈሰ ፡ ሐሰት ፡ ወእግዚአብሔር ፡ ሐለየ ፡ ላዕሌክ ፡ እ ኪተ ። ”ወቀርበ ፡ ሴዴቅያስ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ከናአን ፡ ወጸፍዖ ፡ መልታሕቶ ፡ ለሚክያስ ፡ ወይ ቤሎ ፡ አየ ፡ ፍኖተ ፣ ሐለፈ ፡ መንፈሰ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡፣ ከመ ፡ ይትናገር ፡ ምስሌከ ። ወይቤ ፡ ሚክያስ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ትሬኢ ፡ በይእቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡እንዘ ፡ ትበውእ ፡ ወትወፅእ ፡ እም ውሥጥ ፡ ለውሥጥ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትፃአ ። ”ወይቤ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡፣ እስራኤል ፡፤ ንሥአዎ ፡ ለሚ ክያስ ። ወይቤ ፡ አግብአዎ ፡ ኀበ ፣ እሜር ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ብሔር ፣ ወኅበ፣ ኢዮአስ፣መ ልአክ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ ንጉሥ ። ”ወትነግርዎሙ ፡ ከመዝ ፣ ወከመዝ ፣ ይቤ ፣ ንጉሥ ፣ አን ብርዎ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ፡ ወይብላዕ ፡ ኅብስተ ፡ በምንዳቤ ፡ ወይስተ ይ ፡ ማየ ፡ በምንዳቤ ፡ እስከ ፡ እትመየጥ ፡ በሰላም ። ” ወይቤ ፡ ሚክያስ ፡ እመ ፡ ተመይ ጦ ፡ ተመየጥከ ፡ በሰላም ፣ ኢተናገረ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በላዕሌየ ፡ ወስምፁዑ ፡ ዘንተ፣ ዙልክሙ ፡ አሕዛብ ። ”ወዐርገ ፤ ንጉሠ ፡ እስራኤል ፤ ወኢዮሳፍጥ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ይሁዳ ፣ በራሞት ፡ እንተ ፣ ገለኣድ ። ”ወይቤሎ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ እስራኤል ፡፤ ለኢዮሳፍጥ ፡ አልብሰኒ ፣ አልባሳተ ፡ ነኪራተ ፡ ወአእበውእ ፡ውስትተ ፡ ቀትል ፡ ወአንተ ፡ ልበስ ፡ አልባስየ ፡ ወተላበ ሰ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ወቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቀትል። ”ወንጉሠ ፡ ሶርያ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ዙሎሙ፣ መላእክተ ፡ ሰረገላት ፡ አለ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ኢትትቃተሉ ፡ ወኢለንዑስ ፡ ወኢለዐ
the mouth of all his prophets. The Lord said to him: You can deceive him; go and do so. Behold, now the Lord has put thus the spirit of falsehood in the mouth of all his prophets; the Lord has decided evil against you." Zedekiah, son of Chenaanah, approached, struck Micaiah on the cheek and said to him: "By what way has the spirit of the Lord passed to speak to you?" Micaiah said: "Behold, you will see it the day when you will pass (text: you will enter and go out) from chamber to chamber to escape." The king of Israel said: "Seize Micaiah." He added: "Deliver him to Amon, governor of the city, and to Joash, commander, son of the king. You will say to them this: Thus has said the king: Put this (man) in prison; let him eat bread in affliction and let him drink water in affliction, until I return in peace." Micaiah said: "If you really return in peace, the Lord has not spoken through me. Hear this, you all, peoples." The king of Israel and Jehoshaphat, king of Judah, went up against Ramoth of Gilead. The king of Israel said to Jehoshaphat: "Clothe me in foreign garments and I will enter into the battle; as for you, clothe yourself in my garments." The king of Israel disguised himself and entered into the battle. The king of Syria commanded all the chiefs of the chariots who were with him and said: "Do not fight either against the small, or against the great, but


[p. 695] {#po.23.p695}
ቢይ ፡ ዘአንበለ ፡ ለንጉሠ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ባሕቲቱ ። “ወሶበ ፡ ርአይዎ ፡ መላእክተ ፡ ሰረገ ላቲሁ ፡ ለንጉሠ ፡ ሶርያ ፡ ይቤሉ ፡ "ዝንቱ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ወዐገትዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይቅትልዎ ። ወዓውየወ ፡ ኢዮሳፍጥ ፡ ወእግዚአብሔር ፡ አድኀኖ ፡ ወሜጦሙ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እምኔሁ ። ”ወኮነ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ርእዩ ፡ መላእክተ ፡ ሰረገላት ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢኮነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ወተግኅሠ ፡ እምኔሁ ። ”ወአሐዱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ወሰቀ ፡ ቀስ ቶ ፡ ወኢተኅዚዩቦ ፡ ነደፎ ፡ ወሄፅ ፡ ለንጉሠ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ማዕከለ ፡ ሰንቡኡ ፡ ወማዕከ ለ ፡ እንግድዓሁ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ለሊቀ ፡ አፍራሲሁ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ይፄዓን ፡ ሚጥ ፡ እዴክ ፡ ወአ ውፅአኒ ፡ እምቀትል ፡ እስመ ፡ ሐመምኩ ። “ወተመውአ ፡ ቀትል ፡ በይእቲ ፡ዕለት ፡ ወ ንጉሠ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ይቀውም ፡ ዲበ ፡ ሰረገላቲሁ ፡ እስከ ፡ መስየ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ገጾሙ ፡ ለሰ
only against the king of Israel." When the chiefs of the chariots of the king of Syria caught sight of (Jehoshaphat), they said: "* It is the king of Israel," and they surrounded him to kill him. Jehoshaphat cried out (to) the Lord and the Lord saved him and turned them away from him. When the chiefs of the chariots saw that it was not he, the king of Israel, they moved away from him. (But) a man drew his bow and shot (an arrow) without aiming, he struck the king of Israel between the lungs and the breastplate. (The king) said to his charioteer beside whom he was mounted: "Turn the bridle and bring me out of the battle, for I am wounded." The battle was lost that day. The king of Israel stood on his chariot until the evening before the people of Syria and he died at the setting of the sun.
ብአ ፡ ሶርያ ፡ ወሞተ ፡ ዐሪቦ ፡ ፀሓይ። እ1እ, 'ወተመይጠ ፤ ኢዮሣፍጥ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤቱ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ በሰላም ። ወወፅአ ፡ ይትቀበሎ ፡ ኢዩ ፡ ወልደ ፡ አናኒ ፤ ነቢይ ፣ ወይቤሎ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፣ ኢዮሣፍጥ ፡ እመሰ ፡ ለኃጥእ ፡ አንተ ፡ ትትራዳአ ፡ ወዘሂ ፡ ጸልአ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፤ አንተ ፡ ታፈቅር ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፣ መጽአ ፡ መቅሠፍተ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ላዕሌክ ። ቃላት ፡ ሠናያት ፡ ተረክበ ፡፣ ላዕሌከ ፡ እስመ ፡ አግኃሥከ ፡ አማልክተ ፡ እምድረ ፡ ይሁ ዳ፣ አርታዕፅከ፣ ልበከ ፣ከመ ፡ ትኅሥሦ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር። 'ወኀደረ ፡ ዮሳፍጥ ፡ ውስተ፣ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወወፅአ ፡ካዕበ ፡ ምስለ ሕዝብ ፡ አምቤርሳቤሕ ፡ እስከ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ኤፍሬም
XIX, Jehoshaphat, king of Judah, returned in peace to his palace in Jerusalem. The prophet Jehu, son of Hanani, advanced to meet King Jehoshaphat and said to him: "Since you help the sinner and you love him who hates the Lord, because of that the punishment of the Lord will come upon you. (But) there have been found in you good works, for you have caused the (false) gods to disappear from the land of Judah and you have made your heart straight to seek the Lord." Jehoshaphat dwelt in Jerusalem. He went again toward the people from Beersheba to the mountain of Ephraim and he brought them back to the


[p. 696] {#po.23.p696}
ወሜጦሙ ፡ ኀበ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አምላከ ፡ አበዊሆሙ ። ”ወሜመ ፡ መኳንንተ ፡ ው ስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ አኅጉረ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወውስተ ፡ ዓፅዋን ፡ በሀገር ። "ወይቤ ፣ ለመኳንንት ፡ አ እምሩ ፡ ዘትገብሩ ፡ አንትሙ ፡ እስመ ፡ አኮ ፡ ለሰብእ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ አላ ፡ ለአግዚአብሔር ፡ ወምስሌክሙ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ፍትሕ ። 'ወይእዜኒ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ፍርሀተ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ላዕሌ ክሙ ፡ ወትዕቀቡ ፡ ወትግበሩ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኢኮነ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አግዚአብሔር ፡ ዓመፃ ፣ ወኢኀ ፊረ ፡ገጽ ፡ ወኢነሚአ ፡ ኅልያን ። "አስመ ፡ በኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ጫመ ፡ ኢዮሳፍጥ ፣ ከህና ተ ፡ ወሌዋዊያነ ፡ ወሊቃናተ ፡ አበወ ፣ እስራኤል ፡ ይፍትሑ ፡ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወይኩ ንኑ ፡ ኩሎ ፣ ዘየኅድር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ። "ከመዝ ፣ ሠርፆሙ ፣ ወይቤ ፡ዘንተ፣ ግበሩ ፡፣ በፍርሃተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡፣ ወበርትዕ ፣ ወበልብ ፡፣ ንጹሕ ። "ኩሉ ፣ ብአሲ፣ ዘመጽአ ፡ ለፍትሕ ፡፤ ለአኀኅዊሁ ፡ አለ ፣ የኅድሩ ፡ ውስተ ፣፡ በሐውርቲሆሙ ፡፣ ማዕከ ለ ፡ ደም ፡ ወደም ፡ ወማዕከለ ፡ ሥርዓት ፡ ወነገር ፡ ወጽድቅ ፣ ወፍትሕ ፣ ወታስተጋ ብፅዎሙ ፡ ወኢየአብሱ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፤ ወኢይኩን ፡ መዓቱ ፡ ለአግዚአብሔር ፡ ላዕሌክሙ ፡ ወላዕለ ፤፡ አኅዊክሙ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ግበሩ ፡ ወኢተአብሱ ። ''ወናሁ ፡ አማርያ፣ ካህን ፣ ይሰፍነክሙ ፣ በኩሉ ፡ ቃለ ፡ አግዚአብሔር ፣ ወዝብዲያስ ፣ ወልደ ፡ እስማዔ ል ፡ መስፍን ፡ ለቤተ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዘንጉሥ ፡ ወጸሐፍት ፡ ወሌዋውያን ፣ ቅድ
Lord, God of their fathers. He established judges in all the cities of Yehudah and in the fortified places of (each) city. He said to the judges: "Know what you are doing, for you will not judge for men, but for the Lord, and you will answer for the sentence. Now let the fear of the Lord be upon * you, so that you may act with vigilance, for iniquity is not with the Lord, nor the acceptance of persons, nor the receiving of corrupting gifts." Yehoshaphat established in Yerushalayim (some) priests, Levites, and heads of families of Esra'el, to judge (the affairs) of the Lord and to render justice to all those who dwelt in Yerushalayim. He instructed them thus and said: "Do this in the fear of the Lord, with uprightness and with a pure heart. All those of (your) brothers dwelling in their cities who shall come for a judgment between kinsman and kinsman (text: blood and blood), between rule and precept and (between) law and ordinance, you shall reconcile them, so that they do not sin against the Lord and the wrath of the Lord does not fall upon you and upon your brothers. Do thus, so that you do not sin. Behold that Amaryah, priest, shall be set over you for all matters concerning the Lord, and Zebdiyas, son of Esma'el, chief of the house of Yehudah, (shall be set over) all (matters) concerning the king. The scribes and the Levites shall be (as judges) before you."


[p. 697] {#po.23.p697}
መ ፡ ገጽክሙ ፡ ትጸንዑ ፡ ወትገብሩ ። ወይኩን ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ምስሌክሙ ፡ ር ።
You shall be strong and you shall be active. May the Lord be with you (and with) the good."
ቂ3, 'ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ አሞን ፣ ወደቂቀ ፣ ሞአብ ፣ ወምስሌሆ ሙ ፡ አሞናዊያን ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዮሳፍጥ ፣ ከመ ፣ ይትቃተልዎ ። ወመጽኡ ፡ ወአርአይዎ ፡ ለኢዮሳፍጥ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፣ መጽኡ ፡ ላዕሌክ ፡ ብዙኃን ፣ አማፅዶተ ፡ ባሕር ፣ ወእምሶር ያ ፤ ወአሙንቱሰ ፡ በአሳሶን ፡ ታማር ፡ እንተ ፡ ጋዲ ። ወፈርሃ ፡ ወአርትዓ ፡ ገጾ ኢ ዮሳፍጥ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኅሥሦ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወሰበከ ፡ ጾመ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ይሁዳ ። 'ወአስ ተጋብአ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ይኅሥሦ ፡ ለአግዚአብሔር ። እምኩሉ ፡ በሐውርተ ፡ ይሁዳ ፣ መጽ አ ፡ከመ፣ ይኅዓሥሥዎዖ ፡ለእግዚአብሔር። ወቆመ ፣፤ ኢዮሣፍጥ ፡ በማኅበረ ፡ እስራኤል ፣ በኢየሩሳሌም ፣ በቤተ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፣፡ ቅድመ ፡ ገጸ ፡ ዓፀድ ፣ ሐዲስ ። "ወይቤ ፡ እግዚ አብሔር ፡ አምላከ ፡ አበዊነ ፡ አኮኑ ፡ አንተ ፣ በሰማይ ፣ ወአንተ ፡ አግዚኦሙ ፣፡ ለዙሎ ሙ ፡ ነገሥተ ፡ አሕዛብ ። ውስተ ፡፣ እዴከ ፡ ኃይል ፡ ወፅንዕ ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ በቅድሜከ ፣ ዘይጸንፅዕ ። አኮኑ ፡ አንተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሠረውከ ፡ ዙሎ ፡ ዘየሐድር ውስተ ፡ዛቲ፣ ምድር ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ገጸ ፡ ሕዝብከ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ወወኀብኮ ፡ ለዘርአ ፡ አብርሃም ፡ ፍ ቁርከ ፡ ለዓለም ። ወኅደሩ ፣ ውስቴታ ፡ ወሐነፁ ፣ ዲቤሃ ፡ መቅደሰ ፡ ለስመ ፡ ዚአከ ። ወይቤሉ ፡'"እመቦ ፣ ዘመጽአ ፡ ላዕሌነ ፡ እኩይ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ሞት ፡ ወረኃብ ፡ ንቀውም ፡ቅ ድሜሁ ፡ ለዝ ፡ ቤት ፡ ወቅድሜከ ፡ እስመ ፡ ስመ ፡ ዚአከ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ለዝ ፡ ቤት ፡ ወን
XX, Then the children of Amon, the children of Mo'ab, and with them the Amonawiyan advanced against Yehoshaphat to make war on him. It was announced to Yehoshaphat and they said to him: "Many (enemies) are advancing against you from beyond the (Dead) Sea and from Sorya; they are at Asason-Tamar which is Gadi." Yehoshaphat was afraid. He composed himself to seek the Lord and he proclaimed a fast throughout all Yehudah. He gathered Yehudah to seek the Lord. From all the cities of Yehudah they came to seek the Lord. Yehoshaphat stood in the assembly of Esra'el, at Yerushalayim, in the house of the Lord, before the new court. He said: "Lord, God of our fathers, art thou not in heaven and art thou not the master * of all the kings of the peoples? In thy hand are strength and power; there is no one who (can) resist thee. Is it not thou, Lord, who didst exterminate all the inhabitants of this land before the face of thy people of Esra'el and who didst give it forever to the posterity of Abreham, thy beloved? They have dwelt there; they have built there a temple to thy name. They have said: If any calamity comes upon us: punishment, death, famine, we will stand before this house and before thee, for thy name is upon this house; we will cry out to thee in our affliction; thou (us)"


[p. 698] {#po.23.p698}
ጸርኅ ፡ ኅቤከ ፡ እምንዳቤነ ፡ ወትሰምዕ ፡ ወታድኅነነ ። "ወናሁ ፣ ይእዜ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ አሞ ን፣ወሞአብ ፡ ወደቂቀ ፡ ሴይር ፡ እለ፣ ኢአብሑ ፡ ይኅልፉ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ እንተ ፡ ኅቤ ሆሙ ፣ ወጺኦሙ ፡ እምድረ ፡ ግብጽ ፣ አስመ ፡ ሰሰሉ ፣፡ እምኔሆሙ ፣ ተኀብእዎሙ ፡ከ መ ይደምስስዎሙ ፡ '' ወናሁ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ስሑታን ፡ ወመጽኡ ፡ ያውፅኡነ ፡ አምርስትነ ፡ እንተ ፡ ወኅብከነ ። “እግዚኦ ፡ አምላክነ ፡ ኢትፈትሕኑ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ እስ መ ፡ አልብነ ፡ ኃይለ ፡ በዘንክል ፡ ቀዊመ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ዘመጽአ ፡ ላዕሌነ፣ ወኢናአምር ፡ ዘንገብሮሙ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ አፅይንቲነሰ ፡ ኀቤከ ። “ወኩሉ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ይቀው ሙ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ አግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአንስቶሙኒ ፣ ወደቂቆሙኒ ። ''ወኡዝሔል ፡ ወልደ ፡ ዘካርያስ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ልብአንያ ፣ ወልዱ ፡ ለኤሌሄል ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ለምንታንያስ ፡ ሌዋዊ ፡ እ ምደቂቀ ፡ አሳፍ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ መንፈሰ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በማዕከለ ፡ ማኅበር ። ”ወይ ቤ፣ ስምዑ ፡ ኩልክሙ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወአለ ፡ ተኀድሩ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፣ ወንጉሥ ፡ ኢዮሣፍ ጥ፣ ከመዝ ፡ ይቤ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አንትሙ ፡ ኪያሆሙ ፡ ኢትፍርሁ ፡ ወኢትደንግ ፁ ፡ እምገጸ ፡ ራዕዩ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ እስመ ፡ አኮ ፡ ዘዚአክሙ ፡ ፀብእ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ”'ጌሠመ ፡ ረዱ ፡ ላዕፅሌፖሙ ፡ ወናሁ ፡ የዐርጉ ፡ ወይወርዱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምዕራገ ፡ አስኢ ፡ ወትትራከብዎሙ ፡ ውስ” ተ ፡ ጽንፈ ፡ ተከዚ ፡ በገዳመ ፡ ኢያሪሄል ። ""አኮ ፡ አንትሙ ፡ ዘትትቃተልዎሙ ፡ አላ ፡ አእምሩ ፡ ወለብዉ ፡ መድኃኒተ ፡ እግዚአ
wilt hear and thou wilt save us. Behold now the children of Amon, (the children of) Mo'ab, and the children of Seyer, (on whose lands) thou didst not permit (text: one did not permit) (the children of) Esra'el to pass, when they came out of the land of Gebs (Egypt), for they turned away from them and disappeared before them so as not to destroy them, behold (therefore) that now they act wickedly and come to drive us out of our inheritance which thou hast given us. O Lord, our God, wilt thou not judge them? Indeed, we do not have (enough) strength to be able to resist this multitude that has come against us. We do not know what we shall do against them, but our eyes (are turned) toward thee." All Yehudah stood before the Lord with their wives and their children. (There was) Ouzhel, son of Zakaryas, descendant of Leb'anya, descendant of Elehel, descendant of Mentanyas, the Levite, of the posterity (text: of the sons) of Asaf; upon him was the spirit of the Lord in the midst of the assembly. He said: "Listen, all you of Yehudah, you who dwell in Yerushalayim, and you, King Yehoshaphat. Thus says the Lord: As for you, do not fear them and do not be terrified at the sight of this multitude, for (this) war is not yours, but the Lord's. Tomorrow descend against them; behold they will go up, (then) descend by the hill of As'i; you will meet them * at the end of the torrent in the desert of Yarihel. It is not you who"


[p. 699] {#po.23.p699}
ብሔር ፡-.እንተ ፡ ምስሌክሙ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ኢትፍርሁ ፡ ወኢትደንግፁ ፡ ጌሠመ ፣ ትወፅኡ ፣ ትትቀበልዎሙ ፡ ወእግዚአብሔር ፡ ምስሌክሙ ።፡ "ወሰገደ ፣፡ ኢዮሳ ፍጥ ፡ በገጹ ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወአለ ፡ የኅድሩ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወድቁ ፡ በገጾሙ ፡ ለእግ ዚአብሔር ፡ ወሰገዱ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ”' ወቆሙ ፡ ሌዋውያን ፡ እምደቂቀ ፡ ቀዓት ፡ወ እምደቂቀ ፡ ቆሬ ፡ ይሰብሕዎ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ አምላክ ፡ በቃል ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ ወልዑል ። ”ወንሱ ፣ በነግህ ፣ ወወፅኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳመ ፣ ቴቁሄሂ ። ወአንዘ ፣ ይወፅኡ ፣፡ ቆመ፣ዮ ሳፍጥ ፣ ወጸርሐ ፣ ወይቤ ፡ ስምዑኒ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወአለ ፡ ትነብሩኒ ፣ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ እመን ዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ አምላክክሙ ፡ ወተአምኑ ፡ እመንዎዖ ፡ ለነቢዩ ፡ ወትረትዕ ፡ ፍኖ ትክሙ ። ወተማከረ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ወዓቀመ ፡ መዘምራነ ፡ ወአለ ፡ ይሴብሑ ፡ ከ መ ፡ ያአኩቱ ፡ ወይግነዩ ፡ ለቅድሳት ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይወፅእ ፣ አንፃረ ፡ ኃይል ፡ ወይቤሉ ፡ ግነ ዩ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ እስመ ፣ ጌር ፡ እስመ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ ምሕረቱ ። ”ወእምድኅረ ፡ ዘመ ሩ ፡ ወአንበቡ ፡ ግናየ ፣ ስብሐት ፣ ወሀቦሙ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ጽንዓ ፡ ከመ ፣ ይትቃ ተሉ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ አሞን ፡ ለሰብአ ፡ ሞአብ ፡ ወለእለ ፡ ይነብሩ ፣ ውስተ ፣ ደብረ፣ ሴይ ር ፡ አለ ፡ ወፅኡ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ይሁዳ ፣ ወወድቁ ። ”ወተንሥኡ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ አሞን ፡ ወሞኦ ብ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ እለ ፡ ይነብሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ሴይር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይደምስስዎሙ ፡ ወያኅልቅ
will fight them, but know and understand that the salvation of the Lord will be with you, (with) Yehudah and (with) Yerushalayim; do not fear and do not be terrified; tomorrow you will advance to meet them and the Lord will be with you." Yehoshaphat prostrated himself face (to the ground); all Yehudah and those who dwelt in Yerushalayim fell on their faces before the Lord and worshipped the Lord. The Levites of the sons of Qa'at and of the sons of Qore stood to glorify the Lord God with a loud and high voice. They rose early in the morning and advanced toward the desert of Tegouhe. While they were advancing, Yehoshaphat stood, cried out, and said: "Listen to me, Yehudah, and you who dwell in Yerushalayim, put your trust in the Lord, your God, and your trust (will be rewarded); put your trust in his prophet and your success will be assured." He took counsel with the people. He placed (in front) the psalmists and the singers to celebrate (the Lord) and sing the sacred prayers, while advancing before the army. They said: "Give thanks to the Lord, for he is good, for his mercy is forever." After they had sung and recited the thanksgivings, the Lord incited (text: gave strength to) the children of Amon to fight against the people of Mo'ab and against the inhabitants of the mountain of Seyer who had advanced against Yehudah and (who) were defeated. (Indeed), the children of Amon and (the children of) Mo'ab rose (jointly) against the inhabitants of the mountain of Seyer to


[p. 700] {#po.23.p700}
ዎሙ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ቀተልዎሙ ፡ ወአኅለቅዎሙ ፡ ለእለ ፡ የኀድሩ ፡ ሴይር ፡ ተንሥ ኡ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይደማሰሱ ፡ ወይትኅለቁ ። ወይሁዳሰ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ አንጻረ ፣ መንገለ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ወርእዩ ፡ ዝኩ ፣ ሠራዊተ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ወናሁ፣ ዙሎሙ ፣፡ ምዉታን፣ ወውዱቃን ፡ ዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ሕያው ፡ እምኔሆ”ሙ ። ”ወበጽሐ ፡ ኢዮሳፍጥ፣ ወሕዝበ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይማሀርኩ ፡ ምሕርካ ፡ ወረከቡ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ምህርካ ፡ ወንዋየ ኒ ፡ ዘመፍትው ፡ ወማኅረኩ ፡ ኩሎ ። ወነበሩ ፡ ሠሉሰ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይመሐርኩ ፤ ወያስተጋብኡ ፡ ምሀርካሆሙ ፡ እስመ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ጥቀ ፡ እሙንቱ ። ”ወበራብዕት ፡ ዕ ለት ፡ ተጋብኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፈለገ ፣ በረከት ፡ ወአእኩትዎ ፡ ለአግዚአብሔር ፡ ወበእንተ ዝ ፡ ተሰምየ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ መካን ፡ ፈለገ ፡ በረከት ፡ እስከ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ። "ወተ መይጡ ፡ ዙሉ ፡ እደው ፡ ዘይሁዳ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፣፡ ወኢዮሳፍጥኒ ፤ ንጉሥ ፣ ምስሌሆሙ ፡፤ በሐት ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ እስመ ፡ አስተፍሥሖሙ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በጸላእቶ ሙ ።፡፡ ኖወቦኡ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ በጸናጽል ፡ ወበናብሊስ ፡ ወበቃለ ፡ ቀርን ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ፣ እግዚአብሔር ። ”ወኮነ ፡ ግርማ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ዙሉ ፡ ነገሥተ ፡ ምድር ፡ እስ መ ፡ ሰምዑ ፡ ከመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይትቃተል ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ለጸሳአተ ፡ እስራኤል ። ”ወ ኮነ ፡ ንግሠ ፡ ለኢዮሳፍጥ ፡ በሰላም ፡ ወአዕረፎ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አምላኩ ፣ እምጽነ ፊሁ ። ኝወነግሠ ፡ ኢዮሳፍጥ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ከዊኖ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ፡ ወክልዔ ፡ ዓመት 0፣ አመ ፡ ይነግሥ ፡ ወነግሠ ፣ በኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ፳ወ፳ ፡ ዓመተ ። ወስማ ፡ ለእሙ ፡ አዙባት ፡
destroy and annihilate them. After they had killed and annihilated the inhabitants of (the mountain of) Seyer, they rose one against the other to destroy and annihilate each other. (The children of) Yehudah arrived at the watchtower looking toward the desert; they saw this numerous army: behold all were dead and fallen on the ground; no one among * them was alive. Yehoshaphat and his people came to take the booty; they found abundant booty as well as precious objects and they took all these spoils. They remained three days gathering and collecting the booty, for it was extremely abundant. The fourth day they gathered near the valley of blessing and they blessed (text: thanked) the Lord; that is why this place was named valley of blessing until this day. All the men of Yehudah returned to Yerushalayim; King Yehoshaphat was with them; they were in great gladness, because the Lord had given them joy over their enemies. They entered Yerushalayim into the house of the Lord to the sound of cymbals, zithers, and trumpets. The terror of the Lord was upon all the kings of the land, because they learned that the Lord had fought against the enemies of Esra'el. The kingdom of Yehoshaphat was in peace and the Lord, his God, gave him tranquility at his borders. Yehoshaphat reigned over Yehudah. He was thirty-two years old when he became king. He reigned in Yerushalayim twenty-five years. The name of


[p. 701] {#po.23.p701}
ወለተ ፡ ሲሊ ። ”ወሖረ ፡ በፍናወ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ዓሳ ፣ ወኢተግኅሰ ፡ እምገቢረ ፡ ፍኖት ፡ርት ዕት ፡ በቅድመ ፣፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ”አላ ፣ ዓዲ ፣ ሀለዉ ፡ አውግር ፡ ወዓዲሆሙ ፡ሕዝብ፣ ኢረትዓ ፡ ልቦሙ ፡ ኅበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር፣ አምላከ ፡ አበዊሆሙ ።፡ ” ወአለ ፡ ተርፉ ፡ ቃላ ተ ፡ ዮሳፍጥ ፡ ቀደምት ፡ ወደኃርት ፡ ነዮን › ጽሑፋት ፣ በመጽሐፈ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ነቢይ ፡ ወልደ ፡ አናኒ ፣ ዘጸሐፈ ፣ በመጽሐፈ ፡ ነገሥተ ፡ አስራኤል ። ”ወእምድኅረ ፡ ዝንቱ፣ ተ ደመረ ፡ ዮሳፍጥ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አካዝያስ ፣ ንጉሠ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ወዝንቱ ፡ አበሳ ፣ ዘገብረ ። ”ወኅ” ብረ ፣ ኀቤሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይግበር ፣ አኅማረ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሖሩ ፡ ብሔ ረ ፡ ተርሴስ ፡ ወገብሩ ፡ አኅማረ ፡ በምድረ ፡ ጋስዮን ፡ጋቤር ። ”ወተነበየ ፡ ኤልዔዜር ፣ ወልደ ፣፡ አድያ ፡ ዘእምብሔረ ፡ ማርሶዕስ ፡ ላዕለ ፣ ዮሣፍጥ ፣ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ በከመ፣ ተደመርከ ፡ ወተአደውከ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አካዝያስ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ሠረወ ፣ እግዚአብ ሔር ፡ ተግባሪከ ፡ ወተቀጥቀጣ ፡ አኅማሪከ ፡ ወኢክህለ ፡ ሐዊረ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ተርሴስ ።
his mother was Azoubat, daughter of Sili. He walked in the ways of his father Asa and he did not turn away from acting according to the straight way before the Lord. However, there were still high places, and the people also did not direct (entirely) their heart (text: their heart was not straight) toward the Lord, God of his fathers. The other actions of Yehoshaphat, the first and the last, behold they are written in the book of the prophet Yasou, son of Anani, who inserted (them) into the book of the kings of Esra'el. Afterward Yehoshaphat, king of Yehudah, allied himself with Akayas, King of Esra'el, who committed sin. * He associated himself with him in order to build ships to go to the land of Tarses. They built (these) ships in the land of Gaasyon-Gaber. Elezer, son of Adya, from the land of Marsos, prophesied against Yehoshaphat, saying: "Since you have allied yourself, in transgressing (my order), with Akayas, king of Esra'el, the Lord has destroyed your work; your ships have broken and have not been able to go to the land of Tarses."
እኣ1, 'ወኖመ ፣፤ ኢዮሳፍጥ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አበዊሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ዳዊት ። ወነግ ሠ፣ ኢዮራም ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ሀየንቴሁ ። ወቦቱ ፡ አኃው ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ ኢዮሳፍጥ ፡ ፡ ወዝ ንቱ ፣ ውእቱ ፡ አስማቲሆሙ ፡ አዛርያስ ፡ ወኢይሄል ፡ ወዘካርያስ ፡ ወአዛርያስ ፡ ወ ሚሳኤል ፡ ወስፋጥያስ ፡ ወእሉ፣ ዙሎሙ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ ኢዮሳፍጥ ፣ ንጉሠ ፡ ይሁዳ ። "ወ ወሀቦሙ ፣ አቡሆሙ ፡ ጥሪተ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ወርቀ ፡ ወብሩረ ፡፣ ወንዋየ ፡ ሐቅል ፡ ምስለ፣0፣
XXI, Yehoshaphat slept with his fathers in the city of Dawit. Yoram, his son, reigned in his place. He had six brothers, sons of Yehoshaphat. Here are their names: Azaryas, Yehel, Zakaryas, Azaryas, Misa'el, and Sefatyas; all these were sons of Yehoshaphat, king of Yehudah. Their father gave them many goods: gold, silver, weapons, as well as cities


[p. 702] {#po.23.p702}
አኅጉር ፡ አለ ፣ ሕኑፃት ፡ አረፋቲሆን ፣፤ በምድረ ፣ ይሁዳ ፣ ወመንግሥተሰ ፡ ወሀቦ ፥ ለኢዮራም ፡ ወልዱ ፡ አስመ ፡፣ በኩሩ ፡ ውእቱ ። 'ወተንሥአ ፡ ኢዮራም ፡ በመንግ ሥተ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ ወጸንዓ ፣ ወቀተለ ፣ ኩሎሙ ፡፣ አኅዊሁ ፡ በሰይፍ ፡፣ ወመላእክተ ፡ እ ስራኤል ። ወከዊኖ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ፡ ወ፪ ፡ ዓመት ፡ አመ ፡ ተሠይመ ፡ ኢዮራም ፡ በመን ግሥተ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ ወቿ ፡ ዓመተ ፡ ነግሠ ፣ በኢ” የሩሳሌም ። "ወሖረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፍናወ፡ነ ገሥተ ፣ እስራኤል ፡ በከመ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ቤተ ፣ አከአብ ፡ እስመ ፡ ወለተ ፡ አከአብ ፡ ነሥ አ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ብእሲተ ፡ ወገብረ ፡ እኪተ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ። 'ወኢፈቀደ ፡ አግ ዚኣብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሠሩ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ በእንተ ፡ ኪዳን ፡ ዘተካየዶ ፡ ለዳዊት ፡ ወበ ከመ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ከመ ፡ የሀቦ ፡ ማኅቶተ ፣ ንግሥ ፡፣ ወለደቂቁ ፡ እስከ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ በኩሉ ፡ መዋዕል ። 'ወበውአቱ ፣ መዋዕል ፡ ርሕቀ ፡ ኤይም ፣ እምይሁዳ ፡ ወአንገሠ ፡፣ ላዕሌ7ፖ ሙ ፡ ንጉሠ ። ወሖረ ፡ ኢዮራም ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ምስለ ፡ ዙሉ ፡ መላእክቲሁ ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ አፍ ራሊሁ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፣ ተንሥአ ፣ በሌሊት ፣ ወቀተሎ ፡ ለኤዶም ፣ ዘዐገ ቶ፣ ወለመላእክተ ፡ ሰረገላቲሁ ። "ወተሰብረ ፡ ወጐየ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ በበ ፡ ተዐይኒሆሙ ። ወተ ግኅሠ፣ ኤዶም ፡ እምብሔረ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ እስከ ፡ ዛቲ ዕለት ። አሜሃ ፡ ተግኅሠ፣ ሎቤና፣በ ውእቱ ፡ መዋፅል ፡ እምብሔረ ፡ ዚኣሁ ፡ እስመ ፡ ረስዖ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ አምላክ ፡ አ
whose walls were (solidly) built in the land of Yehudah; but he gave the kingdom to Yoram, his son, because he was his firstborn. Yoram took possession (text: rose) of his kingdom and strengthened himself. He killed by the sword all his brothers and (some of the) princes of Esra'el. Yoram was thirty-two years old when he was put at the head of his kingdom. He reigned eight years in * Yerushalayim. He walked in the ways of the kings of Esra'el, as the house of Aka'ab had done, for he had taken for a wife a daughter of Aka'ab, and he did evil before the Lord. (But) the Lord did not want to exterminate the house of Dawit because of the covenant that he had concluded with Dawit and because he had told him that he would give the lamp of royalty to him and to his sons forever and for all time. In those days Edom separated from Yehudah and gave itself a king. King Yoram went out with all his chiefs and all his cavalry; then having risen at night, he defeated Edom who had surrounded him as well as the chiefs of the chariots. (Edom) was put to rout and the people fled each to his tent. Edom detached itself from the land of Yehudah until this day. Then Lobena detached itself at that time from the land of (Yehudah), because (Yoram) had forgotten the Lord, God


[p. 703] {#po.23.p703}
በዊሁ። '"'እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ገብረ ፡ አውግረ ፡ በአሀጉረ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወአዘመወ ፡ወአርኀቀ፣ ለአለ ፡ የሐድሩ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፣ ወለሕዝበ ፣ ይሁዳኒ ፡ ወአስሐቶሙ ። “ወበጽሐ ፡ቃለ፣ መጽሐፍ ፡ዘኀበ ፣ ኤልያስ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ይቤ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ አምላከ ፡ ዳዊ ት ፡ አቡከ ፡ ሀየንተ ፡ ዘኢሖርከ ፣ በፍናወ ፡ አቡከ ፡ ኢዮሳፍጥ ፡ ወበፍናወ ፡ አሳ ፡ ንጉ ሠ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ”ወሖርከ ፡ በፍኖተ ፡ ነገሥተ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ወአዘመውከ ፡ ሕዝበ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወሖርከ ፡ በፍኖተ ፡ ነገሥተ ፣ እስራኤል ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘመዎሙ ፡ ቤተ ፡ አከአብ፣ወ ለአኃዊከኒ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ አበከ ፡ አለ ፡ ይጌርዉ ፡ እምኔከ ፡ ቀተልኮሙ ፡ “ወናሁ ፡ አግዚኣ ብሔር ፡ ይቀትለከ ፡ በመቅሠፍት ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ በማእከለ ፡ ሕዝብከ ፡ ወበማእከለ ፡ ደቂቅከ ፡ ወበማእከለ ፡ አንስትያከ ፡ ወበማአከለ ፡ ዙሉ ፡ ጥሪትከ ። ”ወ አንተሰ ፡ በደዌ ፡መሪር፣ ወእኩይ ፡ ደዌ ፡ ክርሥ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይወፅእ ፡ አማዑቲከ ፡ አእምሕማምክ ፡ዘበበ ፡ ዕለት ። "ወ አንሥአ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ላዕለ፤ ኢዮራም ፡ ሕዝበ፣ ኢሎፍሊ ፡ ወለሰብአ ፡ አራብያ፣ወ ለአጽናፈ ፡ ኢትዮጵያ ። '"ወዐርጉ ፡ ወበጽሑ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወተሐየልዎ ፡ ወአስ
of his fathers. Indeed, he had established high places in the cities of Yehudah; he had pushed to prostitution and turned away (from the Lord) those who dwelt in Yerushalayim as well as the people of Yehudah and he had led them astray. A written message from the prophet Elyas reached (him), which said: "Thus has said the Lord, God of Dawit, thy father: Because thou hast not walked in the ways of thy father Yehoshaphat and in the ways of Asa, king of Yehudah, (but) because thou hast walked in the way of the kings of Esra'el, that thou hast pushed to prostitution the people of Yehudah, that thou hast walked in the way of the kings of Esra'el as the house of Aka'ab had pushed them to prostitution, and that thou hast assassinated thy brothers, sons of thy father, who were better than thou, behold that the Lord will make thee perish by a great plague in the midst of thy people, in the midst of thy children, in the midst of thy wives, and in the midst of all thy goods. * Thou shalt be struck by a painful and pernicious disease, a disease of the belly, such that thy bowels will come out day by day as a result of thy ailment." The Lord stirred up against Yoram the people of the Ilofeli, the people of Arabya, and those of the borders of Ityopya. They went up and arrived at the


[p. 704] {#po.23.p704}
ተጋብኡ ፡ ወነሥኡ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ጥሪተ ፡ ዘረከቡ ፡ በብሔረ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወለደቂቁኒ ፡ ወለኦ ዋልዲሁኒ ፡ ወኢተርፈ ፡ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ለደቂቁ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ አካዝያስ ፡ ዘይንዕስ ፡ እ ምደቂቁ ። ”ወእምድኅረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፣ ኩሉ ፣ ቀተሎ ፣ እግዚኣብሔር ፡፣ በዝንቱ ፡ደዌ ፣ ከርሥ ፡ ዘኢተረክበ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ፈውስ ። ”ወኮነ፣ አመ ፡ በጽሐ ፣ ሰኑይ ፣፡ መዋዕል ፡ እ ምዘ ፡ ደወየ ፡ ወፅአ ፡ አማዑቱ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ደዌሁ ፡ ወሞተ ፡ በእኪት ፡ ደዌ ። ወኢ ገብረ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ፀአተ ፡ ቀበረ ፡ ዚኣሁ ፣ በላዕሌሁ ፡ በከመ ፡ ቀበረ ፡ አበዊሁ ። ”ወከ ዊኖ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ሰላሳ ፡ ወ፪ ፡ ዓመት ፡ አመ ፡ ይነግሥ ፡ ወሰማኒተ ፡ ዓመተ ፡ ነግሠ ፡ በኢየሩሳሌም ። ወሖረ ፡ በፍኖት ፡ እንተ ፡ ኢኮነት ፡ ርትዕተ ። ወተቀብረ ፡ በሀየ ፡ በሀ ገረ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ወኢኮነሰ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መቃብረ ፡ ነገሥት ።
land of Yehudah; they prevailed over (Yehudah); they gathered and took all the goods they found in the land of Yehudah; (they took away) the sons and the daughters (of Yoram); there remained none of his children, except Akayas who was the youngest of his sons. After all these things the Lord made him perish of this disease of the belly for which there was no cure. When two years (text: days) had passed, since he was sick, his bowels came out as a result of this disease and he died of (this) evil ailment. The people did not give him a funeral similar to the funeral of his fathers. He was thirty-two years old when he became king: he reigned eight years in Yerushalayim. He walked in the way that was not straight. He was buried in the city of Dawit, but not in the sepulcher of the kings
እእ11, 'ወአንገሥዎ ፡ እለ ፡ ይነብሩ ፣ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ለአኮዝያስ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ን ኡስ እምድኅሬሁ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ አኃዊሁ ፣ መላህቅት ፣ ተቀትሉ ፡ በብሔረ ፡ በዳ፤ አመ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ አራቢያ ፡ ወአሞዛዊያን ። "ወነግሠ ፡ አካዝያስ ፣
XXII, Those who dwelt in Yerushalayim made king after him Akayas, his (youngest) son, because all his older brothers had been killed in the desert, when the people of Arabiya and the Amozawiyan came against (Yoram). Akayas, son of Yoram, king of Yehudah, became king, when he


[p. 705] {#po.23.p705}
ወልደ ፡ ኢዮራም ፣ ንጉሠ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡-ከዊኖ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ፳ ፡ ዓመት፣ ወነግሠ ፡ አካዝያስ ፡ ወአሐተ ፡ ዓመተ ፡ ነግሠ ፡ በኢየሩሳሌም ። ወስማ ፡ ለእሙ ፡ ጎቶልያ ፡ ወለተ ፡ ዘምሪ ። ወዝንቱ ፡ ሖረ ፡ በፍኖተ ፡ አከአብ ፣ እስመ ›፣ አሙ ፡ መምክረ ፡ ኮነት ፡ ከመ ፣ ይኅጥ ኡ ። "ወገብረ ፡ እኪተ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ በከመ ፡ ቤተ ፡ አከአብ ። እስመ ፡ አሙንቱ ፡ ኮንዎ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ሞተ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ሐጻንያተ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሰርውዎ ፡ "ወበምክ ረ ፡ ዚአሆሙ ፡ ሖረ ። ወሖረ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ኢዮራም ፡ ወልድ ፡ አከአብ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፀብእ፣ ላዕለ ፡ አዛሄል ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ሶርያ ፣ በብሔረ ፣ ራሞት ፣፡ዝገለኣድ ። ወነደፍዎ ፡ ለኢዮራም ፡ ወአቅ-ሰልዎ ። ወተመይጠ ፡ ወገብአ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ኢይዝራኤል ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትፈወስ ፣፡ እም ቅስል ፡ ዘአቀሰልዎ ፡ ሰብአ ፣ ሶርያ ፣ በብሔረ ፡ ራሞት ፡፣ እንዘ ፡ ይትቃተሎ፣ ለአዛሄል ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ሶርያ ። ወኢኮዝያስ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ኢዮራም ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወረደ፣ ከመ ፡ የሐውጾ ፡ ለኢዮራም ፡ ወልዶ ፡ አከአብ ፣ በኢይዝራኤል ፡ እስመ ፡ ይደዊ ። " ወእምኅበ ፣ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ኮነት ፡ ፅልወተ ፡ ኢኮዝያስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምጻእ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ኢ ዮራም ። ወሶበ ፣ መጽአ ፡፣ ወፅአ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ኢዮራም ፡ ኅበ ፣ ኢያኡ ፡ ወልደ ፡ናእስ ፡ መሲሑ ፡ ለእግዚኣብሔር ፡ "ወኮነ ፡ በከመ ፡ ተበቀሎ ፡ ኢያኡ ፡ ለቤተ ፡ አከአብ ፡ ወ
was twenty years old. (Thus) reigned Akayas. He reigned one year in Yerushalayim. The name of his mother was Gotolya (Athaliah), daughter of Zamri. This (king) walked in the way of Aka'ab, for his mother was his counselor for sin. He did evil before the Lord like the house of Aka'ab. Indeed, after the death of his father (men of the house of Aka'ab) became his counselors for his perdition, and he walked according to their advice. He went with Yoram, son of Aka'ab, to make war on Azahael, king of Sorya, in the land of Ramot of Gala'ad. (Archers) struck Yoram and wounded him. (Yoram) returned and went back to the land of Yezra'el to treat himself for the wound that the men * of Sorya had made to him in the land of Ramot, while he was fighting against Azahael, king of Sorya. Ikozyas, son of Yoram, king of Yehudah, went down to visit Yoram, son of Aka'ab, in Yezra'el, because he was sick. It was the loss of Ikozyas (willed) by the Lord to go toward Yoram. When he had come, he walked with Yoram against Ya'ou, son of Na'es, the anointed of the Lord. (But) Ya'ou, avenging himself on the house of Aka'ab, met the princes of Yehudah and the (sons of the) brothers of Ikozyas


[p. 706] {#po.23.p706}
ረከበ ፡ መላእክተ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወአኃዊሁ ፡ ለኢኮዝያስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትለአክዎ ፡ ንጉሠ ፥ ይሁዳ ፡፣ ቀተሎሙ ። ወይቤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኅሥሦ ፡ ለኢኮዝያስ ፡ ወዴገኖ ፡ ወረከቦ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትፌወስ ፤ በብሔረ ፡ ሰማርያ ፡ ወአምጽአዎዖ ፡ ኅበ ፣ ኢያኡ ፡ ወቀተሎ ። ወቀበር ፡ እስ መ ፡ ይቤሉ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ኢዮሳፍጥ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ዘኀሠሦ ፣፡ ለእግዚኣብሔር ፡በኩሉ ፡ ልቡ ። ወአልቦ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ቤቱ ፡ ለኢኮዝያስ ፡ ዘይጸናዕ ፡ በኀይል ፡ በውስተ ፣ መንግሥት ። "ወጎቶልያ ፤ እሙ ፡፤ ለኢኮዝያስ ፡ ርእየት ፡ ከመ ፡ ሞተ ፡ እጓላ ፣ ወተንሥአት ፡ ወአሕ ጐለት ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘርአ ፡ መንግሥት ፡ ዘእምቤተ ፡ ይሁዳ ።፡ ''ወነሥአቶ ፡ ዮሳቤት ፡ ወለተ፣ ንጉሥ ፡ ለኢዮአስ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ኢኮዝያስ ፡ ወሰረቀቶ ፡ ወነሥአቶ ፡ ሠሪቃ ፡ አማአከለ ፡ ደቂ ቀ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ አለ ፡ ተቀትሉ ፡ ወአንበረቶ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወለኃፃኒቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውሳጢ ፡ ኀ በ፣ምስካቡ ። ወኀብአቶ ፡ ዮሳቤት ፡ ወለተ ፣ ንጉሥ ፡ ለኢዮአስ ፡ እኅቱ ፡ ለኢኮዝያስ፣፤ ብአሲቱ ፡ ለኢዮአዳ ፡ ካህን ። ወኀብአቶ ፡ እምገጻ ፡ ለጎቶልያ ፡ ወኢቀተለቶ ። "”'ወነበ ረ ፡ ምስሌሃ ፡ ኅቡአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ፡ ክረምተ ፡ ወጎቶልያ ፡ ነግ
who served the king of Yehudah, (and) killed them. He ordered to search for Ikozyas; they set out to search for him and they found him while he was treating himself in the land of Samarya; they brought him to Ya'ou, who killed him. They buried him, saying: "It is the son of Yehoshaphat, who sought the Lord with all his heart." There was no one in the house of Ikozyas who was strong enough to (assume) the royalty. Gotolya (Athaliah), mother of Ikozyas, seeing that her son was dead, rose and made perish all the royal lineage of the house of Yehudah. Yosabeet, daughter of the king, took Lyo'as, son of Ikozyas. She stole him and took him furtively from the midst of the children of the king who were killed; she put him with his nurse in a secluded bedroom. Yosabet, daughter of the king, (who) had hidden Lyo'as, was the sister of Ikozyas (and) the wife of the priest Lyo'ada. She hid him from the eyes of Gotolya, (who) could not kill him. He remained hidden with her in the house of the Lord during the six years that Gotolya reigned over the land.
ሠት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድር። እ.እ1]1, 'ወበሳብዕ ፡ ዓመት ፡ ጸንዐ ፡ ኢዮአዳ ፣ ወነሥአ ፡ መላእክተ ፡፻ ፡ ለአዛር ያ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ኢዮራም ፡ ወእስማዔል ፡ ወልደ ፡ ኢዮአናን ፡ ወአዛርያ ፡፣ ወልደ ፡ ዔቤድ ፣
XXIII, The seventh year Lyo'ada decided; he took the chiefs of hundreds: Azarya son of Lyoram, Esma'el son of Lyo'anan, Azarya son of Ebed,


[p. 707] {#po.23.p707}
ወማትያ ፡ ወልደ ፡ አዳእያ ፣ ወኤልሳፍጥ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ዘካርያስ ፣ ም”ስሌሁ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ቤተ ፣ እግዚኣብሔር ። ወዐገትዎ ፡ ለይሁዳ ፣ ወአስተጋብኡ ፡ ሌዋዊያን ፣ እምኩ ሉ ፡ በሓውርተ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ መላእክተ ፡ አበወ ፡ ዘመዶሙ ፡ ለእስራኤል ፡ ወመጽኡ ፡ ው ስተ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ። 'ወሠርዑ ፡ ሥርዓተ ፡ ዙሉ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ በማኅበሮሙ ፡ በቤተ ፡ እግ ዚኣብሔር ፡ ምስለ ፡ ንጉሥ ። ወአርአዮሙ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፣፡ናሁ ፡ወ ልደ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ይነግሥ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ ዲበ ፡፣ ቤተ ፣ ዳዊት ። 'ወና ሁ ፡ ዝንቱ፡ ነገር ፡ ዘትገብሩ ፡ ሣልስተ ፡ አዴክሙ ፡ይሖር ፡ በሰንበት ፡ ክህናት ፡ ወሌዋዊ ያን ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዥሉ ፡ አናቅጺሃ ፣ ለምብዋኣቲክሙ ፡'"ወሣልስተ ፡ እዴክሙ ፡ በቤተ ፣ ንጉሥ ፡ ወሣልስተ ፡-እዴክሙ ፡በአናቅጽ ፡ ማእከላዊ ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ በዐጸደ ፡ ቤተ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ። "ወኢይባእ ፡ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ እግዚኣብሔር ፡ እመ ፡ ኢኮኑ ፡ክህናተ፣ወ ሌዋዊያነ ፡ ወአለ ፣፡ ይትለአክዎሙ ፡፣ ለሌዋዊያን ፡ ውእቶሙ ፡ ይበውኡ ፡ አስመ ፡ ቅዱሳ ን ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ የዐቅቡ ፡ ቤተ ፣ አግዚኣብሔር ። 'ወዓገትዎ ፡ ሌዋው ያን ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ በድኅረ ፡ አውዱ ። ወይቤ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ወብእሲ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ሐቅሉ ፡ ይጹ ር፣0ውስተ ፡ እደዊሁ ፡ ወዘገብአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤቱ ፡ ይመውት ። ወየሀልዉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ንጉ
Matya son of Ada'eya, and Elasfet son of Zakaryas with him in the house of the Lord. * They traveled through (the land of) Yehudah, gathered the Levites from all the cities of Yehudah (as well as) the heads of families of Esra'el (who) came to Yerushalayim. All Yehudah made an agreement with the king in an assembly (held) in the house of the Lord. (Lyo'ada) showed them the son of the king and said to them: "Here is the son of the king; (it is he who must) reign, as the Lord has said, over the house of Dawit. Here are the things that you will do. Let a third of you, priests and Levites, enter (the day of) the Sabbath (and guard) all the doors of your entrances; let a third (stand) in the house of the king; let a third (guard) the middle door and let all the people (stand) in the court of the house of the Lord. Let (no one) enter the house of the Lord, if it is not the priests, the Levites, and those who serve the Levites. These latter will enter, because they are consecrated and all the people will guard the house of the Lord. The Levites will surround the king, making a circle around him." He said: "Let each man carry his weapons in his hand. Whoever shall penetrate into the temple shall be put to death. Let (these armed men) be with the king, when he enters and when he goes out."


[p. 708] {#po.23.p708}
ሥ ፡ እንዘ ፡ የሐውር ፡ ወእንዘ ፡ ይወፅእ ። ወገብሩ ፡ ሌዋዊያን ፡ ወሉ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ በከ መ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ኢዮአዳ ፡ ካህን ። ወነሥአ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ በበ ፡ ፡ ፅደወ ፣ እምርአሰ ፡ ሰንበት ፡ እስከ ፡ ተፍጻሜተ ፡ ሰንበት ፡ አስመ ፡ ኢሰዐረ ፣ ኢዮአዳ ፡ ካህን ፣ በእብሬተ ፡ መዋዕሊ ሁ ።ወወሀበ ፡ አስይፍተ ፡ ወወላትወ ፡ ወኩያንወ ፡ እለ ፣ ሀለዉ ፡ ለዳዊት ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤቱ ። ”ወአቀመ ፡ ኩሎ ፡፣ ሕዝበ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ንዋየ ፣ ኀቅሉ፣ አምአውድ ፡ ቤት ፡ ዘበየማን ፡ እስከ ፣ አውደ ፡ ቤት ፡ ዘበፀጋም ፣ ቤተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወቤተ ፡ ንጉሥ ። ''አውፅኦ ፡ ለወልደ ፣ ንጉሥ ፡ ወሜመ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ አክሊለ ፣ መንግ ሥት ፡ ወስምዕ ። ወአንገሥዎ ፡ ወቀብአዎ ፡ ኢዮአዳ ፡ ወደቂቁ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ሕየው ፡ አበ ፡ ነጋሚ ። ”“ወሰምዓት ፡ ጎቶልያ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ አለ ፡ ይረውጹ ፡ ወይገንዩ ፡ አለ ፣ ይዌድስዎ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ወቦአት ፡ ቤተ ፡ አግዚአብሔር ፡ ኅበ ፡ ንጉሥ ። ”ወርእየቶ ፡ ለንጉ ሥ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምቅዋሙ ፡ ይቀውም ፡ ወበምብዋኢሁ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ወአ ቅርንት ፣፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ይትፌሥሑ ፡ ወይነፍሔሑ ፡ አቅርንተ ፡ ወአለ ፡ ይዜምሩ ፡ በ ፅንዚራት ፡ ወይሴብሑ ፡ ወይዜምሩ ፡ ውዳሴ ። ወሰጠጠት ፡ ጎቶልያ ፡ ዓጽፋ ፡ ወ” አው
The Levites and all Yehudah did as the priest Lyo'ada had ordered. Each kept with him his men both (those) of the beginning of the week and (those) of the end of the week, for the priest Lyo'ada had not dismissed (them) at (their) turn and at their day. He gave (them) the swords, the shields, and the spears that had belonged to King Dawit (and which were) in the house (of the Lord). He placed all the people, each (man) with his weapons, from the right side of the temple to the left side, (near the altar of) the house of the Lord and (near) the house of the king. He made the son of the king advance, put upon (his head) the royal crown and (in his hand the book of) the testimony. Lyo'ada and his sons proclaimed him king, anointed him, and said to him: "Long live the king!" Gotolya heard the voice of the people who were running, giving thanks, and cheering the king. She entered the house of the Lord (going) toward the king. She saw the king standing on his platform; at the entrance were the princes and the trumpets; all the people were rejoicing; they were sounding trumpets; the singers (to the sound of) instruments were celebrating (the king) and singing praises. Gotolya tore her mantle, * cried out and said


[p. 709] {#po.23.p709}
የወት ፡ ወትቤ ፡ ማዕሌት ፡ ማዕሌት ። "ወወፅአ ፣ ኢዮአዳ ፡ካህን ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፣ለመላ አክተ ፡ ፻ ፣ ወለመላእክተ ፣ ኃይል ፣ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ አውፅአዋ ፡ አፍአ ፣ እምቤተ ፡ እግ ዚአብሔር ፡ ወዘተለዋ ፡ ድኅሬሃ ፡ ይሙት ፡ በመጥባሕት ። እስመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ክህን ፡ ኢት ሙት ፡ በቤተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ”ወወፅአት ፡ እንተ ፡ ምርዋጸ ፡ አፍራስ ፡ ዘቤተ ፡ ንጉ ሥ፣ ወቀተልዋ ፡ በህየ ። "ወሠርዓ ፣ ኢዮአዳ ፣ ሥርዓተ ፡ ማአከሌሁ ፡ ወማእከለ ፡ ሕዝ ብ ፡ ወንጉሥ ፡ ወሕዝብኒ ፡ ይፍርሀዎ ፡ ለአግዚአብሔር ። '' ወቦአ ፡ ኩሉ ፡፣ ሕዝበ፣ም ድር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ በዐል ፡ ወደምሰስዎ ፡ ለምሥዋዒሁ ፡ ወለኩሉ ፡ አማልክተ ፡ግ ብት ፡ ሐረጽዎሙ ፡ ወለማታን ፡ ካህነ ፡ በዐል ፡ ቀተልዎ ፡ በምሥዋዕ ። "ወአስተአኅ ዘ፣፤ ኢዮዓዳ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ቤተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በእደ ፡ ካህናት ፡ ወሌዋውያን ፡ እ ለ ፡ በአስተባርዮ ፡ ዘሠርዓ ፡ ዳዊት ። ወሠርዖሙ ፡ በበ ፡ ጾታሆሙ ፡ ወአዕረገ ፡ መሥዋ ፅተ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ በከመ ፡ ጽሑፍ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኦሪተ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ በፍሥሓ ፡ ወበስብሐ ት ፡ በእደ ፡ ዳዊት ። “ወጫመ ፡ ዓፀውተ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ኀዋኅወ ፡ ቤተ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይባእ ፡ ርኩስ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዙሉ ፡ ግብር ። ”ወነሥአ ፡ አበወ ፡ ዘመዶሙ ፡ ወኀየልተ ፤ ወመላእክተ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ወኩሎ ፡ አሕዛበ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወአውረድዎ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ አምቤተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአንበርዎ ፡ ዲበ ፡ መንበረ ፡ መንግሥት ። ” ወተፈሥሑ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ አሕዛ
"Treason, treason!" The priest Lyo'ada advanced, addressed the chiefs of hundreds and the chiefs of the army and said to them: "Drag her out of the house of the Lord. Let whoever follows her die by the sword." The priest, in fact, had said: "Let her not die in the house of the Lord." She went out, crossing the track of the horses of the house of the king; they killed her there. Lyo'ada made a pact between him, the people, and the king, so that the people would fear the Lord. All the people of the land entered the house of Ba'al, destroyed his altar, broke all the new gods, and killed Matan, priest of Ba'al, at the altar. Lyo'ada committed all the service of the house of the Lord to the hands of the priests and the Levites according to the distribution (of functions) that Dawit had established. He distributed them each to his rank to offer sacrifices to the Lord, as it is written in the law of Mouse, with rejoicings and canticles according to the ordinance of Dawit. He put porters at the doors of the house of the Lord, so that no (man) impure in any way should enter. He took the heads of families, the powerful (men), the princes of the people, and all the populations of the land who brought the king down from the house of the Lord and seated him on the royal throne. All the peoples of the land rejoiced. The land was tranquil, (after) they had killed Gotolya by the sword.
በ፣ምድር ፡ ወብሔር ፡ ኀድአ ፡ ወለጎቶልያ ፡ ቀተልዋ ፡ በመጥባሕት ። እኣ፤', ወከዊኖ ፣ ኢዮአስ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ፡ ዓመት ፡ አመ ፡ ይነግሥ ፡ ወነግሠ ፡ ኢ
XXIV, Lyo'as was seven years old, when he became king. Lyo'as reigned


[p. 710] {#po.23.p710}
ዮአስ ፡ አርብዓ ፡ ዓመተ ፡ በኢየሩሳሌም ። ወስማ ፡ ለእሙ ፡ ዐብያ ፡ ዘእምብሔረ ፡ ባር ስባሔ ። "ወገብረ ፤ ኢዮአስ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ አግዚአብሔር ፡ በኩሉ ፡ መዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለኢዮአዳ ፡ ካሀን ። "ወነሥአ ፡ ሎቱ ፣ ኢዮአዳ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ክልኡ ፡ አንስተ ፡ ወወለደ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ ወአዋልደ ። ወኮነ፣ እምድኅረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዐርገ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኅሊናሁ ፡ ለኢዮአስ ፡ ከመ ፡ የሐድስ ፡ ቤተ ፡ አግዚአብሔር ። "ወአስተጋብአ ፡ ካህናተ ፡ ወሌዋዊያነ ፡ ወይቤ ሎሙ ፡ ሔሩ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ኩሉ ፣ አኅጉረ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወአስተጋብኡ ፣ እምኩሉ ፣ በሓውር ተ ፡ አሕዛበ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ወርቀ ፡ ወብሩረ ፡ ዘየአክሎ ፡ ለቤተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለለዓ መት ። ወኢያፍጠኑ ፡ ሌዋውያን ። ወጸውዖ ፣ ኢዮአስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ለኢዮአዳ ፡ መልአ ክ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ለምንት ፡ ኢሐወፅከ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሌዋውያን ፡ ከመ ፡ ያብኡ ፡ እምይሁዳ ፡ወ አምኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ዘሥሩዕ ፡ እምኅበ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወእምኅበ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ ቀሩልዔሁ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ አመ ፡ አስተጋብአ ፡ ዙሎ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ውስተ ፡ መርጡል። 'እስመ፣ ጎቶልያ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ኃጥአት ፡ ወደቂቃኒ ፣ አመዝበሩ ፣ ቤተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወንዋየ ፡ ቅ ድሳት ፡ ዘቤተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ገብሩ ፡ ለበአሊም ። "ወይቤ ፡ ንጉሥ ፣ ይግበሩ ፡ ሙዳ የ፣ምጽዋት ፣ ወያንብሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አንቀጸ ፤ ቤተ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አንተ ፣ አፍአ ። "ወይሰብኩ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዙሉ ፣ አኅጉረ ፣ ይሁዳ ፣ ወኢየሩሳሌም ፣ ወያብኡ ፡ ለእ ግዚአብሔር ፡ በከመ ፣ ይቤ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ ሞልዔሁ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ላዕለ ፡ እስራኤል ፥
forty years in Yerushalayim. The name of his mother was Abya, from the land of Barsebahe. Lyo'as did good before the Lord during all the days of the priest Lyo'ada. The prince (of the priests) Lyo'ada took for (Lyo'as) two wives from whom he fathered sons and daughters. Afterward it came (text: it went up) to the mind of Lyo'as * to restore the house of the Lord. He gathered the priests and the Levites and said to them: "Go into all the cities of Yehudah and collect each year from all the lands of the people of Esra'el the gold and the silver necessary for (the repair of) the house of the Lord." The Levites did not hasten. King Lyo'as called the prince (of the priests) Lyo'ad and said to him: "Why have you not watched over the Levites, so that they bring from Yehudah and from Yerushalayim what has been prescribed by the Lord and by Mouse, the servant of the Lord, when he assembled all Esra'el at (the tent of) the testimony? Indeed, the sinful Gotolya and her sons have ravaged the House of the Lord and have destined the sacred objects of the house of the Lord (to the service) of the Ba'alim." The king prescribed to make a chest for alms and to put (it) at the door of the house of the Lord on the outside. They published in all the cities of Yehudah and in Yerushalayim that (everyone had to) bring offerings to the Lord, as Mouse, the servant of the Lord, had prescribed to Esra'el in the desert. All the chiefs and all the people
በገዳም ። "'ወወኅቡ ፡ ዙሉ ፡ መኳንንት ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ወአብኡ ፡መባአ ፡ ወወደዩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሙዳየ ፡ ምጽዋት ፡ እስከ ፡ መልአት ። ''ወኮነ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ያበውኡ ፡ ሙዳየ ፡ ምጽ ዋት ፡ ኅበ ፣ መገብተ ፣፡ ንጉሥ ፡ በአዴሆሙ ፡ ለሌዋዊያን ፣ ወርአዮሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ በገኃ ፡ ብሩር ፡ መጽአ ፣ ጸሓፊሁ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፣፡ ወመጋቧሁ ፡ ለካህን ፣፡ ዐቢይ ፡ ወገልገልዋ ፡ ለ ሙዳየ ፡ ምጽዋት ፣ ወአግብእዋ ፤ ውስተ ፡ መካና ። ወከመዝ ፡ ይገብሩ ፡ ለለ ፡ ዕለት፣ ወአስተጋብኡ ፡ ብሩረ ፡ ብዙኃ ። ”ወወሀበ ፡ ንጉሥ ፣፡ ወኢዮአዳ ፡ ካሀን ፡ ለአለ ፡ ይገ ብሩ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ቤተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአምኔሁ ፡ ያጸአጽኡ ፡ ለነደቅት ፡ ወለጸረብት ፡ እ ስመ ፡ ይገብሩ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ቤተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ “ወአቅነዩ ፡ አለ ፡ ይገብሩ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ቤ ተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወተፈጸመ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ግብር ፡ በእደዊሆሙ ፡ ወአቀምዋ ፡ ለቤተ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በዲበ መሠረቱ ፡ በጽኑዕ ። ''ወፈጺሞሙ ፡ አምጽኡ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ንኘጉሥ፣ ወኀበ ፣ ኢዮአዳ ፡ ካህን ፡ ዘተርፈ ፡ ብሩረ ። ወገብርዎ ፣ ንዋያተ ፡ ለቤተ ፣ እግዚአብ ሔር ፡ ወንዋያተ ፡ ጽንሓሓት ፡ ወጽዋዓተ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ወብሩር ። ወአዕረጉ ፡ ጽንሃሃተ ፡ ለቤተ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለኢዮአዳ ። “ወረስዓ ፡ ኢዮአ ዳ ፡ ፈጺሞ ፡ መዋዕሊሁ ፡ ወሞተ ፡ ከዊኖ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ምአት ፡ ወሰላሳ ፡ ዓመት ፡ አመ ፣ ይመውት ጦ። “ወቀበርዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ምስለ ፡ ነገሥት ፡ አስመ ፡ገብረ ፡ ሠና የምስለ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ በቅድመ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ።፡ "ወኮነ ፡ እምድኅረ ፥ ሞተ ፡ ኢዮ አዳ ፡ ቦኡ ፡ መላእክተ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወሰገዳ- ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፣ ወአሜሃ ፡ ሰምዖሙ ፡ ንጉ
gave, brought, and deposited (their) offerings in the chest of alms until it was filled. When they brought the chest of alms to the stewards of the king by the hand of the Levites and they saw that the money was abundant, the secretary of the king and the steward of the high priest came to empty the chest of alms and carried it back to its place. They did thus each day. They collected much money. The king and the priest Lyo'ada gave (it) to those who did the work of the house of the Lord; with this (money) they paid the masons and the carpenters, for they were doing the work (of repair) of the house of the Lord. Those who were doing the work (of repair) of the house of the Lord worked (with diligence); all the work was finished by their hands; they re-established solidly the house of the Lord on its foundations. When they had finished, they brought to the * king and to the priest Lyo'ada the money that remained. They made of it vases for the house of the Lord, utensils for the holocausts, and cups of gold and silver. They offered continually holocausts in the house of the Lord during the days of Lyo'ada. Lyo'ada grew old; having finished his days, he died. He was one hundred and thirty years old, when he died. They buried him in the city of Dawit with the kings, because he had done good toward Esra'el before the Lord. After the death of Lyo'ada the princes of Yehudah came to prostrate themselves before the king; then the king listened to them.


[p. 711] {#po.23.p711}


[p. 712] {#po.23.p712}
ሥ ። “ወኅደግዎ ፡ ለአግዚአብሔር ፡ አምላከ ፡ አቡሆሙ ፡ ወተለዉ ፡ አማልክተ ፡ ግብ ት ። ወኮነ ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ መቅሠፍት ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወኢየሩሳሌም ፡ በውእቱ ፡ መዋዕል ። “ወፈነወ ፡ ኀቤሆሙ ፡ ነቢያተ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትመየጡ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአስምዑ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ወኢሰምፁ ። ”ወመንፈሰ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለብሰ ፡ አዛርያ ፡ ወልደ ፤፡ ኢዮአ ዳ ፡ ካህን ፡፣ ወተንሥአ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ አሕዛብ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ይቤ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለምንት ፡ ኢተሐውሩ ፡ በትእዛዘ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፤ ወኢትሜርሁ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኀደግምዎ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ አምላክክሙ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡የኀድገክሙ ።፡ ወሮጹ ፡ዲቤሁ ፡ወአኀዙ፣ ይውሣግርዎ ፡ በትእዛዘ ፡ ኢዮአስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ በዓፀደ ፣ ቤተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ”ወኢተዘ ከረ ፡ ኢዮአስ ፡ ሣሀለ ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ኢዮአዳ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ወቀተሎ ፡ ለወልዱ፣ አዛርያስ ። እንዘ ፡ ይመውት ፡ ይቤ ፡ ናሁ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይሬኢ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ይኩን ን ። ”ወኮነ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ፈጸመ ፡ ዓመተ ፡ ዐርገ ፡ በላዕሌሁ ፡ ኃይለ ፤ ሠራዊተ ፡ ሶር ያ ፤ ወበጽሐ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ በሓውርተ ፣ ይሁዳ ፣ ወኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወሠረወ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ መላ እክተ ፡ እለ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ወኩሎ ፣ ምሕርክና ፡ ዚ አሆሙ ፡ ነሥአ ፣ ወፈነወ ፣ ለንጉሠ ፡ ደማስቆ ። ”እስመ ፡ ውጉዳን ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ሠራዊተ ፡ ሶርያ ፣ ወእግ ዚአብሔር ፡ መጠዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እደዊሆሙ ፡ ለሠራዊት ፡ ጽኑዓን ፣ ወብዙኃን ፡ ፈድ ፋደ ፡ እስመ ፡ ረስዕዎ ፡ ለአግዚአብሔር ፡ አምላከ ፡ አበዊሆሙ ። ወምስለ ፡ ኢዮአስ ፡
They abandoned the Lord, God of their fathers, and followed the new gods. There was a great punishment upon Yehudah and upon Yerushalayim in those days. The Lord sent them prophets, so that they would convert toward him; (these) protested before them, (but) they did not listen. Azarya, son of the priest Lyo'ada, was filled (text: was clothed) with the spirit of the Lord. He stood before the people and said to them: "Thus has said the Lord: Why do you not walk in the commandments of the Lord? You will not prosper, for you have abandoned the Lord, your God, and he himself will abandon you." They rushed upon him and began to stone him by order of King Lyo'as in the court of the house of the Lord. Lyo'as did not remember the kindness that Lyo'ada, his father, had had for him and he killed Azaryas, his son. (The latter) said, while dying: "Behold that the Lord sees; may he himself do justice." After the year had passed, the army of Sorya went up against him, arrived at the land of Yehudah and at Yerushalayim, exterminated all the chiefs who were among the people, took all * their spoils, and sent (them) to the king of Damaqso. Although the troops of Sorya had come few in number, the Lord delivered into their hands troops extremely strong and numerous, because (the children of Yehudah) had forgotten the Lord, God of their fathers. (The Sorawiyan) did

ግብሩ ፡-ፍትሐ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ሖሩ ፡፤ እምኔሁ ፣ ኀዲጎሙ ፡ ኪያሁ ፡ በደዌ ፡ ዐቢ ይ ። ወተበቀልዎ ፡ አግብርተ ፡ ዚአሁ በእንተ ፡ ደመ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ኢዮአዳ ፡ክሀን ፡ ወቀተ ልዎ ፡ በምስካበ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ ወሞተ ። ወቀበርዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ወኢደመርዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መቃብረ ነገሥት ። ”ወአለሰ ፡ ቀተልዎ ፡ ዘቤድ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ሰማት ፣ አሞናዊት፣፥ ወኢዮዛበድ ፡፣ ወልደ ፡ ሰማሪት ፣ ሞአባዊት ። "ወደቂቁ ፣ ኩሎሙ ፡፣ ቀበርዎ ፡ ወላሐ ውዎ ። ወአለሂ ፡ ተርፋ ፡ ቃላጊቲሁ ፡ ናሁ ፣ ጽሑፋት ፡ ውስተ ፣ መጽሐፈ ፡ ነገሥት ።
justice on Lyo'as. Afterward they moved away from him, leaving him (stricken) with a grave disease. His servants punished him for the blood of the son of the priest Lyo'ada; they killed him in his bed and he died. They buried him in the city of Dawit, (but) they did not put him in the sepulcher of the kings. Those who killed him were Zabed, son of Samat Amonawit, and Lyozabad, son of Samarit Mo'abawit. All his sons buried him and mourned him. Behold that his other actions are written in the book of the Kings. Amasyas, his son, reigned after him.
ወነግሠ ፣ አማስያስ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ እምድኅሬሁ ። ኣዲኒነ', 'ከዊኖ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ዕሥራ ፡ ወኃምስቱ ፡ ዓመት ፡ አመ ፡ ይነግሥ ፡ አማስያ ስ ፡ ፅሥራ ፡ ወተስዓተ ፣ ዓመተ ፡ ነግሠ ፡ በኢየሩሳሌም ። ስማ ፡ ለእሙ ፡ ኢያኤዬዳ ን ፡ አምሀገረ ፣ ኢየሩሳሌም ። ወገብረ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ በቅድመ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአኮሰ ፡ በምልአት ፡ ወፍጽምት ፡ ልብ ። "ወኮነ ፣ እምድኅረ ፣፤ ተጠኣጥአ ፣ ወጸንዓ ፡ መንግሥ ት ፡ በአደ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ ወቀተሎሙ ፡ ለአግብርቲሁ ፡ አለ ፡ ቀተልዎ ፡ ለአቡሁ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወለደቂቆሙሰ ፣፡ ኢቀተለ ፡ በከመ ፡ ኪዳነ ፡ ሕገ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በከመ ፡ ጽሑፍ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዘ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፣ ኢይመውቱ ፡ አበው ፡ ሀየንተ ፡ ደቂ ቆሙ ፣ ወኢይመውቱ ፡ ደቂቅ ፡ ሀየንተ ፣ አበዊሆሙ ፡ አላ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ በከ መ፡ ፉራ ፡ ወበአበሳ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ ይመውት ። "ወአስተጋብአ ፡ አማስያስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ሠ ራዊተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ይሁዳ ፣፤ ወአቀሞሙ ፡በበ ፡ አብያተ ፡ አበዊሆሙ ፡ ወሜመ ፡ እምኔሆ
XXV, Amasyas was twenty-five years old, when he became king. He reigned twenty-nine years in Yerushalayim. The name of his mother was Lya'eyedan from the city of Yerushalayim. He did good before the Lord, but not with a whole and perfect heart. After the kingdom was well established and strengthened in his hand, he killed the servants who had assassinated the king, his father; but he did not kill their sons according to the pact of the law of the Lord, according to what is written (and) as the Lord has ordered, saying: "The fathers shall not die in the place of their sons; the sons shall not die in the place of their fathers; but each shall die for his fault and for his own sin." King Amasyas gathered the troops of the house of Yehudah and organized them by families. He


[p. 714] {#po.23.p714}
ሙ ፣ ርኡሳነ ፡ እልፍ ፡ ወምአት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ በሐውርተ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወኢየሩሳ ሌም ፡ ወጐልቆሙ ፡ አምጣነ ፡ ፅሥራ ፡፣ ዓመት ፡ ወለፌ ፡ ወረከቦሙ ፣ በጉልቀ፦ ፥ ሠላሳ ፡ አልፍ ፡ ጸናዕት ፡ ወአለ ፡ ይክሉ ፣ ፀቢአ ፡ ወይጸውሩ ፡ ሐገፋ ፡ ወረምሐ ። "ወ ተንስበ ፣ እምውስተ ፣ እስራኤል ፡ ዓሠርተ ፡ ምእተ ፡ ጸናዕተ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወዓስቦሙ ፣ ፻ ፣ መክሊተ ፡ ብሩር ። "ወመጽአ ፡ ነቢየ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሎ ፡ ን ጉሥ ፣ ኢይፃእ ፡ ምስሌከ ፡ ሠራዊተ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ እስመ ፡ ኢኮነ ፡፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ምስለ ፡ አስራኤል ፣ ወኩሎሙ ፡ ደቂቀ ፤ ኤፍሬም ። "ለእመ ፡፣ ነሣእከ ፡ ወሐለይከ ፡ ቦ ፣፡ እምኔሆሙ ፣፡መዊአ ፡ ወእግዚአብሔር ፡ ያወድቀከ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ጸላእትከ ፡ እስመ ፡ ብዉሕ ፡ በኀበ ፡ አግዚአብሔር ፡ አጽንዖ ፣ ወአውድቆ ። "ወይቤሎ ፣ አማስያ ፡ ለነቢ የ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወምንተ ፡ እንከ ፡ እገብር ፡ በእንተ ፣ መክሊት ፡፣ ዘወሀብኩ ፡፤ ለሠ ራዊተ ፣ እስራኤል ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ነቢየ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኢይሰአኖ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ የሀብከ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ አምአላንቱ ። '' ወአሌለዮሙ ፡ አማስያስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ለሠራዊ ት ፡ እለ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ እምበሐውርተ ፡ ኤፍሬም ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሑሩ ፡ ወይግብኡ ፡ በበ ፣መኀድሪሆሙ። ወተምፅፁ ፡ ፈድፋደዶ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወተመይጡ ፡ ውስተ፡መ ኀድሪሆሙ ፡ በመዓት ፡ ዐቢይ ። ''ወአማስያስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ጸንዓ ፣ ወአስተጋብአ ፡ ሕዝ በ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ ወሖሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡፣ አፍላገ ፡ አጐሪም ፡ ወቀተሎሙ ፡ በህየ ፡ ለደቂቀ ፡ ሴይ ር ፡ አሐደ ፡ እልፈ ። ”“ወአርባዕተ ፣ አልፈ ፡ ፄወዉ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወአምጽአዎሙ፣፤
set over them * chiefs of thousands and (chiefs) of hundreds in all the lands of Yehudah and in Yerushalayim. (He made) the census of those who were twenty years old and above and he found them to be three hundred thousand (men) valiant and capable of making war and of carrying the shield and the spear. He took into his pay (one hundred) thousand (men) very strong from Esra'el; their pay was one hundred talents of silver. A prophet of the Lord came to find him, saying to him: "(O) king, let the army of Esra'el not walk with thee, for the Lord is not with Esra'el, (nor) with all the children of Efrem. If thou (take them) and if thou thinkest that victory will be obtained by them, the Lord will make thee fall before thy enemies, for it belongs to the Lord to strengthen and to make fall." Amasya said to the prophet of the Lord: "What shall I do then concerning the talents that I have given to the troops of Esra'el?" The prophet of the Lord said to him: "It is not impossible for the Lord to give thee much more than these (talents)." King Amasyas dispersed the troops that had come toward him from the cities of Efrem, so that they might go away and return each to his land. They were very irritated against Yehudah and they returned to their lands in great anger. King Amasyas strengthened himself, gathered his people, went toward the valley of Agouerim, and defeated there ten thousand children of Seyer. The children of Yehudah


[p. 715] {#po.23.p715}
ላዕለ ፡ ርአሰ ፣ ቀልቀል ፡ ወአፅደፍዎሙ ፡ እምርአሰ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ቀልቀል ፡ ወዙሎሙ፣ ነቅዑ ። “ወአደው ፡ ጸናዕት ፡ አለ ፡ አግብአ ፡ አማስያስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይሖሩ ፡ ምስሌሁ ለፀብእ ፡ ወሮዱ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ አህጉረ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ እምብሔረ ፡ ሰማርያ ፡ እስከ ፡ ቤቴሮን ፡ወ ቀተለ ፡ አምኔሆሙ ፡ ሠላሳ ፡ ምእተ ፡ ወማሀረኩ ፡ ምሕርካና ፡ ዐቢየ ። ”ወኮነ ፡ እም ድኅረ ፡ መጽአ ፡ አማስያስ ፡ ቀቲሎ ፡ ብሔረ ፤ ኤዶምያስ ፡ ወአምጽአ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ አ ማልክተ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ ሴይር ፡ ወአቀሞሙ ፡ ሎቱ ፣ አማልክተ ፥ ወሎሙ ፡ ይሰግድ ፡ ወው አቱሰ ፡ በርአሱ ፡ ይሠውዕ ፡ ሎሙ ። ”ወኮነ ፡ መቅሠፍተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ላዕለ ፡ አ ማስያ ፡ ንጉሥ ። ወፈነወ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ነቢያተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ወለምንት ፡ ኃሠሥከ ፡ አ ማልክተ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ እለ፣ ኢይባልሑ ፡ ሕዝበ ፡ ዚአሆሙ ፡ እምእደ ፡ ዚአከ ። ”ወኮኑ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትናገርዎ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ አማስያስ ፡ ዘለከ ፡ ጫምኩክ ፡ መምክር የ ፡ ተዐቀብ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢትትመንደብ ። ወኢያርመመ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ እስመ; መከረ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ላዕሌከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሰሩከ ፡ እስመ ፡፣ ገበርከ ፡ ዘንተ ፣፡ ወኢሰማዕ ከ ፡ ምክረ ፡ ዚአየ ። '"ወተማከረ ፡ አማስያ ፣ ንጉሥ ፡ ወለአከ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዮአስ ፡ ወል ደ፣ኢዮአክዝ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ኢያኡ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ነዓአ ፡ ንትረኦ ይ ፡ ገጸ ፤ በገጽ ። "ወለአከ ፡ ዮአስ ፣ ንጉሠ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ኅበ ፣ አማስያ ፣ ንጉሠ፣ ይሁዳ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ አቃኒ ፡ ዘውስተ ፡ ሊባኖስ ፡ ለአከ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዘግባ ፡ ዘሊባኖስ ፡
took ten thousand (text: forty thousand) prisoners; they led them to the summit of a precipice and threw them from the summit of this precipice; all were mangled. The valiant men that Amasyas had dismissed, so that they would not go with him to the war, invaded all the cities of Yehudah from the land of Samarya to Beteron. They killed three thousand (men) among (the inhabitants) and took great booty. * When Amasyas had returned, after having defeated the (men) of the land of Edomyas, and had brought with him the gods of the children of Seyer, he made them his own gods, worshipped them, and sacrificed himself before them. The punishment of the Lord came upon King Amasya. (The Lord) sent him prophets, saying: "Why hast thou sought the gods of the people who have not delivered their own people from thy hand?" While they were saying this to him, Amasyas said this (to one of the prophets): "(Is it that) I have established thee my counselor? Take care not to be punished." The prophet did not remain silent, but said: "The Lord has resolved to exterminate thee, because thou hast done this and thou hast not listened to my counsel." King Amasya took counsel and sent (messengers) to Yo'as, son of Lyo'akaz, son of Ya'ou, king of Esra'el, saying: "Come, so that we may see each other face to face." Yo'as, king of Esra'el, sent (messengers) to Amasya, king of Yehudah, saying: "The thistle that is on the Libanos sent (messengers) to the cedar of the Libanos, saying: Give thy


[p. 716] {#po.23.p716}
እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ሀበኒ ፡ ወለተከ ፡ ለወልድየ ፡ ብእሲተ ፡ ወነዮሙ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ አራዊተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ዘውስተ ፡ ሊባኖስ ፡ ወኬድዎ ፡ ለአቃኒ ። ”እንዘ ፡ ትቤ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ቀተልኩ ፡ በሓውር ተ ፡ ኤይምያስ ፡ ወይትዔበየከ ፡ ልብከ ፡ ፈድፋደ ። ወይእዜኒ ፡ ንበር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤትከ ። ወለምንት ፡ ታመክር ፡ ወትትራዳእ ፡ ለአኪት ፡ ወትወድቅ ፡ አንተ ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ምስሌከ ። ” ወኢሰምዓ ፡ አማስያስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ እስመ ፡ አምኅበ ፣፡ አግዚአብሔር ፡ ኮነት ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያግብኦ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እደ ፡ ጸላእቱ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኃሠሠ ፡ አማልክተ ፡ ኤይማው ያን ። *” ወዓርገ ፡ ዮአስ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ወተረአዩ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ ውእቱኒ ፡ ወአማስያ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ በቤተ ፡ ሳሚስ ፡ እንተ ፡ ደወለ ፡ ይሁዳ ። ”“ወወድቁ ፡ ይሁ ዳ ፣ አንጻረ ፣ እስራኤል ፣ ወገ ፣ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ ወገብኡ ፡ በበ ፣ መኅድሪሆሙ ። "ወለአማስያ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ "ይሁዳ ፡ ወልደ ፣ ዮአስ ፡ ዴገኖ ፡ ዮአስ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ እስ ራኤል ፡ በቤተ ፡ ሰምሴ ፣ ወአኀዞ ፡ ወአብኦ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ። ወነሰተ ፣፡ ዓረፍ ተ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ እምአንቀጸ ፣ ኤፍሬም ፡ እስከ ፡ አንቀጸ ፣ ማእዝንት ፡ ዓርባዕቱ ፡ ም እት ፡ በእመት ። ”ወኩሎ ፡ ወርቀ ፡ ወብሩረ ፡ ወኩሎ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ዘተረክበ ፡ ውስተ ፡ቤ ተ፣፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በአዴሁ ፡ ለአብዴዶመ ፥ ወመዛግብተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ንጉሥ ፣ ወለደቂ ቀ ፣ ፈላስያን ፡ ወሜጦሙ ፡ ወአግብኦሙ- ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ሰማርያ ። ”ወሐይወ ፡ አማስያስ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ኢዮአስ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወእምድኅረ ፡ ሞቱ ፡ ለኢዮአስ ፡ ወል ደ ፡ ኢዩ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ አስራኤል ፡ ዓሠርተ ፡ ወኃምስተ ፡ ዓመተ ። ወእለ ፡ ተርፋ ፡ ቃላ
daughter to my son for a wife. Behold that the beasts of the desert of the Libanos arrived and trampled underfoot the thistle. When thou hast said: Behold that I have defeated the (men) of the land of Edomyas, thy heart has greatly puffed up. Now remain in thy house. Why dost thou prepare and favor (thy own) misfortune? Thou shalt fall thyself and all Yehudah (shall fall) with thee." King Amasyas did not listen (to him), for it was (the will) of the Lord to deliver him into the hands of his enemies, because he had sought the gods of the Edomawyan. Yo'as, king of Esra'el, went up and they saw each other, he and Amasya, king of Yehudah, at Beta-Samis of the territory of Yehudah. (The men of) Yehudah were put to rout before Esra'el, fled all together, and returned each to his dwelling. Yo'as, king of Esra'el, pursued Amasya, king of * Yehudah, son of Yo'as, took him prisoner at Beta-Samse, and made him enter with him into Yerushalayim. He demolished the walls of Yerushalayim from the gate of Efrem to the gate of the Angle for four hundred cubits. He took and carried away with him to the land of Samarya all the gold, the silver, all the vases that were in the house of the Lord under the guard of Abdedomou, the treasures of the house of the king, and hostages. Amasyas, son of Lyo'as, king of Yehudah, lived fifteen years after the death of Lyo'as, son of Lyou, king of Esra'el. The other actions of Amasya, the first


[p. 717] {#po.23.p717}
ተ፣ አማስያ ፣ ቀደምት፣ወደኃርት፣ አኮኑ ጽሑፋት ፣ ውስተ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ነገሥተ ፡እ ስራኤል ፡ወይሁዳ ። ” ወበውእቱ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ አመ ፡ ርኅቀ ፡ አማስያ ፡ እምፍኖተ ፡ እግዚ አብሔር ፡ ወገብሩ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ማዕሌተ ፡ ወጐየ ፡ እምብሔረ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ለብሔረ ፡ ለኪ ስ። ወለአኩ ፡ ድኅሬሁ ፡ ወዴገንዎ ፡ብሔረ ፡ለኪስ ፡ ወቀተልዎ ፡ በህየ። ”“ወአጾርዎ፣መ ልዕልተ ፡ አፍራስ ፣፡ ወአብጽሕዖ ፡ ወቀበርዎ ፡ ምስለ አበዊሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ዳዊት ። ኣኢናዝ፤, ' ወነሥአዎ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ አሕዛበ ፡ ምድር ፡ ለኦዝያስ ፤ ወአንገሥዎ ፡ ከዊ ኖ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ዓሠርቱ ፡ ወስድስቱ ፡ ዓመት ፡ ወአንገሥዎ ፡ ሀየንተ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ አማስያስ ። ውእቱ ፡ ሐነጸ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ዔላት ፡ ውእቱኒ ፡ አግብአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እደ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ እምድኅ ረ ፡ ሞተ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አበዊሁ ። "ከዊኖ ፣ ወልደ ፡ ዓሠርቱ ፡ ወስድስቱ፣፤ ዓመት ፡ አመ ፡ ይነግሥ ፡ ኦዝያስ ፡ ወኃምጳሳ ፡ ወ፪ ፡ ዓመተ ፣ ነግሠ ፡ በኢየሩሳሌም ። ወ ስማ ፡ ለአሙ ፤ ኢዬዩኬልያስ ፡ እምሀገረ ፣ ኢየሩሳሌም ። ወገብረ ፡ ርትዓ ፣፡ በቅድመ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡-በከመ ፡፣ ኩሉ ፡ ዘገብረ ፣ አማ" ስያ ፡ አቡሁ ። ወሀሎ ፡ ይኅሥሦ ፡ ለእ ግዚአብሔር ፡ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡፣ ዘካርያስ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ዘይነብር ፣ በፈሪሃ ፣፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወበመዋዕሊሁኒ ፡ ኃሠሦ ፣፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወእግዚአብሔር ፡ ይሴርሕ ፡ ሎቱ ። "ወወፅ አ፣ ወቀተሎሙ ፡ ለሕዝበ ፡ ኢሎፍሊ ። ወአውደቀ ፡ ዓረፋቲሃ ፡ ለጌት ፡ ወዓረፋቲሃ ፡ ለኢያቤስ ፡ ወዓረፋቲሃ ፡ ለዓዛጦን ፣ ወአሕነጸ ፣ አህጉረ ፣ በምድረ ፡ አዛጦን ፣ ወበበሐ ውርተ ፣ ኢሎፍሊ ። 'ወአጽንዖ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ኢሎፍሊ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወላዕለ ፡ አሕዛ
and the last, are they not written in the book of the Kings of Esra'el and of Yehudah? In those days, when Amasya had turned away from the way of the Lord, they made a conspiracy against him and he fled from the city of Yerushalayim to the city of Lakis. (But) they dispatched (men) behind him (who) pursued him to the city of Lakis and killed him there. They transported him on horses, brought him, and buried him with his fathers in the city of Dawit. XXVI, All the peoples of the land took Ozyas and made him king, while he was sixteen years old; they made him king in the place of Amasyas, his father. He rebuilt the city of Elat and brought (it) back under the domination of Yehudah, after the king, his father, was dead (and buried) with his fathers. Ozyas was sixteen years old, when he became king; he reigned fifty-two years in Yerushalayim. The name of his mother was Lyekelyas from the city of Yerushalayim. He did what was right before the Lord according to all that Amasya his father had done. He sought the Lord in the days of the prophet Zakaryas who dwelt in the fear of the Lord; in those days he sought the Lord and the Lord made him prosper. He went to fight the people of the Ilofeli. He overturned the walls of Get, the walls of Lyabes, and the walls of Azaton. He had cities (fortified) built in the land of Azaton and in the land of the Ilofeli. The Lord made him strong against the Ilofeli, against the peoples of Arabya who


[p. 718] {#po.23.p718}
በ ፣ዓረብያ ፡ እለ ፡ የኅድሩ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ ኩኩሕ ፡ ወላዕለ ፡ አሞናውያን ። ወወሀቡ አሞናውያን ፡ አምኃ ፡ ለኦዝያስ ፡ ንጉሥ ። ወበጽሐ ፡ ስሙ ፡ እስከ ፣ በሐውርተ ፡ ግብ ጽ ፡ እስመ ፡ ጸንዓ ፡ ፈድፋደ ። ወአሕነጸ ፡ ኦዝያስ ፡ ማኅፈደ ፡ በኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ዘመንገ ለ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ አንቀጸ ፣ ፈለግ ፡ ወመልዕልተ ፡ ማዕዘንት ፡ ወጸንዓ ። ""ወሐነጸ ፡ ማኅፈ ዴ ፡ በገዳም ፡ ወወቀረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩኩሕ ፡ አግባበ ፡ ብዙኃነ ፡ እስመ ፡ እንስሳ ፣ ብዙ ኃን ፡ ተረክበ ፡ በብሔረ ፡ ሴፌል ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወበብሔረ ፡ ሐቅል ፡ ወአቀብተ ፡ አዕፃደ፣ወ ይን ፡ በውስተ ፡ አድባር ፡ ወገ”ዳም ፡ በብሔረ ፡ ቀርሜሎስ ፡ አስመ ፣ መፍቀሬ ፡ ሐረስ ት ፡ ውእቱ ። "' ወኮንዎ ፡ ለኦዝያስ ፡ ሰራዊት ፡ አለ ፡ ይክሉ ፡ ቀቲለ ፡ ወይክሉ ፡ ወዒአ ፡ ፀብእ ፡ ለጐልቀ። ወኮነ፣ ጐልቆሙ ፡ በእዴሁ ፡ ለኢያሄል ፡ ጸሐፊ ፡ ወበእደ ፡ መሷኣ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወበእዴሁ ፡ ለሐናንያ ፡ ዐቃቤ ፡ መንግሥት ። ”ወኮነ ፡ ጐልቆሙ ፡ለአበው ፡ ጸናዕት ፡ በውስተ ፡ ፀብእ ፡ ፳፻ ፡ ወ፪፻ ። “ወሰራዊት ፡ ጸናዕት ፡ ለቀትል ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ቭ፳ ፡ ወድፎ፻ ፡ ወ፳፻ ፡ እሉ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ አለ ፡ ይክሉ ፡ ቀቲለ ፡ ወጽኑዓን ፣፡ በሐይል ፡ወ ይክሉ ፡ ረዲኦቶ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ፀሩ ። “ወአስተዳለወ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ኦዝያስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡
dwelt (in Arabia) Petraea (text: on the rock) and against the Amonawyan. The Amonawyan gave presents to King Ozyas. His fame reached the land of Gebs (Egypt), for he was extremely powerful. Ozyas had towers built in Yerushalayim, at the gate of the Valley and (at the gate) of the Angle, and he fortified them (text: and they were strong). He built towers in the desert; he dug many cisterns in the rock, for he possessed numerous herds in the low region (sefel) and in the plain; (he put) vine-growers on the mountains and in * the countryside of the land of Qarmelos, for he was the friend of the farmers. Ozyas had troops able to fight and able to go to war according to (the order of) the census. The census had been made by Lyahhel scribe, by Masia commissioner, and by Hananya royal lieutenant. The number of the heads of families valiant in combat was two thousand six hundred. Under them was an army valiant in combat of three hundred and seven thousand five hundred (men); these could make war, were valiant in combat, and could help the king against his enemies. King Ozyas prepared for all the men of his army
